13:21  LA: Et dirumpam cervicalia vestra, et liberabo populum meum de manu vestra, neque erunt ultra in manibus vestris ad prædandum : et scietis quia ego Dominus.
       EN: I will tear your pillows apart and deliver My people from your hand. They will no longer be in your hands as prey, and you will know that I am the Lord.

13:22  LA: Pro eo quod mœrere fecistis cor justi mendaciter, quem ego non contristavi, et confortastis manus impii, ut non reverteretur a via sua mala, et viveret :
       EN: Because you have falsely grieved the heart of the righteous man whom I did not grieve, and strengthened the hands of the wicked man so that he would not turn from his evil way and live,

13:23  LA: propterea vana non videbitis, et divinationes non divinabitis amplius, et eruam populum meum de manu vestra : et scietis quia ego Dominus.]
       EN: therefore you will no longer see empty things or practice divinations. I will rescue My people from your hand, and you will know that I am the Lord.”

EZEKIEL 14

14:1  LA: Et venerunt ad me viri seniorum Israël, et sederunt coram me.
      EN: Men from among the elders of Israel came to me and sat before me.

14:2  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

14:3  LA: Fili hominis, viri isti posuerunt immunditias suas in cordibus suis, et scandalum iniquitatis suæ statuerunt contra faciem suam : numquid interrogatus respondebo eis ?
      EN: “Son of man, these men have set their unclean things in their hearts and placed the stumbling block of their iniquity before their faces. Should I answer them when they inquire?

14:4  LA: Propter hoc loquere eis, et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Homo, homo de domo Israël, qui posuerit immunditias suas in corde suo, et scandalum iniquitatis suæ statuerit contra faciem suam, et venerit ad prophetam interrogans per eum me : ego Dominus respondebo ei in multitudine immunditiarum suarum,
      EN: Therefore speak to them and say to them: Thus says the Lord God: Any man of the house of Israel who sets his unclean things in his heart and places the stumbling block of his iniquity before his face, and comes to a prophet to inquire of Me through him—I, the Lord, will answer him according to the multitude of his unclean things,

14:5  LA: ut capiatur domus Israël in corde suo, quo recesserunt a me in cunctis idolis suis.
      EN: so that the house of Israel may be caught in its own heart, in which they have turned away from Me through all their idols.

14:6  LA: Propterea dic ad domum Israël : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Convertimini, et recedite ab idolis vestris, et ab universis contaminationibus vestris avertite facies vestras.
      EN: Therefore say to the house of Israel: Thus says the Lord God: Turn back and withdraw from your idols, and turn your faces away from all your defilements.

14:7  LA: Quia homo, homo de domo Israël, et de proselytis quicumque advena fuerit in Israël, si alienatus fuerit a me, et posuerit idola sua in corde suo, et scandalum iniquitatis suæ statuerit contra faciem suam, et venerit ad prophetam ut interroget per eum me : ego Dominus respondebo ei per me :
      EN: For any man of the house of Israel, or any of the converts living as a foreigner in Israel, who becomes estranged from Me, sets his idols in his heart and places the stumbling block of his iniquity before his face, and comes to a prophet to inquire of Me through him—I, the Lord, will answer him Myself.

14:8  LA: et ponam faciem meam super hominem illum, et faciam eum in exemplum et in proverbium, et disperdam eum de medio populi mei : et scietis quia ego Dominus.
      EN: I will set My face against that man and make him an example and a proverb, and destroy him from the midst of My people; and you will know that I am the Lord.

14:9  LA: Et propheta cum erraverit, et locutus fuerit verbum, ego Dominus decepi prophetam illum, et extendam manum meam super illum, et delebo eum de medio populi mei Israël.
      EN: When a prophet goes astray and speaks a word, I, the Lord, have deceived that prophet. I will stretch out My hand against him and wipe him out from the midst of My people Israel.

14:10  LA: Et portabunt iniquitatem suam : juxta iniquitatem interrogantis, sic iniquitas prophetæ erit :
       EN: They will bear their iniquity. The iniquity of the prophet will be like the iniquity of the one who inquires,

14:11  LA: ut non erret ultra domus Israël a me, neque polluatur in universis prævaricationibus suis : sed sint mihi in populum, et ego sim eis in Deum, ait Dominus exercituum.
       EN: so that the house of Israel may no longer stray from Me or be polluted by all its transgressions, but may be My people, and I may be their God, says the Lord of hosts.”

14:12  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

14:13  LA: [Fili hominis, terra cum peccaverit mihi, ut prævaricetur prævaricans, extendam manum meam super eam, et conteram virgam panis ejus, et immittam in eam famem, et interficiam de ea hominem et jumentum.
       EN: “Son of man, when a land sins against Me by acting treacherously, I will stretch out My hand against it, break its staff of bread, send famine upon it, and cut off human beings and livestock from it.

14:14  LA: Et si fuerint tres viri isti in medio ejus, Noë, Daniel, et Job, ipsi justitia sua liberabunt animas suas, ait Dominus exercituum.
       EN: Even if these three men, Noah, Daniel, and Job, were within it, by their own righteousness they would save their own lives, says the Lord of hosts.

14:15  LA: Quod si et bestias pessimas induxero super terram ut vastem eam, et fuerit invia, eo quod non sit pertransiens propter bestias :
       EN: If I also bring savage beasts upon the land to lay it waste, so that it becomes impassable, with no one passing through because of the beasts,

14:16  LA: tres viri isti si fuerint in ea, vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, quia nec filios nec filias liberabunt, sed ipsi soli liberabuntur, terra autem desolabitur.
       EN: even if these three men were in it—as I live, says the Lord God—they would deliver neither sons nor daughters; only they themselves would be delivered, and the land would be desolate.

14:17  LA: Vel si gladium induxero super terram illam, et dixero gladio : Transi per terram : et interfecero de ea hominem et jumentum,
       EN: Or if I bring a sword upon that land and say to the sword, ‘Pass through the land,’ and cut off human beings and livestock from it,

14:18  LA: et tres viri isti fuerint in medio ejus : vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, non liberabunt filios neque filias, sed ipsi soli liberabuntur.
       EN: even if these three men were within it—as I live, says the Lord God—they would deliver neither sons nor daughters; only they themselves would be delivered.

14:19  LA: Si autem et pestilentiam immisero super terram illam, et effudero indignationem meam super eam in sanguine, ut auferam ex ea hominem et jumentum,
       EN: Or if I also send pestilence upon that land and pour out My indignation upon it in blood, to remove human beings and livestock from it,

14:20  LA: et Noë, et Daniel, et Job fuerint in medio ejus : vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, quia filium et filiam non liberabunt, sed ipsi justitia sua liberabunt animas suas.
       EN: even if Noah, Daniel, and Job were within it—as I live, says the Lord God—they would deliver neither son nor daughter, but would deliver their own souls by their righteousness.

14:21  LA: Quoniam hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Quod etsi quatuor judicia mea pessima, gladium, et famem, ac bestias malas, et pestilentiam, immisero in Jerusalem, ut interficiam de ea hominem et pecus,
       EN: For thus says the Lord God: Even if I send My four terrible judgments—sword, famine, savage beasts, and pestilence—upon Jerusalem, to cut off human beings and cattle from it,

14:22  LA: tamen relinquetur in ea salvatio educentium filios et filias : ecce ipsi ingredientur ad vos, et videbitis viam eorum et adinventiones eorum, et consolabimini super malo quod induxi in Jerusalem, in omnibus quæ importavi super eam.
       EN: yet there will remain in it a surviving band bringing out sons and daughters. Behold, they will come to you, and you will see their way and their deeds, and you will be consoled over the evil I have brought upon Jerusalem, over everything I have brought upon it.

14:23  LA: Et consolabuntur vos, cum videritis viam eorum et adinventiones eorum : et cognoscetis quod non frustra fecerim omnia quæ feci in ea, ait Dominus Deus.]
       EN: They will console you when you see their way and their deeds; and you will know that I have not done without cause all that I have done in it, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 15

15:1  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

15:2  LA: [Fili hominis, quid fiet de ligno vitis, ex omnibus lignis nemorum quæ sunt inter ligna silvarum ?
      EN: “Son of man, what can be made from vine wood, out of all the woodland trees among the trees of the forest?

15:3  LA: numquid tolletur de ea lignum ut fiat opus, aut fabricabitur de ea paxillus ut dependeat in eo quodcumque vas ?
      EN: Can wood be taken from it to make something, or can a peg be fashioned from it to hang any vessel on?

15:4  LA: Ecce igni datum est in escam : utramque partem ejus consumpsit ignis, et medietas ejus redacta est in favillam : numquid utile erit ad opus ?
      EN: Behold, it has been given to the fire as fuel. The fire has consumed both its ends, and its middle has been reduced to ash. Will it be useful for making anything?

15:5  LA: Etiam cum esset integrum, non erat aptum ad opus : quanto magis cum illud ignis devoraverit et combusserit, nihil ex eo fiet operis ?
      EN: Even when it was whole, it was unfit for making anything. How much less can anything be made from it when fire has devoured and burned it!

15:6  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Quomodo lignum vitis inter ligna silvarum, quod dedi igni ad devorandum, sic tradam habitatores Jerusalem.
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Like the vine wood among the trees of the forest that I have given to the fire to devour, so I will hand over the inhabitants of Jerusalem.

15:7  LA: Et ponam faciem meam in eos : de igne egredientur, et ignis consumet eos : et scietis quia ego Dominus, cum posuero faciem meam in eos,
      EN: I will set My face against them. They will come out of the fire, and fire will consume them. You will know that I am the Lord when I set My face against them

15:8  LA: et dedero terram inviam et desolatam, eo quod prævaricatores extiterint, dicit Dominus Deus.]
      EN: and make the land impassable and desolate because they have proved treacherous, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 16

16:1  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

16:2  LA: Fili hominis, notas fac Jerusalem abominationes suas,
      EN: “Son of man, make Jerusalem know her abominations.

16:3  LA: et dices : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Jerusalem : Radix tua et generatio tua de terra Chanaan : pater tuus Amorrhæus, et mater tua Cethæa.
      EN: Say: Thus says the Lord God to Jerusalem: Your root and your birth are from the land of Canaan. Your father was an Amorite, and your mother a Hittite.

16:4  LA: Et quando nata es, in die ortus tui non est præcisus umbilicus tuus, et aqua non es lota in salutem, nec sale salita, nec involuta pannis.
      EN: When you were born, on the day of your birth, your umbilical cord was not cut, you were not washed with water for your health, or rubbed with salt, or wrapped in cloths.

16:5  LA: Non pepercit super te oculus, ut faceret tibi unum de his, misertus tui : sed projecta es super faciem terræ in abjectione animæ tuæ in die qua nata es.
      EN: No eye spared you, taking pity on you to do even one of these things for you. Instead you were thrown onto the face of the ground, your life despised, on the day you were born.

16:6  LA: Transiens autem per te, vidi te conculcari in sanguine tuo : et dixi tibi cum esses in sanguine tuo : Vive, dixi, inquam, tibi : in sanguine tuo vive.
      EN: Passing by you, I saw you trampled in your blood. I said to you while you were in your blood, ‘Live!’ I said to you, I tell you, ‘In your blood, live!’

16:7  LA: Multiplicatam quasi germen agri dedi te : et multiplicata es, et grandis effecta, et ingressa es, et pervenisti ad mundum muliebrem : ubera tua intumuerunt, et pilus tuus germinavit : et eras nuda, et confusione plena.
      EN: I made you grow like a shoot of the field. You grew and became tall, and entered upon and attained the adornment of womanhood. Your breasts swelled and your hair sprouted, yet you were naked and full of shame.

16:8  LA: Et transivi per te, et vidi te : et ecce tempus tuum, tempus amantium : et expandi amictum meum super te, et operui ignominiam tuam : et juravi tibi, et ingressus sum pactum tecum, ait Dominus Deus, et facta es mihi.
      EN: I passed by you and saw you, and behold, your time was the time of lovers. I spread My mantle over you and covered your shame. I swore an oath to you and entered into a covenant with you, says the Lord God, and you became Mine.

16:9  LA: Et lavi te aqua, et emundavi sanguinem tuum ex te, et unxi te oleo.
      EN: I washed you with water, cleansed your blood from you, and anointed you with oil.

16:10  LA: Et vestivi te discoloribus, et calceavi te janthino, et cinxi te bysso, et indui te subtilibus :
       EN: I clothed you in many-colored garments, put violet-colored shoes on you, girded you with fine linen, and dressed you in delicate fabrics.

16:11  LA: et ornavi te ornamento, et dedi armillas in manibus tuis, et torquem circa collum tuum :
       EN: I adorned you with ornaments, put bracelets on your wrists and a necklace around your neck.

16:12  LA: et dedi inaurem super os tuum, et circulos auribus tuis, et coronam decoris in capite tuo.
       EN: I put a ring above your mouth, earrings on your ears, and a beautiful crown on your head.

16:13  LA: Et ornata es auro et argento, et vestita es bysso et polymito et multicoloribus : similam, et mel, et oleum comedisti : et decora facta es vehementer nimis, et profecisti in regnum.
       EN: You were adorned with gold and silver, and clothed in fine linen, woven fabrics, and multicolored garments. You ate fine flour, honey, and oil. You became exceedingly beautiful and rose to royal dignity.

16:14  LA: Et egressum est nomen tuum in gentes propter speciem tuam, quia perfecta eras in decore meo quem posueram super te, dicit Dominus Deus.
       EN: Your fame went out among the nations because of your beauty, for you were perfect in the splendor I had placed upon you, says the Lord God.

16:15  LA: Et habens fiduciam in pulchritudine tua, fornicata es in nomine tuo : et exposuisti fornicationem tuam omni transeunti, ut ejus fieres.
       EN: But you trusted in your beauty and prostituted yourself on the strength of your fame. You offered your prostitution to everyone who passed by, to become his.

16:16  LA: Et sumens de vestimentis tuis, fecisti tibi excelsa hinc inde consuta, et fornicata es super eis sicut non est factum, neque futurum est.
       EN: You took some of your garments and made high places for yourself, stitched together here and there, and prostituted yourself upon them, as has never happened and never will happen again.

16:17  LA: Et tulisti vasa decoris tui de auro meo atque argento meo, quæ dedi tibi, et fecisti tibi imagines masculinas, et fornicata es in eis.
       EN: You took your beautiful vessels of My gold and My silver that I had given you, made male images for yourself, and prostituted yourself with them.

16:18  LA: Et sumpsisti vestimenta tua multicoloria, et operuisti illas, et oleum meum et thymiama meum posuisti coram eis.
       EN: You took your multicolored garments and covered them, and you placed My oil and My incense before them.

16:19  LA: Et panem meum quem dedi tibi, similam, et oleum, et mel, quibus enutrivi te, posuisti in conspectu eorum in odorem suavitatis : et factum est, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: My bread that I had given you—the fine flour, oil, and honey with which I had nourished you—you placed before them as a pleasing fragrance. And so it happened, says the Lord God.

16:20  LA: Et tulisti filios tuos et filias tuas quas generasti mihi, et immolasti eis ad devorandum. Numquid parva est fornicatio tua ?
       EN: You took your sons and daughters whom you had borne to Me and sacrificed them to be devoured by those idols. Was your prostitution a small thing?

16:21  LA: Immolasti filios meos, et dedisti, illos consecrans, eis.
       EN: You sacrificed My children and gave them to the idols, consecrating them to them.

16:22  LA: Et post omnes abominationes tuas et fornicationes, non es recordata dierum adolescentiæ tuæ, quando eras nuda et confusione plena, conculcata in sanguine tuo.
       EN: After all your abominations and prostitutions, you did not remember the days of your youth, when you were naked and full of shame, trampled in your blood.

16:23  LA: Et accidit post omnem malitiam tuam (væ, væ tibi ! ait Dominus Deus),
       EN: And after all your wickedness—woe, woe to you, says the Lord God—

16:24  LA: et ædificasti tibi lupanar, et fecisti tibi prostibulum in cunctis plateis.
       EN: you built yourself a brothel and made yourself a place of prostitution in every square.

16:25  LA: Ad omne caput viæ ædificasti signum prostitutionis tuæ, et abominabilem fecisti decorem tuum : et divisisti pedes tuos omni transeunti, et multiplicasti fornicationes tuas :
       EN: At the head of every road you built a sign of your prostitution and made your beauty abominable. You spread your legs for everyone who passed by and multiplied your prostitutions.

16:26  LA: et fornicata es cum filiis Ægypti, vicinis tuis, magnarum carnium : et multiplicasti fornicationem tuam ad irritandum me.
       EN: You prostituted yourself with the sons of Egypt, your neighbors, great in flesh, and multiplied your prostitution to provoke Me.

16:27  LA: Ecce ego extendam manum meam super te, et auferam justificationem tuam, et dabo te in animas odientium te filiarum Palæstinarum, quæ erubescunt in via tua scelerata.
       EN: Behold, I will stretch out My hand against you and take away what was decreed for you. I will deliver you to the will of those who hate you, the daughters of the Philistines, who blush at your criminal way.

16:28  LA: Et fornicata es in filiis Assyriorum eo quod necdum fueris expleta : et postquam fornicata es, nec sic es satiata :
       EN: You prostituted yourself with the sons of the Assyrians because you were still not satisfied; and after prostituting yourself, even then you were not satisfied.

16:29  LA: et multiplicasti fornicationem tuam in terra Chanaan cum Chaldæis, et nec sic satiata es.
       EN: You multiplied your prostitution in the land of Canaan with the Chaldeans, and even so you were not satisfied.

16:30  LA: In quo mundabo cor tuum, ait Dominus Deus, cum facias omnia hæc opera mulieris meretricis et procacis ?
       EN: With what shall I cleanse your heart, says the Lord God, when you do all these deeds of a shameless prostitute?

16:31  LA: Quia fabricasti lupanar tuum in capite omnis viæ, et excelsum tuum fecisti in omni platea : nec facta es quasi meretrix fastidio augens pretium,
       EN: For you built your brothel at the head of every road and made your high place in every square. You were not like a prostitute increasing her price through disdain,

16:32  LA: sed quasi mulier adultera, quæ super virum suum inducit alienos.
       EN: but like an adulterous wife who takes in strangers besides her husband.

16:33  LA: Omnibus meretricibus dantur mercedes : tu autem dedisti mercedes cunctis amatoribus tuis, et dona donabas eis, ut intrarent ad te undique ad fornicandum tecum.
       EN: Payment is given to all prostitutes, but you gave payment to all your lovers. You kept giving them gifts, so that they would come to you from all around to prostitute themselves with you.

16:34  LA: Factumque est in te contra consuetudinem mulierum in fornicationibus tuis, et post te non erit fornicatio : in eo enim quod dedisti mercedes, et mercedes non accepisti, factum est in te contrarium.
       EN: In your prostitutions you have acted contrary to the custom of women, and there will be no prostitution after you. For in giving payment and receiving none, you have done the reverse.

16:35  LA: Propterea, meretrix, audi verbum Domini.
       EN: Therefore, prostitute, hear the word of the Lord.

16:36  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Quia effusum est æs tuum et revelata est ignominia tua in fornicationibus tuis super amatores tuos, et super idola abominationum tuarum, in sanguine filiorum tuorum quos dedisti eis,
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: Because your money has been poured out and your shame exposed in your prostitutions with your lovers and with the idols of your abominations, in the blood of your children whom you gave to them,

16:37  LA: ecce ego congregabo omnes amatores tuos quibus commista es, et omnes quos dilexisti, cum universis quos oderas : et congregabo eos super te undique, et nudabo ignominiam tuam coram eis, et videbunt omnem turpitudinem tuam.
       EN: behold, I will gather all your lovers with whom you have mingled, all those you loved together with all those you hated. I will gather them against you from every side and uncover your shame before them, and they will see all your nakedness.

16:38  LA: Et judicabo te judiciis adulterarum, et effundentium sanguinem : et dabo te in sanguinem furoris et zeli.
       EN: I will judge you with the judgments of adulteresses and those who shed blood, and I will give you over to the bloodshed of fury and jealousy.

16:39  LA: Et dabo te in manus eorum, et destruent lupanar tuum, et demolientur prostibulum tuum : et denudabunt te vestimentis tuis, et auferent vasa decoris tui, et derelinquent te nudam, plenamque ignominia :
       EN: I will give you into their hands, and they will destroy your brothel and demolish your place of prostitution. They will strip you of your garments, take away your beautiful vessels, and leave you naked and full of shame.

16:40  LA: et adducent super te multitudinem, et lapidabunt te lapidibus, et trucidabunt te gladiis suis :
       EN: They will bring a multitude against you, stone you with stones, and slaughter you with their swords.

16:41  LA: et comburent domos tuas igni, et facient in te judicia in oculis mulierum plurimarum. Et desines fornicari, et mercedes ultra non dabis :
       EN: They will burn your houses with fire and execute judgments upon you before the eyes of many women. You will cease prostituting yourself and will no longer give payment.

16:42  LA: et requiescet indignatio mea in te, et auferetur zelus meus a te : et quiescam, nec irascar amplius.
       EN: My indignation against you will come to rest, and My jealousy will depart from you. I will be quiet and will be angry no more.

16:43  LA: Eo quod non fueris recordata dierum adolescentiæ tuæ, et provocasti me in omnibus his, quapropter et ego vias tuas in capite tuo dedi, ait Dominus Deus, et non feci juxta scelera tua in omnibus abominationibus tuis.
       EN: Because you did not remember the days of your youth and provoked Me in all these things, I too have brought your ways upon your head, says the Lord God. Yet I have not dealt with you according to your crimes in all your abominations.

16:44  LA: Ecce omnis qui dicit vulgo proverbium, in te assumet illud, dicens : Sicut mater, ita et filia ejus.
       EN: Behold, everyone who commonly speaks a proverb will apply it to you, saying, ‘Like mother, like daughter.’

16:45  LA: Filia matris tuæ es tu, quæ projecit virum suum et filios suos : et soror sororum tuarum es tu, quæ projecerunt viros suos et filios suos : mater vestra Cethæa, et pater vester Amorrhæus.
       EN: You are the daughter of your mother, who cast away her husband and her children; and you are the sister of your sisters, who cast away their husbands and their children. Your mother was a Hittite and your father an Amorite.

16:46  LA: Et soror tua major, Samaria, ipsa et filiæ ejus, quæ habitant ad sinistram tuam : soror autem tua minor te, quæ habitat a dextris tuis, Sodoma, et filiæ ejus.
       EN: Your elder sister is Samaria, she and her daughters who dwell to your left; and your sister younger than you, who dwells to your right, is Sodom and her daughters.

16:47  LA: Sed nec in viis earum ambulasti, neque secundum scelera earum fecisti pauxillum minus : pene sceleratiora fecisti illis in omnibus viis tuis.
       EN: Yet you did not walk in their ways, nor did you do even a little less than their crimes; you have done things almost more wicked than theirs in all your ways.

16:48  LA: Vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, quia non fecit Sodoma soror tua, ipsa et filiæ ejus, sicut fecisti tu et filiæ tuæ.
       EN: As I live, says the Lord God, your sister Sodom, she and her daughters, did not do as you and your daughters have done.

16:49  LA: Ecce hæc fuit iniquitas Sodomæ sororis tuæ : superbia, saturitas panis et abundantia, et otium ipsius et filiarum ejus : et manum egeno et pauperi non porrigebant :
       EN: Behold, this was the iniquity of your sister Sodom: pride, fullness of bread, abundance, and idleness, hers and her daughters’. They did not extend a hand to the needy and the poor.

16:50  LA: et elevatæ sunt, et fecerunt abominationes coram me : et abstuli eas sicut vidisti.
       EN: They became exalted and committed abominations before Me, and I removed them, as you have seen.

16:51  LA: Et Samaria dimidium peccatorum tuorum non peccavit : sed vicisti eas sceleribus tuis, et justificasti sorores tuas in omnibus abominationibus tuis quas operata es.
       EN: Samaria did not commit half your sins, but you surpassed them in your crimes. Through all the abominations you have committed, you have made your sisters appear righteous.

16:52  LA: Ergo et tu porta confusionem tuam, quæ vicisti sorores tuas peccatis tuis, sceleratius agens ab eis : justificatæ sunt enim a te : ergo et tu confundere, et porta ignominiam tuam, quæ justificasti sorores tuas.
       EN: You too, therefore, bear your shame, you who have surpassed your sisters in your sins, acting more criminally than they. For they have been made to appear righteous by you. Therefore be ashamed and bear your disgrace, you who have made your sisters appear righteous.

16:53  LA: Et convertam restituens eas conversione Sodomorum cum filiabus suis, et conversione Samariæ et filiarum ejus, et convertam reversionem tuam in medio earum,
       EN: I will turn and restore them, with the restoration of Sodom and her daughters and the restoration of Samaria and her daughters; and I will bring about your return in their midst,

16:54  LA: ut portes ignominiam tuam, et confundaris in omnibus quæ fecisti consolans eas.
       EN: so that you may bear your disgrace and be ashamed of all you have done in giving them consolation.

16:55  LA: Et soror tua Sodoma et filiæ ejus revertentur ad antiquitatem suam, et Samaria et filiæ ejus revertentur ad antiquitatem suam, et tu et filiæ tuæ revertemini ad antiquitatem vestram.
       EN: Your sister Sodom and her daughters will return to their former state, and Samaria and her daughters will return to their former state, and you and your daughters will return to your former state.

16:56  LA: Non fuit autem Sodoma soror tua audita in ore tuo in die superbiæ tuæ,
       EN: Your sister Sodom was not heard of in your mouth in the day of your pride,

16:57  LA: antequam revelaretur malitia tua, sicut hoc tempore in opprobrium filiarum Syriæ, et cunctarum in circuitu tuo filiarum Palæstinarum quæ ambiunt te per gyrum.
       EN: before your wickedness was exposed, as it is now to the reproach of the daughters of Syria and all the daughters of the Philistines around you who surround you on every side.

16:58  LA: Scelus tuum et ignominiam tuam tu portasti, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: You have borne your crime and your disgrace, says the Lord God.

16:59  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Et faciam tibi sicut despexisti juramentum, ut irritum faceres pactum :
       EN: For thus says the Lord God: I will deal with you as you deserve for despising the oath and making the covenant void.

16:60  LA: et recordabor ego pacti mei tecum in diebus adolescentiæ tuæ, et suscitabo tibi pactum sempiternum.
       EN: Yet I will remember My covenant with you in the days of your youth, and I will establish an everlasting covenant for you.

16:61  LA: Et recordaberis viarum tuarum, et confunderis, cum receperis sorores tuas te majores cum minoribus tuis : et dabo eas tibi in filias, sed non ex pacto tuo.
       EN: You will remember your ways and be ashamed when you receive your sisters, those older than you together with those younger than you. I will give them to you as daughters, but not by your covenant.

16:62  LA: Et suscitabo ego pactum meum tecum, et scies quia ego Dominus :
       EN: I Myself will establish My covenant with you, and you will know that I am the Lord,

16:63  LA: ut recorderis, et confundaris, et non sit tibi ultra aperire os præ confusione tua, cum placatus tibi fuero in omnibus quæ fecisti, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: so that you may remember and be ashamed and no longer open your mouth because of your shame, when I have been reconciled to you for all that you have done, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 17

17:1  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

17:2  LA: Fili hominis, propone ænigma, et narra parabolam ad domum Israël,
      EN: “Son of man, put forward a riddle and tell a parable to the house of Israel.

17:3  LA: et dices : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Aquila grandis magnarum alarum, longo membrorum ductu, plena plumis et varietate, venit ad Libanum, et tulit medullam cedri.
      EN: Say: Thus says the Lord God: A great eagle with great wings, long of limb, full of feathers of many colors, came to Lebanon and took the heart of a cedar.

17:4  LA: Summitatem frondium ejus avulsit, et transportavit eam in terram Chanaan : in urbe negotiatorum posuit illam.
      EN: He tore off the top of its foliage and carried it to the land of Canaan; he placed it in a city of merchants.

17:5  LA: Et tulit de semine terræ, et posuit illud in terra pro semine, ut firmaret radicem super aquas multas : in superficie posuit illud.
      EN: He took some of the seed of the land and placed it in soil for sowing, so that it might establish its root beside abundant waters; he placed it on the surface.

17:6  LA: Cumque germinasset, crevit in vineam latiorem, humili statura, respicientibus ramis ejus ad eam, et radices ejus sub illa erant : facta est ergo vinea, et fructificavit in palmites, et emisit propagines.
      EN: When it sprouted, it grew into a spreading vine of low stature, with its branches turned toward the eagle and its roots beneath him. So it became a vine, bore shoots, and put out tendrils.

17:7  LA: Et facta est aquila altera grandis, magnis alis, multisque plumis : et ecce vinea ista quasi mittens radices suas ad eam, palmites suos extendit ad illam, ut irrigaret eam de areolis germinis sui.
      EN: There was another great eagle, with great wings and many feathers; and behold, this vine, as though sending its roots toward him, stretched out its shoots toward him, so that he might water it from the beds in which it grew.

17:8  LA: In terra bona super aquas multas plantata est, ut faciat frondes, et portet fructum, ut sit in vineam grandem.
      EN: It had been planted in good soil beside abundant waters, so that it might produce foliage and bear fruit and become a great vine.

17:9  LA: Dic : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ergone prosperabitur ? nonne radices ejus evellet, et fructus ejus distringet, et siccabit omnes palmites germinis ejus, et arescet, et non in brachio grandi, neque in populo multo, ut evelleret eam radicitus ?
      EN: Say: Thus says the Lord God: Will it then prosper? Will he not pull up its roots, strip off its fruit, and dry up all its growing shoots? It will wither, and no mighty arm or numerous people will be needed to pull it up by the roots.

17:10  LA: Ecce plantata est : ergone prosperabitur ? nonne, cum tetigerit eam ventus urens, siccabitur, et in areis germinis sui arescet ?
       EN: Behold, it has been planted. Will it then prosper? When the scorching wind touches it, will it not dry up and wither in the beds in which it grew?”

17:11  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

17:12  LA: Dic ad domum exasperantem : Nescitis quid ista significent ? Dic : Ecce venit rex Babylonis in Jerusalem, et assumet regem et principes ejus, et adducet eos ad semetipsum in Babylonem.
       EN: “Say to the exasperating house: Do you not know what these things mean? Say: Behold, the king of Babylon has come to Jerusalem. He will take its king and rulers and bring them to himself in Babylon.

17:13  LA: Et tollet de semine regni, ferietque cum eo fœdus, et ab eo accipiet jusjurandum. Sed et fortes terræ tollet,
       EN: He will take one of the royal stock, make a treaty with him, and receive an oath from him. He will also take away the strong men of the land,

17:14  LA: ut sit regnum humile, et non elevetur, sed custodiat pactum ejus, et servet illud.
       EN: so that the kingdom may be lowly and not rise up, but keep his covenant and observe it.

17:15  LA: Qui recedens ab eo misit nuntios ad Ægyptum, ut daret sibi equos et populum multum : numquid prosperabitur, vel consequetur salutem, qui fecit hæc ? et qui dissolvit pactum, numquid effugiet ?
       EN: But he broke away from him and sent messengers to Egypt, so that it might give him horses and many people. Will the man who has done these things prosper or obtain deliverance? Will one who breaks a covenant escape?

17:16  LA: Vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, quoniam in loco regis qui constituit eum regem, cujus fecit irritum juramentum, et solvit pactum quod habebat cum eo, in medio Babylonis morietur.
       EN: As I live, says the Lord God, in the place of the king who made him king, whose oath he made void and whose covenant with him he broke—in the midst of Babylon he will die.

17:17  LA: Et non in exercitu grandi, neque in populo multo, faciet contra eum Pharao prælium : in jactu aggeris, et in exstructione vallorum, ut interficiat animas multas.
       EN: Pharaoh will not wage battle against him with a great army or a numerous people, when a ramp is thrown up and siege works are built to destroy many lives.

17:18  LA: Spreverat enim juramentum, ut solveret fœdus, et ecce dedit manum suam : et cum omnia hæc fecerit, non effugiet.
       EN: For he despised the oath, breaking the treaty, though behold, he had given his hand. Since he has done all these things, he will not escape.

17:19  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Vivo ego, quoniam juramentum quod sprevit, et fœdus quod prævaricatus est, ponam in caput ejus.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: As I live, I will bring upon his head the oath he despised and the treaty he violated.

17:20  LA: Et expandam super eum rete meum, et comprehendetur in sagena mea : et adducam eum in Babylonem, et judicabo eum ibi in prævaricatione qua despexit me.
       EN: I will spread My net over him, and he will be caught in My dragnet. I will bring him to Babylon and judge him there for the treachery by which he despised Me.

17:21  LA: Et omnes profugi ejus, cum universo agmine suo, gladio cadent : residui autem in omnem ventum dispergentur : et scietis quia ego Dominus locutus sum.
       EN: All his fugitives, with his whole force, will fall by the sword, and those who remain will be scattered to every wind. You will know that I, the Lord, have spoken.

17:22  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Et sumam ego de medulla cedri sublimis, et ponam : de vertice ramorum ejus tenerum distringam, et plantabo super montem excelsum et eminentem.
       EN: “Thus says the Lord God: I Myself will take from the heart of the lofty cedar and set it in place. From the top of its branches I will pluck a tender shoot and plant it on a high and prominent mountain.

17:23  LA: In monte sublimi Israël plantabo illud, et erumpet in germen, et faciet fructum, et erit in cedrum magnam : et habitabunt sub ea omnes volucres, et universum volatile sub umbra frondium ejus nidificabit :
       EN: On the lofty mountain of Israel I will plant it. It will burst into growth and bear fruit and become a great cedar. All birds will dwell beneath it, and every winged creature will nest in the shade of its branches.

17:24  LA: et scient omnia ligna regionis quia ego Dominus humiliavi lignum sublime, et exaltavi lignum humile ; et siccavi lignum viride, et frondere feci lignum aridum. Ego Dominus locutus sum, et feci.
       EN: All the trees of the region will know that I, the Lord, have brought down the lofty tree and raised up the lowly tree, have dried up the green tree and made the dry tree flourish. I, the Lord, have spoken and have done it.”

EZEKIEL 18

18:1  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

18:2  LA: Quid est quod inter vos parabolam vertitis in proverbium istud in terra Israël, dicentes : Patres comederunt uvam acerbam, et dentes filiorum obstupescunt ?
      EN: “Why do you turn this saying into a proverb among yourselves in the land of Israel, saying, ‘The fathers have eaten sour grapes, and the children’s teeth are set on edge’?

18:3  LA: Vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, si erit ultra vobis parabola hæc in proverbium in Israël.
      EN: As I live, says the Lord God, you shall no longer use this saying as a proverb in Israel.

18:4  LA: Ecce omnes animæ meæ sunt : ut anima patris, ita et anima filii mea est : anima quæ peccaverit, ipsa morietur.
      EN: Behold, all souls are Mine. As the father’s soul is Mine, so also the son’s soul is Mine. The soul that sins shall die.

18:5  LA: Et vir si fuerit justus, et fecerit judicium et justitiam,
      EN: If a man is righteous and practices judgment and righteousness,

18:6  LA: in montibus non comederit, et oculos suos non levaverit ad idola domus Israël : et uxorem proximi sui non violaverit, et ad mulierem menstruatam non accesserit :
      EN: does not eat on the mountains or lift his eyes to the idols of the house of Israel, does not violate his neighbor’s wife or approach a menstruating woman,

18:7  LA: et hominem non contristaverit, pignus debitori reddiderit, per vim nihil rapuerit : panem suum esurienti dederit, et nudum operuerit vestimento :
      EN: does not afflict anyone, returns a debtor’s pledge and seizes nothing by force, gives his bread to the hungry and covers the naked with a garment,

18:8  LA: ad usuram non commodaverit, et amplius non acceperit : ab iniquitate averterit manum suam, et judicium verum fecerit inter virum et virum :
      EN: does not lend at interest or take an increase, turns his hand away from iniquity and renders true judgment between one man and another,

18:9  LA: in præceptis meis ambulaverit, et judicia mea custodierit, ut faciat veritatem : hic justus est ; vita vivet, ait Dominus Deus.
      EN: walks in My commands and keeps My judgments so as to act faithfully—this man is righteous; he will surely live, says the Lord God.

18:10  LA: Quod si genuerit filium latronem, effundentem sanguinem, et fecerit unum de istis :
       EN: But if he fathers a son who is a robber, who sheds blood and does any of these things,

18:11  LA: et hæc quidem omnia non facientem, sed in montibus comedentem, et uxorem proximi sui polluentem :
       EN: who does none of all these duties, but eats on the mountains and defiles his neighbor’s wife,

18:12  LA: egenum et pauperem contristantem, rapientem rapinas, pignus non reddentem, et ad idola levantem oculos suos, abominationem facientem :
       EN: afflicts the needy and the poor, commits robbery, does not return a pledge, lifts his eyes to idols and commits abomination,

18:13  LA: ad usuram dantem, et amplius accipientem : numquid vivet ? Non vivet : cum universa hæc detestanda fecerit, morte morietur ; sanguis ejus in ipso erit.
       EN: lends at interest and takes an increase—will he live? He will not live. Since he has done all these detestable things, he will surely die; his blood will be upon himself.

18:14  LA: Quod si genuerit filium, qui videns omnia peccata patris sui quæ fecit, timuerit, et non fecerit simile eis :
       EN: But if he fathers a son who sees all the sins his father has committed, fears, and does not do likewise,

18:15  LA: super montes non comederit, et oculos suos non levaverit ad idola domus Israël, et uxorem proximi sui non violaverit :
       EN: does not eat on the mountains or lift his eyes to the idols of the house of Israel, does not violate his neighbor’s wife,

18:16  LA: et virum non contristaverit, pignus non retinuerit, et rapinam non rapuerit : panem suum esurienti dederit, et nudum operuerit vestimento :
       EN: does not afflict anyone or retain a pledge or commit robbery, gives his bread to the hungry and covers the naked with a garment,

18:17  LA: a pauperis injuria averterit manum suam, usuram et superabundantiam non acceperit, judicia mea fecerit, in præceptis meis ambulaverit : hic non morietur in iniquitate patris sui, sed vita vivet.
       EN: withholds his hand from harming the poor, takes neither interest nor excess payment, carries out My judgments and walks in My commands—this man will not die in his father’s iniquity, but will surely live.

18:18  LA: Pater ejus, quia calumniatus est, et vim fecit fratri, et malum operatus est in medio populi sui, ecce mortuus est in iniquitate sua.
       EN: His father, because he practiced extortion, used violence against his brother, and did evil in the midst of his people—behold, he has died in his own iniquity.

18:19  LA: Et dicitis : Quare non portavit filius iniquitatem patris ? Videlicet quia filius judicium et justitiam operatus est, omnia præcepta mea custodivit, et fecit illa, vivet vita.
       EN: Yet you say, ‘Why has the son not borne his father’s iniquity?’ Because the son has practiced judgment and righteousness, kept all My commands, and carried them out, he will surely live.

18:20  LA: Anima quæ peccaverit, ipsa morietur : filius non portabit iniquitatem patris, et pater non portabit iniquitatem filii : justitia justi super eum erit, et impietas impii erit super eum.
       EN: The soul that sins shall die. The son will not bear the father’s iniquity, and the father will not bear the son’s iniquity. The righteousness of the righteous will be upon him, and the wickedness of the wicked will be upon him.

18:21  LA: Si autem impius egerit pœnitentiam ab omnibus peccatis suis quæ operatus est, et custodierit omnia præcepta mea, et fecerit judicium et justitiam, vita vivet, et non morietur.
       EN: But if the wicked man repents of all the sins he has committed, keeps all My commands, and practices judgment and righteousness, he will surely live and will not die.

18:22  LA: Omnium iniquitatum ejus quas operatus est, non recordabor : in justitia sua quam operatus est, vivet.
       EN: I will not remember any of the iniquities he has committed. In the righteousness he has practiced, he will live.

18:23  LA: Numquid voluntatis meæ est mors impii, dicit Dominus Deus, et non ut convertatur a viis suis, et vivat ?
       EN: Is the death of the wicked man what I desire, says the Lord God, and not that he should turn from his ways and live?

18:24  LA: Si autem averterit se justus a justitia sua, et fecerit iniquitatem secundum omnes abominationes quas operari solet impius, numquid vivet ? Omnes justitiæ ejus quas fecerat, non recordabuntur : in prævaricatione qua prævaricatus est, et in peccato suo quod peccavit, in ipsis morietur.
       EN: But if the righteous man turns away from his righteousness and commits iniquity according to all the abominations that the wicked man habitually commits, will he live? None of the righteous deeds he has done will be remembered. In the treachery he has committed and the sin he has sinned—in these he will die.

18:25  LA: Et dixistis : Non est æqua via Domini ! Audite ergo, domus Israël : numquid via mea non est æqua, et non magis viæ vestræ pravæ sunt ?
       EN: Yet you have said, ‘The Lord’s way is not just!’ Hear, then, house of Israel: Is My way unjust? Are not your ways crooked instead?

18:26  LA: Cum enim averterit se justus a justitia sua, et fecerit iniquitatem, morietur in eis : in injustitia quam operatus est morietur.
       EN: For when the righteous man turns away from his righteousness and commits iniquity, he will die in these things; in the injustice he has done he will die.

18:27  LA: Et cum averterit se impius ab impietate sua quam operatus est, et fecerit judicium et justitiam, ipse animam suam vivificabit :
       EN: And when the wicked man turns away from the wickedness he has practiced and carries out judgment and righteousness, he will preserve his own soul alive.

18:28  LA: considerans enim, et avertens se ab omnibus iniquitatibus suis quas operatus est, vita vivet, et non morietur.
       EN: For by considering and turning away from all the iniquities he has committed, he will surely live and will not die.

18:29  LA: Et dicunt filii Israël : Non est æqua via Domini ! Numquid viæ meæ non sunt æquæ, domus Israël, et non magis viæ vestræ pravæ ?
       EN: Yet the children of Israel say, ‘The Lord’s way is not just!’ Are My ways unjust, house of Israel? Are not your ways crooked instead?

18:30  LA: Idcirco unumquemque juxta vias suas judicabo, domus Israël, ait Dominus Deus. Convertimini, et agite pœnitentiam ab omnibus iniquitatibus vestris, et non erit vobis in ruinam iniquitas.
       EN: Therefore I will judge each one according to his ways, house of Israel, says the Lord God. Turn back and repent of all your iniquities, and iniquity will not bring you to ruin.

18:31  LA: Projicite a vobis omnes prævaricationes vestras in quibus prævaricati estis, et facite vobis cor novum, et spiritum novum : et quare moriemini, domus Israël ?
       EN: Cast away from yourselves all the transgressions you have committed, and make yourselves a new heart and a new spirit. Why will you die, house of Israel?

18:32  LA: Quia nolo mortem morientis, dicit Dominus Deus : revertimini, et vivite.
       EN: For I do not desire the death of one who dies, says the Lord God. Turn back and live.

EZEKIEL 19

19:1  LA: Et tu assume planctum super principes Israël,
      EN: “And you, take up a lament over the rulers of Israel,

19:2  LA: et dices : [Quare mater tua leæna inter leones cubavit ? in medio leunculorum enutrivit catulos suos ?
      EN: and say: Why did your mother, a lioness, lie down among lions? Why did she rear her cubs among young lions?

19:3  LA: Et eduxit unum de leunculis suis, et leo factus est : et didicit capere prædam, hominemque comedere.
      EN: She brought up one of her cubs, and he became a lion. He learned to seize prey and to eat human beings.

19:4  LA: Et audierunt de eo gentes : et non absque vulneribus suis ceperunt eum, et adduxerunt eum in catenis in terram Ægypti.
      EN: The nations heard of him; and not without wounds of their own, they captured him and brought him in chains to the land of Egypt.

19:5  LA: Quæ cum vidisset quoniam infirmata est, et periit exspectatio ejus, tulit unum de leunculis suis ; leonem constituit eum.
      EN: When she saw that she had grown weak and her hope had perished, she took one of her cubs and made him a lion.

19:6  LA: Qui incedebat inter leones, et factus est leo : et didicit prædam capere, et homines devorare :
      EN: He went about among lions and became a lion; he learned to seize prey and devour human beings.

19:7  LA: didicit viduas facere, et civitates earum in desertum adducere : et desolata est terra et plenitudo ejus a voce rugitus illius.
      EN: He learned to make widows and turn their cities into a wasteland. The land and all that filled it were desolated at the sound of his roaring.

19:8  LA: Et convenerunt adversus eum gentes undique de provinciis, et expanderunt super eum rete suum : in vulneribus earum captus est,
      EN: The nations gathered against him from the provinces on every side and spread their net over him. Through the wounds they inflicted, he was captured.

19:9  LA: et miserunt eum in caveam : in catenis adduxerunt eum ad regem Babylonis, miseruntque eum in carcerem, ne audiretur vox ejus ultra super montes Israël.
      EN: They put him in a cage and brought him in chains to the king of Babylon. They put him in prison, so that his voice would no longer be heard on the mountains of Israel.

19:10  LA: Mater tua quasi vinea in sanguine tuo super aquam plantata est : fructus ejus et frondes ejus creverunt ex aquis multis.
       EN: Your mother was like a vine in your blood, planted beside water. Its fruit and foliage grew from abundant waters.

19:11  LA: Et factæ sunt ei virgæ solidæ in sceptra dominantium, et exaltata est statura ejus inter frondes, et vidit altitudinem suam in multitudine palmitum suorum.
       EN: It had strong branches for rulers’ scepters, and its stature rose among the foliage, and it saw its own height in the multitude of its shoots.

19:12  LA: Et evulsa est in ira, in terramque projecta, et ventus urens siccavit fructum ejus : marcuerunt et arefactæ sunt virgæ roboris ejus : ignis comedit eam.
       EN: But it was uprooted in wrath and thrown to the ground, and a scorching wind dried up its fruit. Its strong branches withered and dried up; fire devoured it.

19:13  LA: Et nunc transplantata est in desertum, in terra invia et sitienti.
       EN: Now it has been transplanted into the wilderness, into a trackless and thirsty land.

19:14  LA: Et egressus est ignis de virga ramorum ejus, qui fructum ejus comedit : et non fuit in ea virga fortis, sceptrum dominantium.] Planctus est, et erit in planctum.
       EN: Fire has gone out from a rod of its branches and consumed its fruit, and no strong rod remains in it, no ruler’s scepter.” This is a lament, and it will be used as a lament.

EZEKIEL 20

20:1  LA: Et factum est in anno septimo, in quinto, in decima mensis, venerunt viri de senioribus Israël ut interrogarent Dominum, et sederunt coram me.
      EN: In the seventh year, in the fifth month, on the tenth day of the month, men from among the elders of Israel came to inquire of the Lord and sat before me.

20:2  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

20:3  LA: Fili hominis, loquere senioribus Israël, et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Numquid ad interrogandum me vos venistis ? vivo ego quia non respondebo vobis, ait Dominus Deus.
      EN: “Son of man, speak to the elders of Israel and say to them: Thus says the Lord God: Have you come to inquire of Me? As I live, I will not answer you, says the Lord God.

20:4  LA: Si judicas eos, si judicas, fili hominis, abominationes patrum eorum ostende eis.
      EN: If you judge them, if you judge, son of man, show them the abominations of their fathers.

20:5  LA: Et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : In die qua elegi Israël, et levavi manum meam pro stirpe domus Jacob, et apparui eis in terra Ægypti, et levavi manum meam pro eis, dicens : Ego Dominus Deus vester :
      EN: Say to them: Thus says the Lord God: On the day when I chose Israel, lifted My hand for the stock of the house of Jacob, and appeared to them in the land of Egypt, I lifted My hand for them, saying, ‘I am the Lord your God.’

20:6  LA: in die illa levavi manum meam pro eis ut educerem eos de terra Ægypti, in terram quam provideram eis, fluentem lacte et melle, quæ est egregia inter omnes terras.
      EN: On that day I lifted My hand for them, to bring them out of the land of Egypt into a land I had provided for them, flowing with milk and honey, outstanding among all lands.

20:7  LA: Et dixi ad eos : Unusquisque offensiones oculorum suorum abjiciat, et in idolis Ægypti nolite pollui : ego Dominus Deus vester.
      EN: I said to them, ‘Let each of you cast away the offensive things before his eyes, and do not defile yourselves with the idols of Egypt. I am the Lord your God.’

20:8  LA: Et irritaverunt me, nolueruntque me audire : unusquisque abominationes oculorum suorum non projecit, nec idola Ægypti reliquerunt. Et dixi ut effunderem indignationem meam super eos, et implerem iram meam in eis, in medio terræ Ægypti.
      EN: But they provoked Me and refused to hear Me. They did not each cast away the abominations before their eyes, nor did they abandon the idols of Egypt. I said that I would pour out My indignation upon them and spend My wrath against them in the midst of the land of Egypt.

20:9  LA: Et feci propter nomen meum, ut non violaretur coram gentibus in quarum medio erant, et inter quas apparui eis ut educerem eos de terra Ægypti.
      EN: But I acted for the sake of My name, so that it would not be profaned before the nations among whom they lived and among whom I had appeared to them to bring them out of the land of Egypt.

20:10  LA: Ejeci ergo eos de terra Ægypti, et eduxi eos in desertum.
       EN: So I brought them out of the land of Egypt and led them into the wilderness.

20:11  LA: Et dedi eis præcepta mea, et judicia mea ostendi eis, quæ faciens homo vivet in eis.
       EN: I gave them My commands and showed them My judgments, by which a person who carries them out will live.

20:12  LA: Insuper et sabbata mea dedi eis, ut essent signum inter me et eos, et scirent quia ego Dominus sanctificans eos.
       EN: Moreover, I gave them My Sabbaths as a sign between Me and them, so that they would know that I am the Lord who sanctifies them.

20:13  LA: Et irritaverunt me domus Israël in deserto : in præceptis meis non ambulaverunt, et judicia mea projecerunt, quæ faciens homo vivet in eis, et sabbata mea violaverunt vehementer. Dixi ergo ut effunderem furorem meum super eos in deserto, et consumerem eos :
       EN: But the house of Israel provoked Me in the wilderness. They did not walk in My commands and cast aside My judgments, by which a person who carries them out will live; and they gravely violated My Sabbaths. So I said that I would pour out My fury upon them in the wilderness and consume them.

20:14  LA: et feci propter nomen meum, ne violaretur coram gentibus de quibus ejeci eos in conspectu earum.
       EN: But I acted for the sake of My name, so that it would not be profaned before the nations from whom I had brought them out in their sight.

20:15  LA: Ego igitur levavi manum meam super eos in deserto, ne inducerem eos in terram quam dedi eis, fluentem lacte et melle, præcipuam terrarum omnium :
       EN: Therefore I lifted My hand against them in the wilderness, swearing not to bring them into the land I had given them, flowing with milk and honey, foremost among all lands,

20:16  LA: quia judicia mea projecerunt, et in præceptis meis non ambulaverunt, et sabbata mea violaverunt : post idola enim cor eorum gradiebatur.
       EN: because they cast aside My judgments, did not walk in My commands, and violated My Sabbaths; for their heart followed their idols.

20:17  LA: Et pepercit oculus meus super eos, ut non interficerem eos : nec consumpsi eos in deserto.
       EN: Yet My eye spared them from being destroyed, and I did not consume them in the wilderness.

20:18  LA: Dixi autem ad filios eorum in solitudine : In præceptis patrum vestrorum nolite incedere, nec judicia eorum custodiatis, nec in idolis eorum polluamini.
       EN: I said to their children in the wilderness, ‘Do not walk in the commands of your fathers, or keep their judgments, or defile yourselves with their idols.

20:19  LA: Ego Dominus Deus vester : in præceptis meis ambulate : judicia mea custodite, et facite ea,
       EN: I am the Lord your God. Walk in My commands, keep My judgments, and carry them out.

20:20  LA: et sabbata mea sanctificate, ut sint signum inter me et vos, et sciatis quia ego sum Dominus Deus vester.
       EN: Keep My Sabbaths holy as a sign between Me and you, so that you may know that I am the Lord your God.’

20:21  LA: Et exacerbaverunt me filii : in præceptis meis non ambulaverunt, et judicia mea non custodierunt ut facerent ea, quæ cum fecerit homo, vivet in eis, et sabbata mea violaverunt. Et comminatus sum ut effunderem furorem meum super eos, et implerem iram meam in eis in deserto.
       EN: But the children embittered Me. They did not walk in My commands or keep My judgments by carrying them out—judgments by which a person who carries them out will live—and they violated My Sabbaths. I threatened to pour out My fury upon them and spend My wrath against them in the wilderness.

20:22  LA: Averti autem manum meam, et feci propter nomen meum, ut non violaretur coram gentibus de quibus ejeci eos in oculis earum.
       EN: Yet I withdrew My hand and acted for the sake of My name, so that it would not be profaned before the nations from whom I had brought them out before their eyes.

20:23  LA: Iterum levavi manum meam in eos in solitudine, ut dispergerem illos in nationes, et ventilarem in terras,
       EN: Again I lifted My hand against them in the wilderness, swearing to scatter them among the nations and disperse them through the lands,

20:24  LA: eo quod judicia mea non fecissent, et præcepta mea reprobassent, et sabbata mea violassent, et post idola patrum suorum fuissent oculi eorum.
       EN: because they had not carried out My judgments, had rejected My commands and violated My Sabbaths, and their eyes had followed their fathers’ idols.

20:25  LA: Ergo et ego dedi eis præcepta non bona, et judicia in quibus non vivent.
       EN: So I too gave them commands that were not good, and judgments by which they would not live.

20:26  LA: Et pollui eos in muneribus suis, cum offerrent omne quod aperit vulvam, propter delicta sua : et scient quia ego Dominus.
       EN: I defiled them through their offerings, when they offered everything that opens the womb, because of their offenses; and they will know that I am the Lord.

20:27  LA: Quam ob rem loquere ad domum Israël, fili hominis, et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Adhuc et in hoc blasphemaverunt me patres vestri, cum sprevissent me contemnentes,
       EN: Therefore speak to the house of Israel, son of man, and say to them: Thus says the Lord God: In this too your fathers blasphemed Me, when they contemptuously rejected Me.

20:28  LA: et induxissem eos in terram super quam levavi manum meam ut darem eis : viderunt omnem collem excelsum, et omne lignum nemorosum, et immolaverunt ibi victimas suas, et dederunt ibi irritationem oblationis suæ, et posuerunt ibi odorem suavitatis suæ, et libaverunt libationes suas.
       EN: When I had brought them into the land I had lifted My hand to give them, they saw every high hill and every shady tree, and there they slaughtered their sacrifices. There they presented their provoking offerings; there they placed their pleasing fragrances and poured out their libations.

20:29  LA: Et dixi ad eos : Quid est excelsum, ad quod vos ingredimini ? et vocatum est nomen ejus Excelsum usque ad hanc diem.
       EN: I said to them, ‘What is this high place to which you go?’ And its name has been called ‘High Place’ to this day.

20:30  LA: Propterea dic ad domum Israël : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Certe in via patrum vestrorum vos polluimini, et post offendicula eorum vos fornicamini :
       EN: Therefore say to the house of Israel: Thus says the Lord God: Surely you defile yourselves in the way of your fathers and prostitute yourselves after their offensive things.

20:31  LA: et in oblatione donorum vestrorum, cum traducitis filios vestros per ignem, vos polluimini in omnibus idolis vestris usque hodie : et ego respondebo vobis, domus Israël ? Vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, quia non respondebo vobis.
       EN: In presenting your gifts, when you make your children pass through the fire, you defile yourselves with all your idols to this very day. And shall I answer you, house of Israel? As I live, says the Lord God, I will not answer you.

20:32  LA: Neque cogitatio mentis vestræ fiet, dicentium : Erimus sicut gentes et sicut cognationes terræ, ut colamus ligna et lapides.
       EN: What you have in mind will not come to pass when you say, ‘We will be like the nations and like the families of the earth, worshiping wood and stone.’

20:33  LA: Vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, quoniam in manu forti, et in brachio extento, et in furore effuso, regnabo super vos.
       EN: As I live, says the Lord God, with a strong hand, an outstretched arm, and outpoured fury, I will reign over you.

20:34  LA: Et educam vos de populis, et congregabo vos de terris in quibus dispersi estis : in manu valida, et in brachio extento, et in furore effuso, regnabo super vos.
       EN: I will bring you out from the peoples and gather you from the lands through which you have been scattered. With a mighty hand, an outstretched arm, and outpoured fury, I will reign over you.

20:35  LA: Et adducam vos in desertum populorum, et judicabor vobiscum ibi facie ad faciem.
       EN: I will bring you into the wilderness of the peoples, and there I will enter into judgment with you face to face.

20:36  LA: Sicut judicio contendi adversum patres vestros in deserto terræ Ægypti, sic judicabo vos, dicit Dominus Deus.
       EN: As I contended in judgment against your fathers in the wilderness of the land of Egypt, so I will judge you, says the Lord God.

20:37  LA: Et subjiciam vos sceptro meo, et inducam vos in vinculis fœderis.
       EN: I will subject you to My scepter and bring you into the bonds of the covenant.

20:38  LA: Et eligam de vobis transgressores et impios, et de terra incolatus eorum educam eos, et in terram Israël non ingredientur : et scietis quia ego Dominus.
       EN: I will separate out from you the transgressors and the wicked. I will bring them out of the land where they dwell, but they will not enter the land of Israel; and you will know that I am the Lord.

20:39  LA: Et vos, domus Israël, hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Singuli post idola vestra ambulate, et servite eis. Quod si et in hoc non audieritis me, et nomen meum sanctum pollueritis ultra in muneribus vestris et in idolis vestris :
       EN: As for you, house of Israel, thus says the Lord God: Go, each of you, after your idols and serve them. Even if you will not listen to Me in this and continue to profane My holy name with your gifts and your idols,

20:40  LA: in monte sancto meo, in monte excelso Israël, ait Dominus Deus, ibi serviet mihi omnis domus Israël : omnes, inquam, in terra in qua placebunt mihi : et ibi quæram primitias vestras, et initium decimarum vestrarum, in omnibus sanctificationibus vestris.
       EN: on My holy mountain, on the high mountain of Israel, says the Lord God, there the whole house of Israel will serve Me—all of them, I say, in the land where they will be pleasing to Me. There I will require your firstfruits and the first of your tithes, together with all your holy offerings.

20:41  LA: In odorem suavitatis suscipiam vos, cum eduxero vos de populis, et congregavero vos de terris in quas dispersi estis : et sanctificabor in vobis in oculis nationum.
       EN: I will receive you as a pleasing fragrance when I bring you out from the peoples and gather you from the lands through which you have been scattered; and I will show Myself holy in you before the eyes of the nations.

20:42  LA: Et scietis quia ego Dominus, cum induxero vos ad terram Israël, in terram pro qua levavi manum meam ut darem eam patribus vestris.
       EN: You will know that I am the Lord when I bring you into the land of Israel, into the land I lifted My hand to give to your fathers.

20:43  LA: Et recordabimini ibi viarum vestrarum, et omnium scelerum vestrorum, quibus polluti estis in eis : et displicebitis vobis in conspectu vestro, in omnibus malitiis vestris quas fecistis.
       EN: There you will remember your ways and all the crimes by which you defiled yourselves, and you will be disgusted with yourselves in your own sight over all the evils you have done.

20:44  LA: Et scietis quia ego Dominus, cum benefecero vobis propter nomen meum, et non secundum vias vestras malas, neque secundum scelera vestra pessima, domus Israël, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: You will know that I am the Lord when I deal kindly with you for the sake of My name, and not according to your evil ways or your utterly wicked crimes, house of Israel, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 21

21:1  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

21:2  LA: [Fili hominis, pone faciem tuam contra viam austri, et stilla ad africum, et propheta ad saltum agri meridiani.
      EN: “Son of man, set your face toward the south, let your words fall toward the southwest, and prophesy against the forest of the southern field.

21:3  LA: Et dices saltui meridiano : Audi verbum Domini : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego succendam in te ignem, et comburam in te omne lignum viride, et omne lignum aridum : non extinguetur flamma succensionis : et comburetur in ea omnis facies ab austro usque ad aquilonem,
      EN: Say to the southern forest: Hear the word of the Lord. Thus says the Lord God: Behold, I will kindle a fire in you and burn up every green tree and every dry tree within you. Its blazing flame will not be extinguished, and every face from south to north will be scorched by it.

21:4  LA: et videbit universa caro quia ego Dominus succendi eam, nec extinguetur.]
      EN: All flesh will see that I, the Lord, have kindled it, and it will not be extinguished.”

21:5  LA: Et dixi : A, a, a, Domine Deus : ipsi dicunt de me : Numquid non per parabolas loquitur iste ?
      EN: I said, “Ah, ah, ah, Lord God! They say of me, ‘Is he not speaking in parables?’”

21:6  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

21:7  LA: [Fili hominis, pone faciem tuam ad Jerusalem, et stilla ad sanctuaria, et propheta contra humum Israël.
      EN: “Son of man, set your face toward Jerusalem, let your words fall toward the sanctuaries, and prophesy against the soil of Israel.

21:8  LA: Et dices terræ Israël : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego ad te, et ejiciam gladium meum de vagina sua, et occidam in te justum et impium.
      EN: Say to the land of Israel: Thus says the Lord God: Behold, I am against you. I will draw My sword from its sheath and kill the righteous and the wicked within you.

21:9  LA: Pro eo autem quod occidi in te justum et impium, idcirco egredietur gladius meus de vagina sua ad omnem carnem, ab austro usque ad aquilonem :
      EN: Because I have killed the righteous and the wicked within you, My sword will therefore go out from its sheath against all flesh, from south to north,

21:10  LA: ut sciat omnis caro quia ego Dominus, eduxi gladium meum de vagina sua irrevocabilem.
       EN: so that all flesh may know that I, the Lord, have drawn My sword from its sheath, never to be recalled.

21:11  LA: Et tu, fili hominis, ingemisce in contritione lumborum, et in amaritudinibus ingemisce coram eis.
       EN: And you, son of man, groan with shattered loins, and groan bitterly before them.

21:12  LA: Cumque dixerint ad te : Quare tu gemis ? dices : Pro auditu : quia venit, et tabescet omne cor, et dissolventur universæ manus, et infirmabitur omnis spiritus, et per cuncta genua fluent aquæ : ecce venit, et fiet, ait Dominus Deus.]
       EN: When they say to you, ‘Why are you groaning?’ you shall say, ‘Because of the report, for it is coming. Every heart will melt, all hands will fall limp, every spirit will grow weak, and water will flow over every knee. Behold, it is coming, and it will happen, says the Lord God.’”

21:13  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

21:14  LA: Fili hominis, propheta, et dices : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : loquere : [Gladius, gladius exacutus est, et limatus :
       EN: “Son of man, prophesy and say: Thus says the Lord God. Speak: A sword, a sword has been sharpened and polished!

21:15  LA: ut cædat victimas, exacutus est : ut splendeat, limatus est : qui moves sceptrum filii mei, succidisti omne lignum.
       EN: It has been sharpened to slaughter victims; it has been polished to flash. You who shake the scepter of My son have cut down every tree.

21:16  LA: Et dedi eum ad levigandum, ut teneatur manu : iste exacutus est gladius, et iste limatus est, ut sit in manu interficientis.
       EN: I have given it to be smoothed, so that it may be held in the hand. This sword has been sharpened and polished to be in the slayer’s hand.

21:17  LA: Clama et ulula, fili hominis, quia hic factus est in populo meo, hic in cunctis ducibus Israël qui fugerant : gladio traditi sunt cum populo meo : idcirco plaude super femur,
       EN: Cry out and wail, son of man, for it has come upon My people, upon all the rulers of Israel who had fled. They have been handed over to the sword with My people. Therefore strike your thigh,

21:18  LA: quia probatus est : et hoc, cum sceptrum subverterit, et non erit, dicit Dominus Deus.
       EN: for it has been tested; and this will happen when it has overthrown the scepter and the scepter is no more, says the Lord God.

21:19  LA: Tu ergo, fili hominis, propheta, et percute manu ad manum : et duplicetur gladius, ac triplicetur gladius interfectorum : hic est gladius occisionis magnæ, qui obstupescere eos facit
       EN: You, therefore, son of man, prophesy and strike hand against hand. Let the sword be doubled, and let the sword of the slain be tripled. This is the sword of the great slaughter that stuns them

21:20  LA: et corde tabescere, et multiplicat ruinas. In omnibus portis eorum dedi conturbationem gladii acuti, et limati ad fulgendum, amicti ad cædem.
       EN: and melts their hearts and multiplies their falls. At all their gates I have set the terror of a sharp sword, polished to flash, clothed for slaughter.

21:21  LA: Exacuere, vade ad dexteram sive ad sinistram, quocumque faciei tuæ est appetitus.
       EN: Be sharpened! Go to the right or to the left, wherever your face is drawn.

21:22  LA: Quin et ego plaudam manu ad manum, et implebo indignationem meam : ego Dominus locutus sum.]
       EN: I too will strike hand against hand and spend My indignation. I, the Lord, have spoken.”

21:23  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

21:24  LA: Et tu, fili hominis, pone tibi duas vias, ut veniat gladius regis Babylonis : de terra una egredientur ambæ : et manu capiet conjecturam ; in capite viæ civitatis conjiciet.
       EN: “And you, son of man, mark out two roads for the sword of the king of Babylon to come. Both will go out from one land. He will take an omen in his hand; at the head of the road to the city he will divine.

21:25  LA: Viam pones ut veniat gladius ad Rabbath filiorum Ammon, et ad Judam in Jerusalem munitissimam.
       EN: Mark out a road for the sword to come to Rabbah of the children of Ammon, and to Judah in strongly fortified Jerusalem.

21:26  LA: Stetit enim rex Babylonis in bivio, in capite duarum viarum, divinationem quærens, commiscens sagittas : interrogavit idola, exta consuluit.
       EN: For the king of Babylon has stood at the fork, at the head of the two roads, seeking divination, mixing arrows. He has questioned idols and consulted entrails.

21:27  LA: Ad dexteram ejus facta est divinatio super Jerusalem, ut ponat arietes, ut aperiat os in cæde, ut elevet vocem in ululatu, ut ponat arietes contra portas, ut comportet aggerem, ut ædificet munitiones.
       EN: On his right the divination pointed to Jerusalem: to place battering rams, to open the mouth for slaughter, to raise the voice in a war cry, to place battering rams against the gates, to heap up a ramp, and to build siege works.

21:28  LA: Eritque quasi consulens frustra oraculum in oculis eorum, et sabbatorum otium imitans : ipse autem recordabitur iniquitatis ad capiendum.
       EN: In their eyes he will be like someone consulting an oracle in vain and imitating the idleness of Sabbaths. But he himself will remember iniquity, to take them captive.

21:29  LA: Idcirco hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pro eo quod recordati estis iniquitatis vestræ, et revelastis prævaricationes vestras, et apparuerunt peccata vestra in omnibus cogitationibus vestris, pro eo, inquam, quod recordati estis, manu capiemini.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Because you have recalled your iniquity, exposed your transgressions, and made your sins appear in all your thoughts—because, I say, you have recalled them—you will be seized by hand.

21:30  LA: Tu autem, profane, impie dux Israël, cujus venit dies in tempore iniquitatis præfinita :
       EN: And you, profane and wicked ruler of Israel, whose day has come at the time appointed for iniquity,

21:31  LA: hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Aufer cidarim, tolle coronam : nonne hæc est quæ humilem sublevavit, et sublimem humiliavit ?
       EN: thus says the Lord God: Remove the turban, take away the crown. Is not this the crown that raised the lowly and brought down the exalted?

21:32  LA: Iniquitatem, iniquitatem, iniquitatem ponam eam : et hoc non factum est, donec veniret cujus est judicium, et tradam ei.
       EN: Iniquity, iniquity, iniquity I will make it. This has not been done until he comes to whom judgment belongs, and I will give it to him.

21:33  LA: Et tu, fili hominis, propheta, et dic : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus ad filios Ammon, et ad opprobrium eorum : et dices : [Mucro, mucro, evaginate ad occidendum : limate ut interficias et fulgeas :
       EN: “And you, son of man, prophesy and say: Thus says the Lord God concerning the children of Ammon and their reproach. Say: Sword, sword, drawn to kill, polished to slay and flash,

21:34  LA: cum tibi viderentur vana, et divinarentur mendacia, ut dareris super colla vulneratorum impiorum, quorum venit dies in tempore iniquitatis præfinita.
       EN: while empty visions were seen for you and lies divined for you, so that you might be laid upon the necks of the wounded wicked, whose day has come at the time appointed for iniquity.

21:35  LA: Revertere ad vaginam tuam, in loco in quo creatus es : in terra nativitatis tuæ judicabo te.
       EN: Return to your sheath, in the place where you were created. In the land of your birth I will judge you.

21:36  LA: Et effundam super te indignationem meam ; in igne furoris mei sufflabo in te : daboque te in manus hominum insipientium, et fabricantium interitum.
       EN: I will pour out My indignation upon you and blow upon you in the fire of My fury. I will give you into the hands of senseless men who fashion destruction.

21:37  LA: Igni eris cibus ; sanguis tuus erit in medio terræ ; oblivioni traderis : quia ego Dominus locutus sum.]
       EN: You will be food for fire; your blood will be in the midst of the land; you will be consigned to oblivion. For I, the Lord, have spoken.”

EZEKIEL 22

22:1  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

22:2  LA: Et tu, fili hominis, nonne judicas, nonne judicas civitatem sanguinum ?
      EN: “And you, son of man, will you not judge, will you not judge the city of bloodshed?

22:3  LA: Et ostendes ei omnes abominationes suas, et dices : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Civitas effundens sanguinem in medio sui, ut veniat tempus ejus : et quæ fecit idola contra semetipsam, ut pollueretur.
      EN: Show her all her abominations, and say: Thus says the Lord God: A city shedding blood within herself, so that her time may come; a city that has made idols against herself, to defile herself!

22:4  LA: In sanguine tuo, qui a te effusus est, deliquisti, et in idolis tuis, quæ fecisti, polluta es : et appropinquare fecisti dies tuos, et adduxisti tempus annorum tuorum : propterea dedi te opprobrium gentibus, et irrisionem universis terris.
      EN: You have incurred guilt through your blood that you have shed, and defiled yourself through your idols that you have made. You have brought your days near and ushered in the time of your years. Therefore I have made you a reproach to the nations and a mockery to all lands.

22:5  LA: Quæ juxta sunt, et quæ procul a te, triumphabunt de te, sordida, nobilis, grandis interitu.
      EN: Those near you and those far from you will gloat over you, filthy, renowned, great in ruin.

22:6  LA: Ecce principes Israël singuli in brachio suo fuerunt in te, ad effundendum sanguinem.
      EN: Behold, the rulers of Israel have each used their own power within you to shed blood.

22:7  LA: Patrem et matrem contumeliis affecerunt : in te advenam calumniati sunt in medio tui : pupillum et viduam contristaverunt apud te.
      EN: They have treated father and mother with contempt. Within you they have extorted from the foreigner in your midst; among you they have afflicted the orphan and the widow.

22:8  LA: Sanctuaria mea sprevisti, et sabbata mea polluisti.
      EN: You have despised My sanctuaries and defiled My Sabbaths.

22:9  LA: Viri detractores fuerunt in te ad effundendum sanguinem, et super montes comederunt in te : scelus operati sunt in medio tui.
      EN: Slanderous men have been within you to shed blood, and within you they have eaten on the mountains. They have committed crime in your midst.

22:10  LA: Verecundiora patris discooperuerunt in te ; immunditiam menstruatæ humiliaverunt in te :
       EN: Within you they have uncovered a father’s private parts; within you they have violated a woman in the uncleanness of her menstruation.

22:11  LA: et unusquisque in uxorem proximi sui operatus est abominationem, et socer nurum suam polluit nefarie : frater sororem suam, filiam patris sui, oppressit in te.
       EN: Each has committed abomination with his neighbor’s wife, and a father-in-law has wickedly defiled his daughter-in-law. Within you a brother has violated his sister, his father’s daughter.

22:12  LA: Munera acceperunt apud te ad effundendum sanguinem : usuram et superabundantiam accepisti, et avare proximos tuos calumniabaris : meique oblita es, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: Among you they have accepted bribes to shed blood. You have taken interest and excess payment, and greedily extorted from your neighbors; and you have forgotten Me, says the Lord God.

22:13  LA: Ecce complosi manus meas super avaritiam tuam quam fecisti, et super sanguinem qui effusus est in medio tui.
       EN: Behold, I have struck My hands together over the greed you have practiced and the blood that has been shed in your midst.

22:14  LA: Numquid sustinebit cor tuum, aut prævalebunt manus tuæ, in diebus quos ego faciam tibi ? Ego Dominus locutus sum, et faciam.
       EN: Will your heart endure, or your hands prevail, in the days when I deal with you? I, the Lord, have spoken, and I will do it.

22:15  LA: Et dispergam te in nationes, et ventilabo te in terras, et deficere faciam immunditiam tuam a te.
       EN: I will scatter you among the nations and disperse you through the lands, and make your uncleanness cease from you.

22:16  LA: Et possidebo te in conspectu gentium : et scies quia ego Dominus.
