       EN: I will possess you in the sight of the nations, and you will know that I am the Lord.”

22:17  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

22:18  LA: Fili hominis, versa est mihi domus Israël in scoriam : omnes isti æs, et stannum, et ferrum, et plumbum in medio fornacis : scoria argenti facti sunt.
       EN: “Son of man, the house of Israel has become dross to Me. All these are bronze, tin, iron, and lead in the midst of a furnace; they have become the dross of silver.

22:19  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Eo quod versi estis omnes in scoriam, propterea ecce ego congregabo vos in medio Jerusalem,
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Because you have all become dross, behold, I will therefore gather you in the midst of Jerusalem,

22:20  LA: congregatione argenti, et æris, et stanni, et ferri, et plumbi, in medio fornacis, ut succendam in ea ignem ad conflandum. Sic congregabo in furore meo, et in ira mea : et requiescam, et conflabo vos.
       EN: as silver, bronze, tin, iron, and lead are gathered into a furnace, so that I may kindle a fire in it to melt them. So I will gather you in My fury and My wrath; I will settle and melt you.

22:21  LA: Et congregabo vos, et succendam vos in igne furoris mei, et conflabimini in medio ejus.
       EN: I will gather you and set you ablaze in the fire of My fury, and you will be melted within it.

22:22  LA: Ut conflatur argentum in medio fornacis, sic eritis in medio ejus : et scietis quia ego Dominus cum effuderim indignationem meam super vos.
       EN: As silver is melted in a furnace, so you will be in its midst; and you will know that I am the Lord when I have poured out My indignation upon you.”

22:23  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

22:24  LA: Fili hominis, dic ei : Tu es terra immunda, et non compluta in die furoris.
       EN: “Son of man, say to her: You are an unclean land, not rained upon on the day of fury.

22:25  LA: Conjuratio prophetarum in medio ejus : sicut leo rugiens, rapiensque prædam, animas devoraverunt : opes et pretium acceperunt : viduas ejus multiplicaverunt in medio illius.
       EN: There is a conspiracy of prophets within her. Like a roaring lion seizing prey, they have devoured souls, taken wealth and payment, and multiplied her widows within her.

22:26  LA: Sacerdotes ejus contempserunt legem meam, et polluerunt sanctuaria mea : inter sanctum et profanum non habuerunt distantiam, et inter pollutum et mundum non intellexerunt : et a sabbatis meis averterunt oculos suos, et coinquinabar in medio eorum.
       EN: Her priests have despised My law and defiled My sanctuaries. They have made no distinction between holy and profane, and have not discerned between unclean and clean. They have turned their eyes away from My Sabbaths, and I have been profaned among them.

22:27  LA: Principes ejus in medio illius quasi lupi rapientes prædam ad effundendum sanguinem, et ad perdendas animas, et avare ad sectanda lucra.
       EN: Her rulers within her are like wolves seizing prey, shedding blood and destroying souls, greedily pursuing profit.

22:28  LA: Prophetæ autem ejus liniebant eos absque temperamento, videntes vana, et divinantes eis mendacium, dicentes : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : cum Dominus non sit locutus.
       EN: Her prophets coated them with unmixed mortar, seeing empty things and divining a lie for them, saying, ‘Thus says the Lord God,’ when the Lord had not spoken.

22:29  LA: Populi terræ calumniabantur calumniam, et rapiebant violenter : egenum et pauperem affligebant, et advenam opprimebant calumnia absque judicio.
       EN: The peoples of the land practiced extortion and seized things by violence. They afflicted the needy and the poor, and oppressed the foreigner through extortion, without justice.

22:30  LA: Et quæsivi de eis virum qui interponeret sepem, et staret oppositus contra me pro terra, ne dissiparem eam : et non inveni.
       EN: I sought among them a man who would set up a hedge and stand facing Me on behalf of the land, so that I would not destroy it; but I found none.

22:31  LA: Et effudi super eos indignationem meam ; in igne iræ meæ consumpsi eos : viam eorum in caput eorum reddidi, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: I poured out My indignation upon them and consumed them in the fire of My wrath. I brought their way back upon their own heads, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 23

23:1  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

23:2  LA: [Fili hominis, duæ mulieres filiæ matris unius fuerunt :
      EN: “Son of man, there were two women, daughters of one mother.

23:3  LA: et fornicatæ sunt in Ægypto, in adolescentia sua fornicatæ sunt : ibi subacta sunt ubera earum, et fractæ sunt mammæ pubertatis earum.
      EN: They prostituted themselves in Egypt; in their youth they prostituted themselves. There their breasts were handled and their virgin breasts bruised.

23:4  LA: Nomina autem earum, Oolla major, et Ooliba soror ejus minor : et habui eas, et pepererunt filios et filias. Porro earum nomina, Samaria Oolla, et Jerusalem Ooliba.
      EN: Their names were Oholah, the elder, and Oholibah, her younger sister. I took them as Mine, and they bore sons and daughters. As for their names, Samaria is Oholah, and Jerusalem is Oholibah.

23:5  LA: Fornicata est igitur super me Oolla, et insanivit in amatores suos, in Assyrios propinquantes,
      EN: Oholah prostituted herself against Me and was mad for her lovers, the Assyrians who came near,

23:6  LA: vestitos hyacintho, principes et magistratus, juvenes cupidinis, universos equites, ascensores equorum.
      EN: clothed in blue, rulers and officials, desirable young men, all horsemen riding horses.

23:7  LA: Et dedit fornicationes suas super eos electos, filios Assyriorum universos : et in omnibus in quos insanivit, in immunditiis eorum polluta est.
      EN: She bestowed her prostitutions upon them, all these choice sons of the Assyrians; and with all those for whom she was mad, she defiled herself in their uncleannesses.

23:8  LA: Insuper et fornicationes suas, quas habuerat in Ægypto, non reliquit : nam et illi dormierunt cum ea in adolescentia ejus, et illi confregerunt ubera pubertatis ejus, et effuderunt fornicationem suam super eam.
      EN: Moreover, she did not abandon the prostitutions she had practiced in Egypt. For they too had lain with her in her youth; they had bruised her virgin breasts and poured out their prostitution upon her.

23:9  LA: Propterea tradidi eam in manus amatorum suorum, in manus filiorum Assur, super quorum insanivit libidine.
      EN: Therefore I delivered her into the hands of her lovers, into the hands of the sons of Assyria, for whose lust she had gone mad.

23:10  LA: Ipsi discooperuerunt ignominiam ejus, filios et filias ejus tulerunt, et ipsam occiderunt gladio : et factæ sunt famosæ mulieres, et judicia perpetraverunt in ea.
       EN: They uncovered her shame, took her sons and daughters, and killed her with the sword. The women became notorious, and they carried out judgments upon her.

23:11  LA: Quod cum vidisset soror ejus Ooliba, plus quam illa insanivit libidine, et fornicationem suam super fornicationem sororis suæ :
       EN: When her sister Oholibah saw this, she became more frenzied with lust than Oholah, and her prostitution exceeded her sister’s prostitution.

23:12  LA: ad filios Assyriorum præbuit impudenter, ducibus et magistratibus ad se venientibus, indutis veste varia, equitibus qui vectabantur equis, et adolescentibus forma cunctis egregia.
       EN: She offered herself shamelessly to the sons of the Assyrians: rulers and officials who came to her, clothed in varied garments, horsemen riding horses, and young men all outstanding in appearance.

23:13  LA: Et vidi quod polluta esset via una ambarum.
       EN: I saw that both were defiled in the same way.

23:14  LA: Et auxit fornicationes suas : cumque vidisset viros depictos in pariete, imagines Chaldæorum expressas coloribus,
       EN: She increased her prostitutions. When she saw men painted on a wall, images of Chaldeans portrayed in colors,

23:15  LA: et accinctos balteis renes, et tiaras tinctas in capitibus eorum, formam ducum omnium, similitudinem filiorum Babylonis, terræque Chaldæorum, in qua orti sunt,
       EN: with belts around their waists and dyed turbans on their heads, all with the appearance of rulers, likenesses of the sons of Babylon and of the land of the Chaldeans in which they were born,

23:16  LA: insanivit super eos concupiscentia oculorum suorum, et misit nuntios ad eos in Chaldæam.
       EN: she went mad for them with the desire of her eyes and sent messengers to them in Chaldea.

23:17  LA: Cumque venissent ad eam filii Babylonis ad cubile mammarum, polluerunt eam stupris suis : et polluta est ab eis, et saturata est anima ejus ab illis.
       EN: When the sons of Babylon came to her, to the bed of her breasts, they defiled her with their illicit acts. She was defiled by them, and her soul was sated with them.

23:18  LA: Denudavit quoque fornicationes suas, et discooperuit ignominiam suam : et recessit anima mea ab ea, sicut recesserat anima mea a sorore ejus :
       EN: She laid bare her prostitutions and uncovered her shame, and My soul withdrew from her as My soul had withdrawn from her sister.

23:19  LA: multiplicavit enim fornicationes suas, recordans dies adolescentiæ suæ, quibus fornicata est in terra Ægypti.
       EN: For she multiplied her prostitutions, remembering the days of her youth when she prostituted herself in the land of Egypt.

23:20  LA: Et insanivit libidine super concubitum eorum, quorum carnes sunt ut carnes asinorum, et sicut fluxus equorum fluxus eorum.
       EN: She went mad with lust for intercourse with them, whose flesh is like the flesh of donkeys and whose emission is like the emission of horses.

23:21  LA: Et visitasti scelus adolescentiæ tuæ, quando subacta sunt in Ægypto ubera tua, et confractæ sunt mammæ pubertatis tuæ.]
       EN: You revisited the crime of your youth, when your breasts were handled in Egypt and your virgin breasts bruised.

23:22  LA: [Propterea, Ooliba, hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego suscitabo omnes amatores tuos contra te, de quibus satiata est anima tua, et congregabo eos adversum te in circuitu :
       EN: “Therefore, Oholibah, thus says the Lord God: Behold, I will rouse against you all your lovers with whom your soul was sated, and gather them against you on every side:

23:23  LA: filios Babylonis, et universos Chaldæos, nobiles, tyrannosque, et principes, omnes filios Assyriorum, juvenes forma egregia, duces et magistratus universos, principes principum, et nominatos ascensores equorum :
       EN: the sons of Babylon and all the Chaldeans, nobles, tyrants, and rulers, all the sons of the Assyrians, young men outstanding in appearance, all rulers and officials, princes of princes and renowned horsemen.

23:24  LA: et venient super te instructi curru et rota, multitudo populorum : lorica, et clypeo, et galea armabuntur contra te undique : et dabo coram eis judicium, et judicabunt te judiciis suis.
       EN: They will come against you equipped with chariot and wheel, a multitude of peoples. With breastplate, shield, and helmet they will arm themselves against you on every side. I will set judgment before them, and they will judge you according to their judgments.

23:25  LA: Et ponam zelum meum in te, quem exercent tecum in furore : nasum tuum et aures tuas præcident, et quæ remanserint, gladio concident. Ipsi filios tuos et filias tuas capient, et novissimum tuum devorabitur igni :
       EN: I will set My jealousy against you, which they will exercise upon you in fury. They will cut off your nose and your ears, and cut down what remains with the sword. They will take your sons and daughters, and the last of you will be devoured by fire.

23:26  LA: et denudabunt te vestimentis tuis, et tollent vasa gloriæ tuæ.
       EN: They will strip you of your garments and take away your splendid vessels.

23:27  LA: Et requiescere faciam scelus tuum de te, et fornicationem tuam de terra Ægypti : nec levabis oculos tuos ad eos, et Ægypti non recordaberis amplius.]
       EN: I will put an end to your crime and to your prostitution from the land of Egypt. You will no longer lift your eyes to them or remember Egypt again.

23:28  LA: [Quia hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego tradam te in manus eorum quos odisti, in manus de quibus satiata est anima tua.
       EN: “For thus says the Lord God: Behold, I will deliver you into the hands of those you hate, into the hands of those with whom your soul was sated.

23:29  LA: Et agent tecum in odio, et tollent omnes labores tuos, et dimittent te nudam et ignominia plenam : et revelabitur ignominia fornicationum tuarum, scelus tuum, et fornicationes tuæ.
       EN: They will deal with you in hatred, take all the fruit of your labors, and leave you naked and full of shame. The shame of your prostitutions will be exposed, your crime and your prostitutions.

23:30  LA: Fecerunt hæc tibi, quia fornicata es post gentes inter quas polluta es in idolis earum.
       EN: They have done these things to you because you prostituted yourself after the nations, among whom you were defiled by their idols.

23:31  LA: In via sororis tuæ ambulasti, et dabo calicem ejus in manu tua.
       EN: You have walked in your sister’s way, and I will place her cup in your hand.

23:32  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Calicem sororis tuæ bibes profundum et latum : eris in derisum et in subsannationem quæ est capacissima.
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: You will drink your sister’s cup, deep and wide. You will become an object of derision and of mockery beyond measure.

23:33  LA: Ebrietate et dolore repleberis : calice mœroris et tristitiæ, calice sororis tuæ Samariæ.
       EN: You will be filled with drunkenness and pain, with a cup of grief and sorrow, the cup of your sister Samaria.

23:34  LA: Et bibes illum, et epotabis usque ad fæces : et fragmenta ejus devorabis, et ubera tua lacerabis, quia ego locutus sum, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: You will drink it and drain it to the dregs. You will devour its fragments and tear your breasts, for I have spoken, says the Lord God.

23:35  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Quia oblita es mei, et projecisti me post corpus tuum, tu quoque porta scelus tuum et fornicationes tuas.]
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Because you have forgotten Me and cast Me behind your back, you too must bear your crime and your prostitutions.”

23:36  LA: Et ait Dominus ad me, dicens : [Fili hominis, numquid judicas Oollam et Oolibam, et annuntias eis scelera earum ?
       EN: The Lord spoke to me, saying, “Son of man, will you judge Oholah and Oholibah and declare their crimes to them?

23:37  LA: Quia adulteratæ sunt, et sanguis in manibus earum, et cum idolis suis fornicatæ sunt : insuper et filios suos quos genuerunt mihi, obtulerunt eis ad devorandum.
       EN: For they have committed adultery, and blood is on their hands. They have prostituted themselves with their idols, and have even offered to them for food their children whom they bore to Me.

23:38  LA: Sed et hoc fecerunt mihi : polluerunt sanctuarium meum in die illa, et sabbata mea profanaverunt.
       EN: This too they have done to Me: they defiled My sanctuary on that day and profaned My Sabbaths.

23:39  LA: Cumque immolarent filios suos idolis suis, et ingrederentur sanctuarium meum in die illa ut polluerent illud, etiam hæc fecerunt in medio domus meæ.
       EN: When they sacrificed their children to their idols and entered My sanctuary on that day to defile it, they did even these things in the midst of My house.

23:40  LA: Miserunt ad viros venientes de longe, ad quos nuntium miserant : itaque ecce venerunt quibus te lavisti, et circumlinisti stibio oculos tuos, et ornata es mundo muliebri.
       EN: They sent for men coming from far away, to whom they had sent a messenger; and behold, they came. For them you washed yourself, painted your eyes with antimony, and adorned yourself with a woman’s finery.

23:41  LA: Sedisti in lecto pulcherrimo, et mensa ornata est ante te : thymiama meum et unguentum meum posuisti super eam.
       EN: You sat upon a beautiful couch, and a table was prepared before you. You placed My incense and My ointment upon it.

23:42  LA: Et vox multitudinis exsultantis erat in ea : et in viris, qui de multitudine hominum adducebantur, et veniebant de deserto, posuerunt armillas in manibus eorum, et coronas speciosas in capitibus eorum.
       EN: The sound of a rejoicing multitude was in her. On the men brought in from the multitude of people, who came from the wilderness, they placed bracelets on their wrists and splendid crowns on their heads.

23:43  LA: Et dixi ei, quæ attrita est in adulteriis : Nunc fornicabitur in fornicatione sua etiam hæc.
       EN: I said of her who was worn out in adulteries, ‘Now she too will prostitute herself in her prostitution.’

23:44  LA: Et ingressi sunt ad eam quasi ad mulierem meretricem : sic ingrediebantur ad Oollam et Oolibam, mulieres nefarias.
       EN: They went in to her as to a prostitute; thus they went in to Oholah and Oholibah, wicked women.

23:45  LA: Viri ergo justi sunt : hi judicabunt eas judicio adulterarum, et judicio effundentium sanguinem : quia adulteræ sunt, et sanguis in manibus earum.
       EN: There are righteous men, therefore: these will judge them with the judgment of adulteresses and of those who shed blood, for they are adulteresses, and blood is on their hands.

23:46  LA: Hæc enim dicit Dominus Deus : Adduc ad eas multitudinem, et trade eas in tumultum et in rapinam.
       EN: For thus says the Lord God: Bring a multitude against them and hand them over to turmoil and plunder.

23:47  LA: Et lapidentur lapidibus populorum, et confodiantur gladiis eorum : filios et filias earum interficient, et domos earum igne succendent.
       EN: Let them be stoned with the peoples’ stones and pierced with their swords. They will kill their sons and daughters and set their houses on fire.

23:48  LA: Et auferam scelus de terra, et discent omnes mulieres ne faciant secundum scelus earum.
       EN: I will remove crime from the land, and all women will learn not to act according to their crime.

23:49  LA: Et dabunt scelus vestrum super vos, et peccata idolorum vestrorum portabitis : et scietis quia ego Dominus Deus.]
       EN: They will bring your crime upon you, and you will bear the sins of your idols; and you will know that I am the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 24

24:1  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me in anno nono, in mense decimo, decima die mensis, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me in the ninth year, in the tenth month, on the tenth day of the month, saying,

24:2  LA: Fili hominis, scribe tibi nomen diei hujus, in qua confirmatus est rex Babylonis adversum Jerusalem hodie.
      EN: “Son of man, write down the date of this day, this very day on which the king of Babylon has established himself against Jerusalem.

24:3  LA: Et dices per proverbium ad domum irritatricem parabolam, et loqueris ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pone ollam ; pone, inquam, et mitte in eam aquam.
      EN: Tell the provoking house a parable in the form of a proverb, and say to them: Thus says the Lord God: Set on the pot; set it on, I say, and pour water into it.

24:4  LA: Congere frusta ejus in eam, omnem partem bonam, femur et armum, electa et ossibus plena.
      EN: Gather its pieces into it, every good piece, thigh and shoulder, choice cuts full of bones.

24:5  LA: Pinguissimum pecus assume, compone quoque strues ossium sub ea : efferbuit coctio ejus, et discocta sunt ossa illius in medio ejus.
      EN: Take the fattest animal, and also arrange piles of bones beneath the pot. Its contents have boiled up, and its bones have been cooked through within it.

24:6  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Væ civitati sanguinum, ollæ cujus rubigo in ea est, et rubigo ejus non exivit de ea ! per partes et per partes suas ejice eam : non cecidit super eam sors.
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Woe to the city of bloodshed, the pot whose rust is within it and whose rust has not left it! Empty it piece by piece; no lot has fallen upon it.

24:7  LA: Sanguis enim ejus in medio ejus est ; super limpidissimam petram effudit illum : non effudit illum super terram, ut possit operiri pulvere.
      EN: For her blood is in her midst. She poured it upon the barest rock; she did not pour it upon the ground where it could be covered with dust.

24:8  LA: Ut superinducerem indignationem meam, et vindicta ulciscerer, dedi sanguinem ejus super petram limpidissimam, ne operiretur.
      EN: To bring on My indignation and exact vengeance, I have placed her blood upon the barest rock, so that it would not be covered.

24:9  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Væ civitati sanguinum, cujus ego grandem faciam pyram !
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Woe to the city of bloodshed, for which I will make a great pyre!

24:10  LA: Congere ossa, quæ igne succendam : consumentur carnes, et coquetur universa compositio, et ossa tabescent.
       EN: Heap up the bones, which I will set ablaze. The flesh will be consumed, the entire mixture will be cooked, and the bones will waste away.

24:11  LA: Pone quoque eam super prunas vacuam, ut incalescat, et liquefiat æs ejus, et confletur in medio ejus inquinamentum ejus, et consumatur rubigo ejus.
       EN: Set the empty pot upon the coals as well, so that it may grow hot and its bronze melt, its filth be melted within it, and its rust be consumed.

24:12  LA: Multo labore sudatum est, et non exivit de ea nimia rubigo ejus, neque per ignem.
       EN: Much labor and sweat have been spent, yet its heavy rust has not left it, even through fire.

24:13  LA: Immunditia tua execrabilis, quia mundare te volui, et non es mundata a sordibus tuis : sed nec mundaberis prius, donec quiescere faciam indignationem meam in te.
       EN: Your uncleanness is abhorrent, because I wanted to cleanse you, yet you were not cleansed of your filth. Nor will you be cleansed before I have brought My indignation against you to rest.

24:14  LA: Ego Dominus locutus sum : veniet, et faciam : non transeam, nec parcam, nec placabor : juxta vias tuas, et juxta adinventiones tuas judicabo te, dicit Dominus.
       EN: I, the Lord, have spoken. It will come, and I will do it. I will not pass over it, or spare, or be appeased. According to your ways and your deeds I will judge you, says the Lord.”

24:15  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

24:16  LA: Fili hominis, ecce ego tollo a te desiderabile oculorum tuorum in plaga : et non planges, neque plorabis, neque fluent lacrimæ tuæ.
       EN: “Son of man, behold, with a blow I am taking from you the delight of your eyes. Yet you shall not lament or weep, nor shall your tears flow.

24:17  LA: Ingemisce tacens : mortuorum luctum non facies : corona tua circumligata sit tibi, et calceamenta tua erunt in pedibus tuis : nec amictu ora velabis, nec cibos lugentium comedes.
       EN: Groan silently; do not make mourning for the dead. Let your headband be tied around you, and let your shoes be on your feet. Do not cover your face with your mantle or eat the food of mourners.”

24:18  LA: Locutus sum ergo ad populum mane, et mortua est uxor mea vespere : fecique mane sicut præceperat mihi.
       EN: So I spoke to the people in the morning, and my wife died in the evening. In the morning I did as He had commanded me.

24:19  LA: Et dixit ad me populus : Quare non indicas nobis quid ista significent quæ tu facis ?
       EN: The people said to me, “Why do you not tell us what these things you are doing mean?”

24:20  LA: Et dixi ad eos : Sermo Domini factus est ad me, dicens :
       EN: I said to them, “The word of the Lord came to me, saying,

24:21  LA: Loquere domui Israël : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego polluam sanctuarium meum, superbiam imperii vestri, et desiderabile oculorum vestrorum, et super quo pavet anima vestra : filii vestri et filiæ vestræ quas reliquistis, gladio cadent.
       EN: ‘Speak to the house of Israel: Thus says the Lord God: Behold, I will defile My sanctuary, the pride of your power, the delight of your eyes, and that over which your soul trembles. Your sons and daughters whom you left behind will fall by the sword.

24:22  LA: Et facietis sicut feci : ora amictu non velabitis, et cibos lugentium non comedetis :
       EN: You will do as I have done. You will not cover your faces with your mantles or eat the food of mourners.

24:23  LA: coronas habebitis in capitibus vestris, et calceamenta in pedibus : non plangetis, neque flebitis, sed tabescetis in iniquitatibus vestris, et unusquisque gemet ad fratrem suum.
       EN: You will have headbands on your heads and shoes on your feet. You will not lament or weep, but will waste away in your iniquities, and each will groan to his brother.

24:24  LA: Eritque Ezechiel vobis in portentum : juxta omnia quæ fecit, facietis cum venerit istud : et scietis quia ego Dominus Deus.
       EN: Ezekiel will be a portent for you. You will do according to all that he has done when this comes, and you will know that I am the Lord God.’

24:25  LA: Et tu, fili hominis, ecce in die qua tollam ab eis fortitudinem eorum, et gaudium dignitatis, et desiderium oculorum eorum, super quo requiescunt animæ eorum, filios et filias eorum :
       EN: “And you, son of man, behold, on the day when I take from them their strength, the joy of their dignity, the desire of their eyes on which their souls rest, their sons and daughters,

24:26  LA: in die illa, cum venerit fugiens ad te ut annuntiet tibi :
       EN: on that day, when a fugitive comes to you to tell you,

24:27  LA: in die, inquam illa, aperietur os tuum cum eo qui fugit, et loqueris, et non silebis ultra : erisque eis in portentum, et scietis quia ego Dominus.
       EN: on that day, I say, your mouth will be opened with the fugitive, and you will speak and no longer be silent. You will be a portent for them, and you will know that I am the Lord.”

EZEKIEL 25

25:1  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

25:2  LA: Fili hominis, pone faciem tuam contra filios Ammon, et prophetabis de eis.
      EN: “Son of man, set your face against the children of Ammon and prophesy concerning them.

25:3  LA: Et dices filiis Ammon : [Audite verbum Domini Dei. Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pro eo quod dixisti : Euge, euge, super sanctuarium meum, quia pollutum est ; et super terram Israël, quoniam desolata est ; et super domum Juda, quoniam ducti sunt in captivitatem :
      EN: Say to the children of Ammon: Hear the word of the Lord God. Thus says the Lord God: Because you said, ‘Aha, aha!’ over My sanctuary, because it was defiled, over the land of Israel, because it was desolated, and over the house of Judah, because they were led into captivity,

25:4  LA: idcirco ego tradam te filiis orientalibus in hæreditatem : et collocabunt caulas suas in te, et ponent in te tentoria sua : ipsi comedent fruges tuas, et ipsi bibent lac tuum.
      EN: therefore I will hand you over to the children of the east as an inheritance. They will set their folds among you and pitch their tents among you; they will eat your produce and drink your milk.

25:5  LA: Daboque Rabbath in habitaculum camelorum, et filios Ammon in cubile pecorum : et scietis quia ego Dominus.
      EN: I will make Rabbah a dwelling for camels and the children of Ammon a resting place for flocks, and you will know that I am the Lord.

25:6  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pro eo quod plausisti manu et percussisti pede, et gavisa es ex toto affectu super terram Israël,
      EN: For thus says the Lord God: Because you clapped your hand and stamped your foot and rejoiced with all your heart over the land of Israel,

25:7  LA: idcirco ecce ego extendam manum meam super te, et tradam te in direptionem gentium, et interficiam te de populis, et perdam de terris, et conteram : et scies quia ego Dominus.]
      EN: therefore, see, I will stretch out My hand against you and hand you over to the nations for plunder. I will cut you off from the peoples, destroy you from the lands, and crush you, and you will know that I am the Lord.

25:8  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pro eo quod dixerunt Moab et Seir : Ecce sicut omnes gentes, domus Juda :
      EN: Thus says the Lord God: Because Moab and Seir said, ‘See, the house of Judah is like all the nations,’

25:9  LA: idcirco ecce ego aperiam humerum Moab de civitatibus, de civitatibus, inquam, ejus, et de finibus ejus, inclytas terræ Bethiesimoth, et Beelmeon, et Cariathaim,
      EN: therefore, see, I will open Moab's flank from the cities—from its cities, I say—and from its borders, the glories of the land: Beth-jeshimoth, Baal-meon, and Kiriathaim,

25:10  LA: filiis orientis cum filiis Ammon, et dabo eam in hæreditatem, ut non sit ultra memoria filiorum Ammon in gentibus.
       EN: to the children of the east, together with the children of Ammon. I will give it as an inheritance, so that there may no longer be any memory of the children of Ammon among the nations.

25:11  LA: Et in Moab faciam judicia, et scient quia ego Dominus.]
       EN: I will execute judgments upon Moab, and they will know that I am the Lord.

25:12  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pro eo quod fecit Idumæa ultionem ut se vindicaret de filiis Juda, peccavitque delinquens, et vindictam expetivit de eis :
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: Because Edom acted in vengeance to avenge itself upon the children of Judah, sinned grievously, and sought revenge upon them,

25:13  LA: idcirco hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Extendam manum meam super Idumæam, et auferam de ea hominem et jumentum, et faciam eam desertam ab austro : et qui sunt in Dedan, gladio cadent.
       EN: therefore thus says the Lord God: I will stretch out My hand against Edom and remove from it human and beast; I will make it a wilderness from the south, and those in Dedan will fall by the sword.

25:14  LA: Et dabo ultionem meam super Idumæam per manum populi mei Israël : et facient in Edom juxta iram meam et furorem meum, et scient vindictam meam, dicit Dominus Deus.]
       EN: I will lay My vengeance upon Edom through the hand of My people Israel. They will act in Edom according to My anger and My fury, and they will know My vengeance, says the Lord God.

25:15  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pro eo quod fecerunt Palæstini vindictam, et ulti se sunt toto animo, interficientes, et implentes inimicitias veteres,
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: Because the Philistines acted in revenge and avenged themselves with all their heart, killing and fulfilling ancient enmities,

25:16  LA: propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego extendam manum meam super Palæstinos, et interficiam interfectores, et perdam reliquias maritimæ regionis,
       EN: therefore thus says the Lord God: See, I will stretch out My hand against the Philistines, kill the killers, and destroy the remnant of the coastal region.

25:17  LA: faciamque in eis ultiones magnas, arguens in furore : et scient quia ego Dominus, cum dedero vindictam meam super eos.]
       EN: I will execute great acts of vengeance upon them, reproving them in fury, and they will know that I am the Lord when I lay My vengeance upon them.”

EZEKIEL 26

26:1  LA: Et factum est in undecimo anno, prima mensis : factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: In the eleventh year, on the first day of the month, the word of the Lord came to me, saying:

26:2  LA: [Fili hominis, pro eo quod dixit Tyrus de Jerusalem : Euge, confractæ sunt portæ populorum, conversa est ad me : implebor ; deserta est :
      EN: “Son of man, because Tyre said concerning Jerusalem, ‘Aha! The gates of the peoples are broken; she has turned toward me. I will be filled; she is deserted,’

26:3  LA: propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego super te, Tyre, et ascendere faciam ad te gentes multas, sicut ascendit mare fluctuans.
      EN: therefore thus says the Lord God: See, I am against you, Tyre; I will bring many nations up against you, as the sea rises in waves.

26:4  LA: Et dissipabunt muros Tyri, et destruent turres ejus : et radam pulverem ejus de ea, et dabo eam in limpidissimam petram.
      EN: They will tear down Tyre's walls and destroy her towers. I will scrape her dust from her and make her a perfectly bare rock.

26:5  LA: Siccatio sagenarum erit in medio maris, quia ego locutus sum, ait Dominus Deus : et erit in direptionem gentibus.
      EN: She will be a drying place for nets in the midst of the sea, for I have spoken, says the Lord God, and she will become plunder for the nations.

26:6  LA: Filiæ quoque ejus quæ sunt in agro, gladio interficientur : et scient quia ego Dominus.]
      EN: Her daughters in the countryside will also be killed by the sword, and they will know that I am the Lord.

26:7  LA: [Quia hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego adducam ad Tyrum Nabuchodonosor regem Babylonis ab aquilone, regem regum, cum equis, et curribus, et equitibus, et cœtu, populoque magno.
      EN: For thus says the Lord God: See, I will bring Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon against Tyre from the north, a king of kings, with horses, chariots, horsemen, an assembly, and a great people.

26:8  LA: Filias tuas quæ sunt in agro, gladio interficiet, et circumdabit te munitionibus, et comportabit aggerem in gyro, et elevabit contra te clypeum :
      EN: He will kill your daughters in the countryside by the sword, surround you with siegeworks, heap up a ramp around you, and raise a shield against you.

26:9  LA: et vineas et arietes temperabit in muros tuos, et turres tuas destruet in armatura sua.
      EN: He will arrange siege shelters and battering rams against your walls and destroy your towers with his weaponry.

26:10  LA: Inundatione equorum ejus operiet te pulvis eorum : a sonitu equitum, et rotarum, et curruum, movebuntur muri tui, cum ingressus fuerit portas tuas quasi per introitum urbis dissipatæ.
       EN: From the flood of his horses their dust will cover you. At the sound of horsemen, wheels, and chariots your walls will shake when he enters your gates as one enters a shattered city.

26:11  LA: Ungulis equorum suorum conculcabit omnes plateas tuas : populum tuum gladio cædet, et statuæ tuæ nobiles in terram corruent.
       EN: With the hoofs of his horses he will trample all your streets; he will cut down your people with the sword, and your splendid statues will fall to the ground.

26:12  LA: Vastabunt opes tuas, diripient negotiationes tuas, et destruent muros tuos, et domos tuas præclaras subvertent : et lapides tuos, et ligna tua, et pulverem tuum in medio aquarum ponent.
       EN: They will devastate your riches, plunder your trade, destroy your walls, and overthrow your splendid houses. They will place your stones, timber, and dust in the midst of the waters.

26:13  LA: Et quiescere faciam multitudinem canticorum tuorum : et sonitus cithararum tuarum non audietur amplius.
       EN: I will silence the multitude of your songs, and the sound of your lyres will be heard no more.

26:14  LA: Et dabo te in limpidissimam petram, siccatio sagenarum eris, nec ædificaberis ultra, quia ego locutus sum, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: I will make you a perfectly bare rock; you will be a drying place for nets and will be rebuilt no more, for I have spoken, says the Lord God.

26:15  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Tyro : Numquid non a sonitu ruinæ tuæ, et gemitu interfectorum tuorum, cum occisi fuerint in medio tui, commovebuntur insulæ ?
       EN: Thus says the Lord God to Tyre: Will the islands not shake at the sound of your fall and the groaning of your slain, when they are killed in your midst?

26:16  LA: Et descendent de sedibus suis omnes principes maris, et auferent exuvias suas, et vestimenta sua varia abjicient, et induentur stupore : in terra sedebunt, et attoniti super repentino casu tuo admirabuntur :
       EN: All the princes of the sea will descend from their thrones, remove their robes, and cast off their many-colored garments; they will clothe themselves in astonishment. They will sit on the ground, stunned and amazed at your sudden fall.

26:17  LA: et assumentes super te lamentum, dicent tibi : Quomodo peristi, quæ habitas in mari, urbs inclyta, quæ fuisti fortis in mari cum habitatoribus tuis, quos formidabant universi ?
       EN: They will raise a lament over you and say to you, ‘How you have perished, you who dwell in the sea, renowned city, who were mighty on the sea with your inhabitants, whom everyone feared!

26:18  LA: Nunc stupebunt naves in die pavoris tui, et turbabuntur insulæ in mari, eo quod nullus egrediatur ex te.
       EN: Now the ships will be stunned on the day of your terror, and the islands in the sea will be troubled because no one goes out from you.’

26:19  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Cum dedero te urbem desolatam, sicut civitates quæ non habitantur ; et adduxero super te abyssum, et operuerint te aquæ multæ ;
       EN: For thus says the Lord God: When I make you a desolate city, like cities that are uninhabited, and bring the abyss upon you, and many waters cover you,

26:20  LA: et detraxero te cum his qui descendunt in lacum ad populum sempiternum ; et collocavero te in terra novissima sicut solitudines veteres, cum his qui deducuntur in lacum, ut non habiteris ; porro cum dedero gloriam in terra viventium :
       EN: when I bring you down with those descending into the pit, to the everlasting people, and settle you in the lowest land, like ancient wastelands, with those brought down to the pit, so that you may not be inhabited—yet when I set glory in the land of the living—

26:21  LA: in nihilum redigam te, et non eris : et requisita non invenieris ultra in sempiternum, dicit Dominus Deus.]
       EN: I will reduce you to nothing, and you will be no more. Though sought, you will never again be found, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 27

27:1  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

27:2  LA: Tu ergo, fili hominis, assume super Tyrum lamentum :
      EN: “You, then, son of man, raise a lament over Tyre.

27:3  LA: et dices Tyro, quæ habitat in introitu maris, negotiationi populorum ad insulas multas : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : O Tyre, tu dixisti : Perfecti decoris ego sum,
      EN: Say to Tyre, who dwells at the entrance of the sea, the trading center of peoples for many islands: Thus says the Lord God: O Tyre, you said, ‘I am perfect in beauty

27:4  LA: et in corde maris sita. Finitimi tui qui te ædificaverunt, impleverunt decorem tuum :
      EN: and situated in the heart of the sea.’ Your neighbors who built you brought your beauty to completion.

27:5  LA: abietibus de Sanir exstruxerunt te cum omnibus tabulatis maris : cedrum de Libano tulerunt ut facerent tibi malum.
      EN: With firs from Senir they built you, with all your seagoing planks; they took a cedar from Lebanon to make you a mast.

27:6  LA: Quercus de Basan dolaverunt in remos tuos, et transtra tua fecerunt tibi ex ebore indico, et prætoriola de insulis Italiæ.
      EN: They hewed oaks from Bashan for your oars and made your benches from Indian ivory, and your cabins from the islands of Italy.

27:7  LA: Byssus varia de Ægypto texta est tibi in velum ut poneretur in malo : hyacinthus et purpura de insulis Elisa facta sunt operimentum tuum.
      EN: Many-colored fine linen from Egypt was woven for your sail, to be placed on the mast; blue and purple from the islands of Elishah formed your covering.

27:8  LA: Habitatores Sidonis et Aradii fuerunt remiges tui : sapientes tui, Tyre, facti sunt gubernatores tui.
      EN: The inhabitants of Sidon and Arvad were your rowers; your wise men, Tyre, became your helmsmen.

27:9  LA: Senes Giblii et prudentes ejus habuerunt nautas ad ministerium variæ supellectilis tuæ : omnes naves maris, et nautæ earum, fuerunt in populo negotiationis tuæ.
      EN: The elders of Gebal and its skilled men had sailors to care for your varied equipment. All the ships of the sea and their sailors were among your trading people.

27:10  LA: Persæ, et Lydii, et Libyes erant in exercitu tuo viri bellatores tui : clypeum et galeam suspenderunt in te pro ornatu tuo.
       EN: Persians, Lydians, and Libyans were in your army, your warriors. They hung shield and helmet in you for your adornment.

27:11  LA: Filii Aradii cum exercitu tuo erant super muros tuos in circuitu : sed et Pigmæi qui erant in turribus tuis, pharetras suas suspenderunt in muris tuis per gyrum : ipsi compleverunt pulchritudinem tuam.
       EN: The children of Arvad were with your army upon your walls all around; the Pygmies in your towers also hung their quivers around your walls. They completed your beauty.

27:12  LA: Carthaginenses negotiatores tui, a multitudine cunctarum divitiarum, argento, ferro, stanno, plumboque repleverunt nundinas tuas.
       EN: The Carthaginians were your merchants; from their abundant riches of every kind they filled your markets with silver, iron, tin, and lead.

27:13  LA: Græcia, Thubal, et Mosoch, ipsi institores tui : mancipia, et vasa ærea advexerunt populo tuo.
       EN: Greece, Tubal, and Meshech were your dealers; they brought slaves and bronze vessels to your people.

27:14  LA: De domo Thogorma, equos, et equites, et mulos adduxerunt ad forum tuum.
       EN: From the house of Togarmah they brought horses, horsemen, and mules to your marketplace.

27:15  LA: Filii Dedan negotiatores tui ; insulæ multæ, negotiatio manus tuæ : dentes eburneos et hebeninos commutaverunt in pretio tuo.
       EN: The children of Dedan were your merchants; many islands traded through your hand. They exchanged ivory tusks and ebony as payment to you.

27:16  LA: Syrus negotiator tuus propter multitudinem operum tuorum : gemmam, et purpuram, et scutulata, et byssum, et sericum, et chodchod proposuerunt in mercatu tuo.
       EN: Syria was your merchant because of the abundance of your wares; it offered gems, purple, patterned fabrics, fine linen, silk, and chodchod in your market.

27:17  LA: Juda et terra Israël, ipsi institores tui in frumento primo : balsamum, et mel, et oleum, et resinam proposuerunt in nundinis tuis.
       EN: Judah and the land of Israel were your dealers in the finest wheat; they offered balsam, honey, oil, and resin in your markets.

27:18  LA: Damascenus negotiator tuus in multitudine operum tuorum, in multitudine diversarum opum, in vino pingui, in lanis coloris optimi.
       EN: Damascus was your merchant because of your abundant wares and your many kinds of wealth, with rich wine and wool of the finest color.

27:19  LA: Dan, et Græcia, et Mosel, in nundinis tuis proposuerunt ferrum fabrefactum : stacte et calamus in negotiatione tua.
       EN: Dan, Greece, and Mosel offered wrought iron in your markets; stacte and aromatic cane were among your merchandise.

27:20  LA: Dedan institores tui in tapetibus ad sedendum.
       EN: Dedan dealt with you in carpets for sitting.

27:21  LA: Arabia et universi principes Cedar, ipsi negotiatores manus tuæ : cum agnis, et arietibus, et hædis, venerunt ad te negotiatores tui.
       EN: Arabia and all the princes of Kedar traded through your hand; your merchants came to you with lambs, rams, and young goats.

27:22  LA: Venditores Saba et Reema, ipsi negotiatores tui : cum universis primis aromatibus, et lapide pretioso, et auro, quod proposuerunt in mercatu tuo.
       EN: The traders of Sheba and Raamah were your merchants, with all the finest spices, precious stones, and gold, which they offered in your market.

27:23  LA: Haran, et Chene, et Eden, negotiatores tui ; Saba, Assur, et Chelmad venditores tui.
       EN: Haran, Canneh, and Eden were your merchants; Sheba, Assyria, and Chilmad were your traders.

27:24  LA: Ipsi negotiatores tui multifariam, involucris hyacinthi, et polymitorum, gazarumque pretiosarum, quæ obvolutæ et astrictæ erant funibus : cedros quoque habebant in negotiationibus tuis.
       EN: They traded with you in many things: bundles of blue cloth and embroidered fabrics and precious treasures, wrapped and bound with cords. They also had cedars among your merchandise.

27:25  LA: Naves maris, principes tui in negotiatione tua : et repleta es, et glorificata nimis in corde maris.]
       EN: The ships of the sea were your leaders in your trade; you were filled and greatly glorified in the heart of the sea.

27:26  LA: [In aquis multis adduxerunt te remiges tui : ventus auster contrivit te in corde maris.
       EN: Your rowers brought you into great waters; the south wind shattered you in the heart of the sea.

27:27  LA: Divitiæ tuæ, et thesauri tui, et multiplex instrumentum tuum : nautæ tui et gubernatores tui, qui tenebant supellectilem tuam, et populo tuo præerant : viri quoque bellatores tui, qui erant in te, cum universa multitudine tua quæ est in medio tui, cadent in corde maris in die ruinæ tuæ :
       EN: Your riches, your treasures, and your varied equipment; your sailors and helmsmen who managed your furnishings and presided over your people; your warriors who were within you, together with your whole multitude in your midst—all will fall into the heart of the sea on the day of your ruin.

27:28  LA: a sonitu clamoris gubernatorum tuorum conturbabuntur classes.
       EN: At the sound of your helmsmen's cry the fleets will be shaken.

27:29  LA: Et descendent de navibus suis omnes qui tenebant remum : nautæ et universi gubernatores maris in terra stabunt.
       EN: All who held an oar will descend from their ships; sailors and all the sea's helmsmen will stand upon the land.

27:30  LA: Et ejulabunt super te voce magna : et clamabunt amare, et superjacient pulverem capitibus suis, et cinere conspergentur.
       EN: They will wail loudly over you and cry bitterly, throw dust upon their heads, and sprinkle themselves with ashes.

27:32  LA: Et assument super te carmen lugubre, et plangent te : Quæ est ut Tyrus, quæ obmutuit in medio maris ?
       EN: They will raise a mournful song over you and lament you: ‘Who is like Tyre, silenced in the midst of the sea?

27:33  LA: Quæ in exitu negotiationum tuarum de mari implesti populos multos : in multitudine divitiarum tuarum, et populorum tuorum, ditasti reges terræ.
       EN: When your merchandise went out from the sea, you filled many peoples; through the abundance of your riches and your peoples you enriched the kings of the earth.

27:34  LA: Nunc contrita es a mari : in profundis aquarum opes tuæ, et omnis multitudo tua quæ erat in medio tui, ceciderunt.
       EN: Now you have been shattered by the sea; your riches and your whole multitude within you have fallen into the depths of the waters.’

27:35  LA: Universi habitatores insularum obstupuerunt super te, et reges earum omnes tempestate perculsi mutaverunt vultus.
       EN: All inhabitants of the islands have been stunned at you, and all their kings, struck by the storm, have changed their expression.

27:36  LA: Negotiatores populorum sibilaverunt super te : ad nihilum deducta es, et non eris usque in perpetuum.]
       EN: The merchants of the peoples have hissed at you. You have been brought to nothing, and you will be no more forever.”

EZEKIEL 28

28:1  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

28:2  LA: Fili hominis, dic principi Tyri : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Eo quod elevatum est cor tuum, et dixisti : Deus ego sum, et in cathedra Dei sedi in corde maris, cum sis homo, et non deus : et dedisti cor tuum quasi cor Dei :
      EN: “Son of man, say to the prince of Tyre: Thus says the Lord God: Because your heart has been lifted up and you said, ‘I am God, and I sit on God's throne in the heart of the sea,’ though you are a man and not a god, and you have made your heart like God's heart—

28:3  LA: ecce sapientior es tu Daniele : omne secretum non est absconditum a te :
      EN: see, you are wiser than Daniel; no secret is hidden from you!

28:4  LA: in sapientia et prudentia tua fecisti tibi fortitudinem, et acquisisti aurum et argentum in thesauris tuis :
      EN: By your wisdom and understanding you have gained power for yourself and acquired gold and silver in your treasuries.

28:5  LA: in multitudine sapientiæ tuæ, et in negotiatione tua multiplicasti tibi fortitudinem, et elevatum est cor tuum in robore tuo :
      EN: By your great wisdom and your trade you have multiplied your power, and your heart has been lifted up in your strength.

28:6  LA: propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Eo quod elevatum est cor tuum quasi cor dei,
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Because your heart has been lifted up like the heart of a god,

28:7  LA: idcirco ecce ego adducam super te alienos, robustissimos gentium : et nudabunt gladios suos super pulchritudinem sapientiæ tuæ, et polluent decorem tuum.
      EN: therefore, see, I will bring foreigners against you, the strongest of the nations. They will bare their swords against the beauty of your wisdom and defile your splendor.

28:8  LA: Interficient, et detrahent te : et morieris in interitu occisorum in corde maris.
      EN: They will kill you and bring you down, and you will die the death of the slain in the heart of the sea.

28:9  LA: Numquid dicens loqueris : Deus ego sum, coram interficientibus te, cum sis homo, et non deus, in manu occidentium te ?
      EN: Will you still say, ‘I am God,’ before those killing you, though you are a man and not a god in the hand of those putting you to death?

28:10  LA: Morte incircumcisorum morieris in manu alienorum, quia ego locutus sum, ait Dominus Deus.]
       EN: You will die the death of the uncircumcised at the hand of foreigners, for I have spoken, says the Lord God.”

28:11  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens : Fili hominis, leva planctum super regem Tyri,
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying, “Son of man, raise a lament over the king of Tyre,

28:12  LA: et dices ei : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Tu signaculum similitudinis, plenus sapientia, et perfectus decore.
       EN: and say to him: Thus says the Lord God: You were the seal of likeness, full of wisdom and perfect in beauty.

28:13  LA: In deliciis paradisi Dei fuisti : omnis lapis pretiosus operimentum tuum, sardius, topazius, et jaspis, chrysolithus, et onyx, et beryllus, sapphirus, et carbunculus, et smaragdus : aurum, opus decoris tui : et foramina tua, in die qua conditus es, præparata sunt.
       EN: You were amid the delights of God's paradise; every precious stone was your covering: sard, topaz, jasper, chrysolite, onyx, beryl, sapphire, carbuncle, and emerald. Gold was the workmanship of your adornment, and your settings were prepared on the day you were created.

28:14  LA: Tu cherub extentus, et protegens, et posui te in monte sancto Dei : in medio lapidum ignitorum ambulasti,
       EN: You were a cherub, outstretched and sheltering, and I placed you on God's holy mountain. You walked amid fiery stones,

28:15  LA: perfectus in viis tuis a die conditionis tuæ, donec inventa est iniquitas in te.
       EN: perfect in your ways from the day of your creation until iniquity was found in you.

28:16  LA: In multitudine negotiationis tuæ repleta sunt interiora tua iniquitate, et peccasti : et ejeci te de monte Dei, et perdidi te, o cherub protegens, de medio lapidum ignitorum.
       EN: Through your abundant trade your inner parts were filled with iniquity, and you sinned. I cast you from God's mountain and destroyed you, O sheltering cherub, from amid the fiery stones.

28:17  LA: Et elevatum est cor tuum in decore tuo ; perdidisti sapientiam tuam in decore tuo : in terram projeci te ; ante faciem regum dedi te ut cernerent te.
       EN: Your heart was lifted up in your beauty; you ruined your wisdom in your splendor. I cast you to the ground and set you before kings, for them to behold you.

28:18  LA: In multitudine iniquitatum tuarum, et iniquitate negotiationis tuæ, polluisti sanctificationem tuam : producam ergo ignem de medio tui, qui comedat te, et dabo te in cinerem super terram, in conspectu omnium videntium te.
       EN: Through your many iniquities and the injustice of your trade you defiled your sanctuary. Therefore I will bring fire out from your midst to consume you, and I will turn you into ashes upon the earth before the eyes of all who see you.

28:19  LA: Omnes qui viderint te in gentibus, obstupescent super te : nihili factus es, et non eris in perpetuum.]
       EN: All who see you among the nations will be stunned at you. You have become nothing, and you will be no more forever.”

28:20  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

28:21  LA: Fili hominis, pone faciem tuam contra Sidonem, et prophetabis de ea :
       EN: “Son of man, set your face against Sidon and prophesy concerning her.

28:22  LA: et dices : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego ad te, Sidon, et glorificabor in medio tui : et scient quia ego Dominus, cum fecero in ea judicia, et sanctificatus fuero in ea.
       EN: Say: Thus says the Lord God: See, I am against you, Sidon, and I will be glorified in your midst. They will know that I am the Lord when I execute judgments upon her and manifest My holiness in her.

28:23  LA: Et immittam ei pestilentiam, et sanguinem in plateis ejus : et corruent interfecti in medio ejus gladio per circuitum, et scient quia ego Dominus.
       EN: I will send pestilence upon her and blood in her streets; the slain will fall in her midst by the sword all around, and they will know that I am the Lord.

28:24  LA: Et non erit ultra domui Israël offendiculum amaritudinis, et spina dolorem inferens undique per circuitum eorum qui adversantur eis : et scient quia ego Dominus Deus.
       EN: There will no longer be for the house of Israel a bitter stumbling block or a thorn bringing pain from all those around them who oppose them, and they will know that I am the Lord God.

28:25  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Quando congregavero domum Israël de populis in quibus dispersi sunt, sanctificabor in eis coram gentibus : et habitabunt in terra sua, quam dedi servo meo Jacob :
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: When I gather the house of Israel from the peoples among whom they were scattered, I will manifest My holiness in them before the nations. They will dwell in their own land, which I gave to My servant Jacob.

28:26  LA: et habitabunt in ea securi, et ædificabunt domos, et plantabunt vineas, et habitabunt confidenter, cum fecero judicia in omnibus qui adversantur eis per circuitum : et scient quia ego Dominus Deus eorum.]
       EN: They will dwell in it securely, build houses, and plant vineyards. They will dwell confidently when I execute judgments upon all those around them who oppose them, and they will know that I am the Lord their God.”

EZEKIEL 29

29:1  LA: In anno decimo, decimo mense, undecima die mensis, factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: In the tenth year, in the tenth month, on the eleventh day of the month, the word of the Lord came to me, saying:

29:2  LA: Fili hominis, pone faciem tuam contra Pharaonem regem Ægypti, et prophetabis de eo, et de Ægypto universa.
      EN: “Son of man, set your face against Pharaoh king of Egypt and prophesy concerning him and all Egypt.

29:3  LA: Loquere, et dices : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego ad te, Pharao rex Ægypti, draco magne, qui cubas in medio fluminum tuorum, et dicis : Meus est fluvius, et ego feci memetipsum.
      EN: Speak and say: Thus says the Lord God: See, I am against you, Pharaoh king of Egypt, great dragon lying amid your rivers, who say, ‘The river is mine, and I made myself.’

29:4  LA: Et ponam frenum in maxillis tuis, et agglutinabo pisces fluminum tuorum squamis tuis, et extraham te de medio fluminum tuorum, et universi pisces tui squamis tuis adhærebunt.
      EN: I will put a bridle in your jaws and make the fish of your rivers cling to your scales. I will draw you out from your rivers, and all your fish will cling to your scales.

29:5  LA: Et projiciam te in desertum, et omnes pisces fluminis tui : super faciem terræ cades ; non colligeris, neque congregaberis : bestiis terræ et volatilibus cæli dedi te ad devorandum.
      EN: I will cast you into the wilderness, you and all the fish of your river. You will fall upon the face of the earth; you will not be gathered or collected. I have given you to the beasts of the earth and the birds of the sky to devour.

29:6  LA: Et scient omnes habitatores Ægypti quia ego Dominus, pro eo quod fuisti baculus arundineus domui Israël :
      EN: All inhabitants of Egypt will know that I am the Lord, because you were a reed staff to the house of Israel.

29:7  LA: quando apprehenderunt te manu, et confractus es, et lacerasti omnem humerum eorum : et innitentibus eis super te comminutus es, et dissolvisti omnes renes eorum.
      EN: When they grasped you with their hand, you broke and tore their whole shoulder; when they leaned on you, you shattered and loosened all their loins.

29:8  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego adducam super te gladium, et interficiam de te hominem et jumentum.
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: See, I will bring a sword against you and cut off from you human and beast.

29:9  LA: Et erit terra Ægypti in desertum et in solitudinem : et scient quia ego Dominus, pro eo quod dixeris : Fluvius meus est, et ego feci eum.
      EN: The land of Egypt will become a deserted wilderness, and they will know that I am the Lord, because you said, ‘The river is mine, and I made it.’

29:10  LA: Idcirco ecce ego ad te, et ad flumina tua : daboque terram Ægypti in solitudines, gladio dissipatam, a turre Syenes usque ad terminos Æthiopiæ.
       EN: Therefore, see, I am against you and your rivers; I will make the land of Egypt a wasteland, devastated by the sword, from the tower of Syene to the borders of Ethiopia.

29:11  LA: Non pertransibit eam pes hominis, neque pes jumenti gradietur in ea, et non habitabitur quadraginta annis.
       EN: No human foot will pass through it, nor will the foot of a beast walk in it, and it will be uninhabited for forty years.

29:12  LA: Daboque terram Ægypti desertam in medio terrarum desertarum, et civitates ejus in medio urbium subversarum, et erunt desolatæ quadraginta annis : et dispergam Ægyptios in nationes, et ventilabo eos in terras.
       EN: I will make the land of Egypt desolate among desolate lands, and its cities among ruined cities; they will be desolated for forty years. I will scatter the Egyptians among the nations and disperse them through the lands.

29:13  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Post finem quadraginta annorum congregabo Ægyptum de populis in quibus dispersi fuerant.
       EN: For thus says the Lord God: At the end of forty years I will gather Egypt from the peoples among whom they were scattered.

29:14  LA: Et reducam captivitatem Ægypti, et collocabo eos in terra Phathures, in terra nativitatis suæ, et erunt ibi in regnum humile.
       EN: I will bring back Egypt's captives and settle them in the land of Pathros, the land of their birth, and there they will be a lowly kingdom.

29:15  LA: Inter cetera regna erit humillima, et non elevabitur ultra super nationes, et imminuam eos ne imperent gentibus.
       EN: It will be the lowliest among the other kingdoms and will no longer exalt itself over the nations. I will diminish them so that they do not rule over the nations.

29:16  LA: Neque erunt ultra domui Israël in confidentia, docentes iniquitatem ut fugiant, et sequantur eos : et scient quia ego Dominus Deus.]
       EN: They will no longer be a source of confidence for the house of Israel, teaching iniquity so that they flee and follow them. They will know that I am the Lord God.”

29:17  LA: Et factum est in vigesimo et septimo anno, in primo, in una mensis : factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: In the twenty-seventh year, in the first month, on the first day of the month, the word of the Lord came to me, saying:

29:18  LA: [Fili hominis, Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis servire fecit exercitum suum servitute magna adversum Tyrum : omne caput decalvatum, et omnis humerus depilatus est : et merces non est reddita ei, neque exercitui ejus, de Tyro, pro servitute qua servivit mihi adversus eam.
       EN: “Son of man, Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon made his army perform hard service against Tyre. Every head was made bald and every shoulder rubbed bare, yet neither he nor his army received wages from Tyre for the service he performed for Me against her.

29:19  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego dabo Nabuchodonosor regem Babylonis in terra Ægypti : et accipiet multitudinem ejus, et deprædabitur manubias ejus, et diripiet spolia ejus : et erit merces exercitui illius,
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: See, I will place Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon in the land of Egypt. He will take its multitude, seize its booty, and plunder its spoil, and it will be wages for his army

29:20  LA: et operi quo servivit adversus eam : dedi ei terram Ægypti pro eo quod laboraverit mihi, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: and for the work by which he served against her. I have given him the land of Egypt because he labored for Me, says the Lord God.

29:21  LA: In die illo pullulabit cornu domui Israël, et tibi dabo apertum os in medio eorum, et scient quia ego Dominus.]
       EN: On that day a horn will sprout for the house of Israel, and I will give you an open mouth among them, and they will know that I am the Lord.”

EZEKIEL 30

30:1  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

30:2  LA: Fili hominis, propheta, et dic : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ululate : Væ, væ diei !
      EN: “Son of man, prophesy and say: Thus says the Lord God: Howl, ‘Alas, alas for the day!’

30:3  LA: quia juxta est dies, et appropinquat dies Domini, dies nubis : tempus gentium erit.
      EN: For the day is near, and the day of the Lord approaches, a day of cloud; it will be the time of the nations.

30:4  LA: Et veniet gladius in Ægyptum, et erit pavor in Æthiopia, cum ceciderint vulnerati in Ægypto, et ablata fuerit multitudo illius, et destructa fundamenta ejus.
      EN: The sword will come into Egypt, and there will be terror in Ethiopia when the wounded fall in Egypt, its multitude is taken away, and its foundations are destroyed.

30:5  LA: Æthiopia, et Libya, et Lydi, et omne reliquum vulgus, et Chub, et filii terræ fœderis, cum eis gladio cadent.
      EN: Ethiopia, Libya, the Lydians, all the remaining people, Chub, and the children of the land of the covenant will fall with them by the sword.

30:6  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Et corruent fulcientes Ægyptum, et destruetur superbia imperii ejus : a turre Syenes gladio cadent in ea, ait Dominus Deus exercituum.
      EN: Thus says the Lord God: Those supporting Egypt will fall, and the pride of its dominion will be destroyed. From the tower of Syene they will fall in it by the sword, says the Lord God of hosts.

30:7  LA: Et dissipabuntur in medio terrarum desolatarum, et urbes ejus in medio civitatum desertarum erunt :
      EN: They will be devastated among desolate lands, and its towns will be among deserted cities.

30:8  LA: et scient quia ego Dominus, cum dedero ignem in Ægypto, et attriti fuerint omnes auxiliatores ejus.
      EN: They will know that I am the Lord when I set fire in Egypt and all its helpers are crushed.

30:9  LA: In die illa egredientur nuntii a facie mea in trieribus ad conterendam Æthiopiæ confidentiam : et erit pavor in eis in die Ægypti, quia absque dubio veniet.
      EN: On that day messengers will go out from My presence in triremes to shatter Ethiopia's confidence. Terror will be among them on Egypt's day, for it will surely come.

30:10  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Cessare faciam multitudinem Ægypti in manu Nabuchodonosor regis Babylonis.
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: I will put an end to Egypt's multitude by the hand of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon.

30:11  LA: Ipse et populus ejus cum eo, fortissimi gentium, adducentur ad disperdendam terram : et evaginabunt gladios suos super Ægyptum, et implebunt terram interfectis.
       EN: He and his people with him, the strongest of the nations, will be brought to destroy the land. They will draw their swords against Egypt and fill the land with the slain.

30:12  LA: Et faciam alveos fluminum aridos, et tradam terram in manus pessimorum : et dissipabo terram et plenitudinem ejus manu alienorum : ego Dominus locutus sum.
       EN: I will make the river channels dry and hand the land over to the most wicked. I will devastate the land and all that fills it by the hand of foreigners. I, the Lord, have spoken.

30:13  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Et disperdam simulacra, et cessare faciam idola de Memphis : et dux de terra Ægypti non erit amplius, et dabo terrorem in terra Ægypti.
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: I will destroy the images and put an end to the idols of Memphis. There will no longer be a ruler from the land of Egypt, and I will put terror in the land of Egypt.

30:14  LA: Et disperdam terram Phathures, et dabo ignem in Taphnis, et faciam judicia in Alexandria.
       EN: I will devastate the land of Pathros, set fire in Tahpanhes, and execute judgments upon Alexandria.

30:15  LA: Et effundam indignationem meam super Pelusium, robur Ægypti, et interficiam multitudinem Alexandriæ.
       EN: I will pour out My indignation upon Pelusium, the stronghold of Egypt, and kill the multitude of Alexandria.

30:16  LA: Et dabo ignem in Ægypto : quasi parturiens dolebit Pelusium, et Alexandria erit dissipata, et in Memphis angustiæ quotidianæ.
       EN: I will set fire in Egypt; Pelusium will suffer like a woman giving birth, Alexandria will be devastated, and Memphis will be in daily distress.

30:17  LA: Juvenes Heliopoleos et Bubasti gladio cadent, et ipsæ captivæ ducentur.
       EN: The young men of Heliopolis and Bubastis will fall by the sword, and the cities themselves will be led captive.

30:18  LA: Et in Taphnis nigrescet dies, cum contrivero ibi sceptra Ægypti, et defecerit in ea superbia potentiæ ejus : ipsam nubes operiet, filiæ autem ejus in captivitatem ducentur.
       EN: In Tahpanhes the day will grow black when I break Egypt's scepters there and the pride of its power fails in it. A cloud will cover it, and its daughters will be led into captivity.

30:19  LA: Et judicia faciam in Ægypto : et scient quia ego Dominus.]
       EN: I will execute judgments upon Egypt, and they will know that I am the Lord.”

30:20  LA: Et factum est in undecimo anno, in primo mense, in septima mensis : factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: In the eleventh year, in the first month, on the seventh day of the month, the word of the Lord came to me, saying:

30:21  LA: [Fili hominis, brachium Pharaonis regis Ægypti confregi, et ecce non est obvolutum ut restitueretur ei sanitas, ut ligaretur pannis, et fasciaretur linteolis, ut recepto robore posset tenere gladium.
       EN: “Son of man, I have broken the arm of Pharaoh king of Egypt. See, it has not been wrapped so that health may be restored to it, bound with cloths and bandaged with linen strips, so that it might regain strength to hold a sword.

30:22  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego ad Pharaonem regem Ægypti, et comminuam brachium ejus forte, sed confractum : et dejiciam gladium de manu ejus,
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: See, I am against Pharaoh king of Egypt, and I will shatter his arm, strong yet broken, and knock the sword from his hand.

30:23  LA: et dispergam Ægyptum in gentibus, et ventilabo eos in terris.
       EN: I will scatter Egypt among the nations and disperse them through the lands.

30:24  LA: Et confortabo brachia regis Babylonis, daboque gladium meum in manu ejus, et confringam brachia Pharaonis, et gement gemitibus interfecti coram facie ejus.
       EN: I will strengthen the arms of the king of Babylon and put My sword in his hand. I will break Pharaoh's arms, and they will groan before him with the groans of the slain.

30:25  LA: Et confortabo brachia regis Babylonis, et brachia Pharaonis concident : et scient quia ego Dominus, cum dedero gladium meum in manu regis Babylonis, et extenderit eum super terram Ægypti.
       EN: I will strengthen the arms of the king of Babylon, and Pharaoh's arms will fall. They will know that I am the Lord when I put My sword in the hand of the king of Babylon and he stretches it over the land of Egypt.

30:26  LA: Et dispergam Ægyptum in nationes, et ventilabo eos in terras : et scient quia ego Dominus.]
       EN: I will scatter Egypt among the nations and disperse them through the lands, and they will know that I am the Lord.”

EZEKIEL 31

31:1  LA: Et factum est in anno undecimo, tertio mense, una mensis : factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: In the eleventh year, in the third month, on the first day of the month, the word of the Lord came to me, saying:

31:2  LA: Fili hominis, dic Pharaoni regi Ægypti et populo ejus : [Cui similis factus es in magnitudine tua ?
      EN: “Son of man, say to Pharaoh king of Egypt and to his people: Whom have you come to resemble in your greatness?

31:3  LA: ecce Assur quasi cedrus in Libano, pulcher ramis, et frondibus nemorosus, excelsusque altitudine, et inter condensas frondes elevatum est cacumen ejus.
      EN: See, Assyria was like a cedar in Lebanon, beautiful in branches, thick with foliage, and lofty in height; its top rose among the dense leaves.

31:4  LA: Aquæ nutrierunt illum ; abyssus exaltavit illum : flumina ejus manabant in circuitu radicum ejus, et rivos suos emisit ad universa ligna regionis.
      EN: Waters nourished it; the deep raised it high. Its rivers flowed around its roots, and it sent its streams to all the trees of the countryside.

31:5  LA: Propterea elevata est altitudo ejus super omnia ligna regionis, et multiplicata sunt arbusta ejus, et elevati sunt rami ejus præ aquis multis.
      EN: Therefore its height rose above all the trees of the countryside, its boughs multiplied, and its branches grew high because of the abundant waters.

31:6  LA: Cumque extendisset umbram suam, in ramis ejus fecerunt nidos omnia volatilia cæli : et sub frondibus ejus genuerunt omnes bestiæ saltuum, et sub umbraculo illius habitabat cœtus gentium plurimarum.
      EN: When it had spread its shade, all the birds of the sky nested in its branches; all the beasts of the forests gave birth beneath its foliage, and a gathering of very many nations dwelt beneath its shelter.

31:7  LA: Eratque pulcherrimus in magnitudine sua, et in dilatatione arbustorum suorum : erat enim radix illius juxta aquas multas.
      EN: It was most beautiful in its greatness and in the spreading of its boughs, for its root was beside abundant waters.

31:8  LA: Cedri non fuerunt altiores illo in paradiso Dei ; abietes non adæquaverunt summitatem ejus, et platani non fuerunt æquæ frondibus illius : omne lignum paradisi Dei non est assimilatum illi, et pulchritudini ejus.
      EN: The cedars in God's paradise were not taller than it; the fir trees did not equal its height, and the plane trees did not match its foliage. No tree in God's paradise was like it in its beauty.

31:9  LA: Quoniam speciosum feci eum, et multis condensisque frondibus, et æmulata sunt eum omnia ligna voluptatis quæ erant in paradiso Dei.]
      EN: For I made it lovely, with many dense leaves, and all the trees of delight in God's paradise envied it.

31:10  LA: [Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pro eo quod sublimatus est in altitudine, et dedit summitatem suam virentem atque condensam, et elevatum est cor ejus in altitudine sua :
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Because it rose high in stature, put forth its green and dense top, and lifted up its heart in its height,

31:11  LA: tradidi eum in manu fortissimi gentium : faciens faciet ei : juxta impietatem ejus ejeci eum.
       EN: I handed it over to the mightiest of the nations; he will surely deal with it. According to its ungodliness I cast it out.

31:12  LA: Et succident eum alieni, et crudelissimi nationum, et projicient eum super montes : et in cunctis convallibus corruent rami ejus, et confringentur arbusta ejus in universis rupibus terræ : et recedent de umbraculo ejus omnes populi terræ, et relinquent eum.
       EN: Foreigners, the cruelest of the nations, will cut it down and cast it upon the mountains. Its branches will fall in every valley, and its boughs will be broken on all the cliffs of the earth. All the peoples of the earth will withdraw from its shelter and abandon it.

31:13  LA: In ruina ejus habitaverunt omnia volatilia cæli, et in ramis ejus fuerunt universæ bestiæ regionis.
       EN: All the birds of the sky have dwelt upon its fallen trunk, and all the beasts of the countryside have been upon its branches.

31:14  LA: Quam ob rem non elevabuntur in altitudine sua omnia ligna aquarum, nec ponent sublimitatem suam inter nemorosa atque frondosa, nec stabunt in sublimitate sua omnia quæ irrigantur aquis : quia omnes traditi sunt in mortem ad terram ultimam, in medio filiorum hominum, ad eos qui descendunt in lacum.
       EN: Therefore no trees by the waters will exalt themselves in their height or set their lofty tops among the dense foliage; nothing watered by the streams will stand in its own loftiness. For they have all been handed over to death, to the lowest land, among the children of humankind, with those who descend into the pit.

31:15  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : In die quando descendit ad inferos, induxi luctum : operui eum abysso, et prohibui flumina ejus, et coërcui aquas multas : contristatus est super eum Libanus, et omnia ligna agri concussa sunt.
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: On the day when it descended to the underworld, I brought mourning. I covered it with the deep, restrained its rivers, and held back the abundant waters. Lebanon grieved over it, and all the trees of the field were shaken.

31:16  LA: A sonitu ruinæ ejus commovi gentes cum deducerem eum ad infernum cum his qui descendebant in lacum : et consolata sunt in terra infima omnia ligna voluptatis egregia atque præclara in Libano, universa quæ irrigabantur aquis.
       EN: At the sound of its fall I shook the nations when I brought it down to the underworld with those descending into the pit. In the lowest land all the trees of delight were comforted, the excellent and splendid trees of Lebanon, all that were watered by streams.

31:17  LA: Nam et ipsi cum eo descendent in infernum ad interfectos gladio : et brachium uniuscujusque sedebit sub umbraculo ejus in medio nationum.
       EN: For they too will descend with it to the underworld, to those slain by the sword, and each one's arm will rest beneath its shelter among the nations.

31:18  LA: Cui assimilatus es, o inclyte atque sublimis inter ligna voluptatis ? ecce deductus es cum lignis voluptatis ad terram ultimam : in medio incircumcisorum dormies, cum eis qui interfecti sunt gladio : ipse est Pharao, et omnis multitudo ejus, dicit Dominus Deus.]
       EN: Whom did you resemble, O renowned and lofty one among the trees of delight? See, you have been brought down with the trees of delight to the lowest land. Among the uncircumcised you will lie, with those slain by the sword. This is Pharaoh and all his multitude, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 32

32:1  LA: Et factum est, duodecimo anno, in mense duodecimo, in una mensis : factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: In the twelfth year, in the twelfth month, on the first day of the month, the word of the Lord came to me, saying:

32:2  LA: Fili hominis, assume lamentum super Pharaonem regem Ægypti, et dices ad eum : [Leoni gentium assimilatus es, et draconi qui est in mari : et ventilabas cornu in fluminibus tuis, et conturbabas aquas pedibus tuis, et conculcabas flumina earum.
      EN: “Son of man, raise a lament over Pharaoh king of Egypt and say to him: You have been likened to a lion of the nations and to a dragon in the sea. You tossed your horn in your rivers, muddied the waters with your feet, and trampled their streams.

32:3  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Expandam super te rete meum in multitudine populorum multorum, et extraham te in sagena mea.
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: I will spread My net over you amid a great multitude of peoples and draw you out in My dragnet.

32:4  LA: Et projiciam te in terram ; super faciem agri abjiciam te : et habitare faciam super te omnia volatilia cæli, et saturabo de te bestias universæ terræ.
      EN: I will cast you upon the land, throw you upon the open field, and make all the birds of the sky settle upon you. I will satisfy the beasts of the whole earth with you.

32:5  LA: Et dabo carnes tuas super montes, et implebo colles tuos sanie tua.
      EN: I will lay your flesh upon the mountains and fill your hills with your putrid fluid.

32:6  LA: Et irrigabo terram fœtore sanguinis tui super montes, et valles implebuntur ex te.
      EN: I will drench the land upon the mountains with your stinking blood, and the valleys will be filled with you.

32:7  LA: Et operiam, cum extinctus fueris, cælum, et nigrescere faciam stellas ejus : solem nube tegam, et luna non dabit lumen suum.
      EN: When you are extinguished I will cover the sky and darken its stars; I will cover the sun with a cloud, and the moon will not give its light.

32:8  LA: Omnia luminaria cæli mœrere faciam super te, et dabo tenebras super terram tuam, dicit Dominus Deus, cum ceciderint vulnerati tui in medio terræ, ait Dominus Deus.
      EN: I will make all the lights of heaven mourn over you and put darkness upon your land, says the Lord God, when your wounded fall in the midst of the land, says the Lord God.

32:9  LA: Et irritabo cor populorum multorum, cum induxero contritionem tuam in gentibus super terras quas nescis.
      EN: I will trouble the heart of many peoples when I bring your ruin among the nations, upon lands you do not know.

32:10  LA: Et stupescere faciam super te populos multos, et reges eorum horrore nimio formidabunt super te, cum volare cœperit gladius meus super facies eorum : et obstupescent repente singuli pro anima sua in die ruinæ tuæ.
       EN: I will stun many peoples over you, and their kings will shudder with great horror over you when My sword begins to fly before their faces. On the day of your ruin each will suddenly be stunned for his own life.

32:11  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Gladius regis Babylonis veniet tibi.
       EN: For thus says the Lord God: The sword of the king of Babylon will come upon you.

32:12  LA: In gladiis fortium dejiciam multitudinem tuam : inexpugnabiles omnes gentes hæ, et vastabunt superbiam Ægypti, et dissipabitur multitudo ejus.
       EN: By the swords of mighty men I will cast down your multitude. All these nations are invincible; they will devastate Egypt's pride, and its multitude will be scattered.

32:13  LA: Et perdam omnia jumenta ejus, quæ erant super aquas plurimas : et non conturbabit eas pes hominis ultra, neque ungula jumentorum turbabit eas.
