       EN: I will destroy all its beasts that were beside the abundant waters. No human foot will muddy them again, nor will the hoof of beasts trouble them.

32:14  LA: Tunc purissimas reddam aquas eorum, et flumina eorum quasi oleum adducam, ait Dominus Deus,
       EN: Then I will make their waters perfectly clear and cause their rivers to flow like oil, says the Lord God,

32:15  LA: cum dedero terram Ægypti desolatam : deseretur autem terra a plenitudine sua quando percussero omnes habitatores ejus : et scient quia ego Dominus.
       EN: when I make the land of Egypt desolate. The land will be emptied of all that fills it when I strike all its inhabitants, and they will know that I am the Lord.

32:16  LA: Planctus est, et plangent eum : filiæ gentium plangent eum : super Ægyptum et super multitudinem ejus plangent eum, ait Dominus Deus.]
       EN: This is a lament, and they will lament it; the daughters of the nations will lament it. Over Egypt and all its multitude they will lament it, says the Lord God.”

32:17  LA: Et factum est in duodecimo anno, in quintadecima mensis : factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: In the twelfth year, on the fifteenth day of the month, the word of the Lord came to me, saying:

32:18  LA: [Fili hominis, cane lugubre super multitudinem Ægypti : et detrahe eam ipsam, et filias gentium robustarum, ad terram ultimam, cum his qui descendunt in lacum.
       EN: “Son of man, sing a dirge over Egypt's multitude and bring her down, her and the daughters of mighty nations, to the lowest land, with those who descend into the pit.

32:20  LA: In medio interfectorum gladio cadent ; gladius datus est : attraxerunt eam et omnes populos ejus.
       EN: They will fall among those slain by the sword. The sword has been given; they have dragged her away with all her peoples.

32:21  LA: Loquentur ei potentissimi robustorum de medio inferni, qui cum auxiliatoribus ejus descenderunt, et dormierunt incircumcisi interfecti gladio.
       EN: The mightiest of the strong will speak to him from the midst of the underworld, those who descended with his helpers and lay uncircumcised, slain by the sword.

32:22  LA: Ibi Assur, et omnis multitudo ejus : in circuitu illius sepulchra ejus, omnes interfecti, et qui ceciderunt gladio.
       EN: There is Assyria and all his multitude, his graves all around him, all slain and fallen by the sword.

32:23  LA: Quorum data sunt sepulchra in novissimis laci, et facta est multitudo ejus per gyrum sepulchri ejus : universi interfecti, cadentesque gladio, qui dederant quondam formidinem in terra viventium.
       EN: Their graves are set in the deepest parts of the pit, and his multitude is all around his grave, all slain and fallen by the sword, who once spread terror in the land of the living.

32:24  LA: Ibi Ælam, et omnis multitudo ejus per gyrum sepulchri sui : omnes hi interfecti, ruentesque gladio, qui descenderunt incircumcisi ad terram ultimam ; qui posuerunt terrorem suum in terra viventium, et portaverunt ignominiam suam cum his qui descendunt in lacum.
       EN: There is Elam and all his multitude around his grave, all slain and fallen by the sword, who descended uncircumcised to the lowest land, who spread their terror in the land of the living and bore their disgrace with those who descend into the pit.

32:25  LA: In medio interfectorum posuerunt cubile ejus in universis populis ejus : in circuitu ejus sepulchrum illius : omnes hi incircumcisi, interfectique gladio. Dederunt enim terrorem suum in terra viventium, et portaverunt ignominiam suam cum his qui descendunt in lacum : in medio interfectorum positi sunt.
       EN: Among the slain they set his resting place amid all his peoples, his burial place all around him, all uncircumcised and slain by the sword. For they spread their terror in the land of the living and bore their disgrace with those who descend into the pit; they were placed among the slain.

32:26  LA: Ibi Mosoch et Thubal, et omnis multitudo ejus : in circuitu ejus sepulchra illius : omnes hi incircumcisi, interfectique et cadentes gladio, quia dederunt formidinem suam in terra viventium.
       EN: There are Meshech and Tubal and all his multitude, his graves all around him, all uncircumcised, slain and fallen by the sword, because they spread their terror in the land of the living.

32:27  LA: Et non dormient cum fortibus, cadentibusque, et incircumcisis, qui descenderunt ad infernum cum armis suis, et posuerunt gladios suos sub capitibus suis, et fuerunt iniquitates eorum in ossibus eorum : quia terror fortium facti sunt in terra viventium.
       EN: They will not lie with the mighty, fallen and uncircumcised, who descended to the underworld with their weapons and placed their swords beneath their heads, their iniquities upon their bones, because they became the terror of the mighty in the land of the living.

32:28  LA: Et tu ergo in medio incircumcisorum contereris, et dormies cum interfectis gladio.
       EN: You too, then, will be crushed among the uncircumcised and lie with those slain by the sword.

32:29  LA: Ibi Idumæa, et reges ejus, et omnes duces ejus, qui dati sunt cum exercitu suo cum interfectis gladio, et qui cum incircumcisis dormierunt, et cum his qui descendunt in lacum.
       EN: There is Edom, its kings and all its leaders, who with their army were placed with those slain by the sword and lay with the uncircumcised, with those who descend into the pit.

32:30  LA: Ibi principes aquilonis omnes, et universi venatores, qui deducti sunt cum interfectis, paventes, et in sua fortitudine confusi : qui dormierunt incircumcisi cum interfectis gladio, et portaverunt confusionem suam cum his qui descendunt in lacum.
       EN: There are all the princes of the north and all the hunters, who were brought down with the slain, trembling and ashamed in their strength. They lay uncircumcised with those slain by the sword and bore their shame with those who descend into the pit.

32:31  LA: Vidit eos Pharao, et consolatus est super universa multitudine sua, quæ interfecta est gladio : Pharao, et omnis exercitus ejus, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: Pharaoh saw them and was comforted concerning all his multitude slain by the sword—Pharaoh and all his army, says the Lord God.

32:32  LA: Quia dedi terrorem meum in terra viventium, et dormivit in medio incircumcisorum cum interfectis gladio : Pharao, et omnis multitudo ejus, ait Dominus Deus.]
       EN: For I have put My terror in the land of the living, and he has lain among the uncircumcised with those slain by the sword—Pharaoh and all his multitude, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 33

33:1  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

33:2  LA: Fili hominis, loquere ad filios populi tui, et dices ad eos : Terra, cum induxero super eam gladium, et tulerit populus terræ virum unum de novissimis suis, et constituerit eum super se speculatorem :
      EN: “Son of man, speak to the children of your people and say to them: When I bring the sword upon a land, and the people of the land take a man from among their least and appoint him as their watchman,

33:3  LA: et ille viderit gladium venientem super terram, et cecinerit buccina, et annuntiaverit populo :
      EN: and he sees the sword coming upon the land, sounds the horn, and warns the people,

33:4  LA: audiens autem quisquis ille est sonitum buccinæ, et non se observaverit, veneritque gladius, et tulerit eum : sanguis ipsius super caput ejus erit.
      EN: if anyone hears the sound of the horn but does not take care, and the sword comes and takes him away, his blood will be upon his own head.

33:5  LA: Sonum buccinæ audivit, et non se observavit : sanguis ejus in ipso erit. Si autem se custodierit, animam suam salvabit.
      EN: He heard the sound of the horn and did not take care; his blood will be upon himself. But if he takes care, he will save his life.

33:6  LA: Quod si speculator viderit gladium venientem, et non insonuerit buccina, et populus se non custodierit, veneritque gladius, et tulerit de eis animam : ille quidem in iniquitate sua captus est ; sanguinem autem ejus de manu speculatoris requiram.
      EN: If the watchman sees the sword coming and does not sound the horn, and the people do not take care, and the sword comes and takes a life from among them, that person is indeed taken in his iniquity, but I will require his blood from the watchman's hand.

33:7  LA: Et tu, fili hominis, speculatorem dedi te domui Israël : audiens ergo ex ore meo sermonem, annuntiabis eis ex me.
      EN: You, son of man, I have appointed as watchman for the house of Israel. Therefore, hearing a word from My mouth, you will warn them from Me.

33:8  LA: Si me dicente ad impium : Impie, morte morieris : non fueris locutus ut se custodiat impius a via sua, ipse impius in iniquitate sua morietur ; sanguinem autem ejus de manu tua requiram.
      EN: If, when I say to the wicked, ‘Wicked one, you will surely die,’ you do not speak so that the wicked may guard against his way, that wicked person will die in his iniquity, but I will require his blood from your hand.

33:9  LA: Si autem annuntiante te ad impium ut a viis suis convertatur, non fuerit conversus a via sua, ipse in iniquitate sua morietur, porro tu animam tuam liberasti.
      EN: But if you warn the wicked to turn from his ways and he does not turn from his way, he will die in his iniquity, while you have delivered your life.

33:10  LA: Tu ergo, fili hominis, dic ad domum Israël : Sic locuti estis, dicentes : Iniquitates nostræ et peccata nostra super nos sunt, et in ipsis nos tabescimus : quomodo ergo vivere poterimus ?
       EN: You, then, son of man, say to the house of Israel: Thus you have spoken, saying, ‘Our iniquities and our sins are upon us, and we waste away in them; how, then, can we live?’

33:11  LA: Dic ad eos : Vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, nolo mortem impii, sed ut convertatur impius a via sua, et vivat. Convertimini, convertimini a viis vestris pessimis, et quare moriemini, domus Israël ?
       EN: Say to them: As I live, says the Lord God, I do not desire the death of the wicked, but that the wicked turn from his way and live. Turn, turn from your most evil ways! Why will you die, house of Israel?

33:12  LA: Tu itaque, fili hominis, dic ad filios populi tui : Justitia justi non liberabit eum, in quacumque die peccaverit, et impietas impii non nocebit ei, in quacumque die conversus fuerit ab impietate sua : et justus non poterit vivere in justitia sua, in quacumque die peccaverit.
       EN: You, therefore, son of man, say to the children of your people: The righteousness of the righteous will not deliver him on any day when he sins; the wickedness of the wicked will not harm him on any day when he turns from his wickedness. The righteous cannot live by his righteousness on any day when he sins.

33:13  LA: Etiamsi dixero justo quod vita vivat, et confisus in justitia sua fecerit iniquitatem, omnes justitiæ ejus oblivioni tradentur, et in iniquitate sua quam operatus est, in ipsa morietur.
       EN: Even if I say to the righteous that he will surely live, but he trusts in his righteousness and commits iniquity, all his righteous deeds will be forgotten, and in the iniquity he has committed he will die.

33:14  LA: Si autem dixero impio : Morte morieris : et egerit pœnitentiam a peccato suo, feceritque judicium et justitiam,
       EN: But if I say to the wicked, ‘You will surely die,’ and he repents of his sin and does what is just and right,

33:15  LA: et pignus restituerit ille impius, rapinamque reddiderit, in mandatis vitæ ambulaverit, nec fecerit quidquam injustum : vita vivet, et non morietur.
       EN: if that wicked person restores the pledge, returns what he stole, walks in the commandments of life, and does nothing unjust, he will surely live; he will not die.

33:16  LA: Omnia peccata ejus quæ peccavit, non imputabuntur ei : judicium et justitiam fecit : vita vivet.
       EN: None of the sins he committed will be charged against him; he has done what is just and right; he will surely live.

33:17  LA: Et dixerunt filii populi tui : Non est æqui ponderis via Domini : et ipsorum via injusta est.
       EN: The children of your people have said, ‘The Lord's way is not evenly balanced,’ while their own way is unjust.

33:18  LA: Cum enim recesserit justus a justitia sua, feceritque iniquitates, morietur in eis.
       EN: For when the righteous withdraws from his righteousness and commits iniquities, he will die in them.

33:19  LA: Et cum recesserit impius ab impietate sua, feceritque judicium et justitiam, vivet in eis.
       EN: When the wicked withdraws from his wickedness and does what is just and right, he will live in them.

33:20  LA: Et dicitis : Non est recta via Domini. Unumquemque juxta vias suas judicabo de vobis, domus Israël.
       EN: Yet you say, ‘The Lord's way is not right.’ I will judge each of you according to his ways, house of Israel.”

33:21  LA: Et factum est in duodecimo anno, in decimo mense, in quinta mensis transmigrationis nostræ, venit ad me qui fugerat de Jerusalem, dicens : Vastata est civitas.
       EN: In the twelfth year of our exile, in the tenth month, on the fifth day of the month, one who had escaped from Jerusalem came to me, saying, “The city has been devastated.”

33:22  LA: Manus autem Domini facta fuerat ad me vespere, antequam veniret qui fugerat : aperuitque os meum donec veniret ad me mane : et aperto ore meo, non silui amplius.
       EN: The Lord’s hand had come upon me the evening before the man who escaped arrived. He opened my mouth, and by the time the man came to me in the morning, my mouth was open and I was silent no longer.

33:23  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

33:24  LA: Fili hominis, qui habitant in ruinosis his super humum Israël, loquentes aiunt : Unus erat Abraham, et hæreditate possedit terram : nos autem multi sumus : nobis data est terra in possessionem.
       EN: “Son of man, those dwelling in these ruins upon the soil of Israel say, ‘Abraham was one man, and he possessed the land as an inheritance. We, however, are many; the land has been given to us as a possession.’

33:25  LA: Idcirco dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Qui in sanguine comeditis, et oculos vestros levatis ad immunditias vestras, et sanguinem funditis, numquid terram hæreditate possidebitis ?
       EN: Therefore say to them: Thus says the Lord God: You who eat with blood, lift your eyes to your unclean things, and shed blood—will you possess the land as an inheritance?

33:27  LA: Hæc dices ad eos : Sic dicit Dominus Deus : Vivo ego, quia qui in ruinosis habitant, gladio cadent : et qui in agro est, bestiis tradetur ad devorandum : qui autem in præsidiis et speluncis sunt, peste morientur.
       EN: Say this to them: Thus says the Lord God: As I live, those dwelling in the ruins will fall by the sword; the one in the open country will be given to beasts to devour, and those in strongholds and caves will die of pestilence.

33:28  LA: Et dabo terram in solitudinem et in desertum, et deficiet superba fortitudo ejus : et desolabuntur montes Israël, eo quod nullus sit qui per eos transeat :
       EN: I will make the land a deserted wilderness, and its proud strength will fail. The mountains of Israel will be desolated, because no one passes through them.

33:29  LA: et scient quia ego Dominus, cum dedero terram eorum desolatam et desertam, propter universas abominationes suas quas operati sunt.
       EN: They will know that I am the Lord when I make their land a desolate wilderness because of all the abominations they have committed.

33:30  LA: Et tu, fili hominis, filii populi tui, qui loquuntur de te juxta muros et in ostiis domorum, et dicunt unus ad alterum, vir ad proximum suum, loquentes : Venite, et audiamus quis sit sermo egrediens a Domino.
       EN: As for you, son of man, the children of your people speak about you beside walls and in doorways, saying to one another, each to his neighbor, ‘Come, let us hear what word comes forth from the Lord.’

33:31  LA: Et veniunt ad te, quasi si ingrediatur populus, et sedent coram te populus meus : et audiunt sermones tuos, et non faciunt eos : quia in canticum oris sui vertunt illos, et avaritiam suam sequitur cor eorum.
       EN: They come to you as a people gathers, and My people sit before you. They hear your words but do not do them, for they turn them into a song in their mouth, while their heart follows their greed.

33:32  LA: Et es eis quasi carmen musicum, quod suavi dulcique sono canitur : et audiunt verba tua, et non faciunt ea.
       EN: You are to them like a musical song sung with a sweet and pleasing sound. They hear your words but do not do them.

33:33  LA: Et cum venerit quod prædictum est (ecce enim venit), tunc scient quod prophetes fuerit inter eos.
       EN: When what was foretold comes—and see, it is coming—then they will know that a prophet was among them.”

EZEKIEL 34

34:1  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

34:2  LA: Fili hominis, propheta de pastoribus Israël : propheta, et dices pastoribus : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Væ pastoribus Israël, qui pascebant semetipsos ! nonne greges a pastoribus pascuntur ?
      EN: “Son of man, prophesy concerning the shepherds of Israel. Prophesy and say to the shepherds: Thus says the Lord God: Woe to the shepherds of Israel who fed themselves! Should the flocks not be fed by the shepherds?

34:3  LA: Lac comedebatis, et lanis operiebamini, et quod crassum erat occidebatis : gregem autem meum non pascebatis.
      EN: You consumed the milk, clothed yourselves with the wool, and killed what was fat, but you did not feed My flock.

34:4  LA: Quod infirmum fuit non consolidastis, et quod ægrotum non sanastis : quod confractum est non alligastis, et quod abjectum est non reduxistis, et quod perierat non quæsistis : sed cum austeritate imperabatis eis, et cum potentia.
      EN: You did not strengthen the weak or heal the sick; you did not bind up the broken, bring back the outcast, or seek the lost. Instead you ruled over them with harshness and force.

34:5  LA: Et dispersæ sunt oves meæ, eo quod non esset pastor : et factæ sunt in devorationem omnium bestiarum agri, et dispersæ sunt.
      EN: My sheep were scattered because there was no shepherd; they became food for all the wild beasts and were scattered.

34:6  LA: Erraverunt greges mei in cunctis montibus, et in universo colle excelso : et super omnem faciem terræ dispersi sunt greges mei, et non erat qui requireret : non erat, inquam, qui requireret.
      EN: My flocks wandered on all the mountains and every high hill. My flocks were scattered over the whole face of the earth, and there was no one to search for them—no one, I say, to search for them.

34:7  LA: Propterea, pastores, audite verbum Domini.
      EN: Therefore, shepherds, hear the word of the Lord.

34:8  LA: Vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, quia pro eo quod facti sunt greges mei in rapinam, et oves meæ in devorationem omnium bestiarum agri, eo quod non esset pastor : neque enim quæsierunt pastores mei gregem meum, sed pascebant pastores semetipsos, et greges meos non pascebant :
      EN: As I live, says the Lord God, because My flocks became plunder and My sheep food for all the wild beasts, because there was no shepherd—for My shepherds did not seek My flock, but the shepherds fed themselves and did not feed My flocks—

34:9  LA: propterea, pastores, audite verbum Domini.
      EN: therefore, shepherds, hear the word of the Lord.

34:10  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego ipse super pastores : requiram gregem meum de manu eorum, et cessare faciam eos, ut ultra non pascant gregem, nec pascant amplius pastores semetipsos : et liberabo gregem meum de ore eorum, et non erit ultra eis in escam.]
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: See, I Myself am against the shepherds. I will require My flock from their hand and put an end to their tending the flock; the shepherds will no longer feed themselves. I will rescue My flock from their mouth, and it will no longer be food for them.

34:11  LA: [Quia hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego ipse requiram oves meas, et visitabo eas.
       EN: For thus says the Lord God: See, I Myself will seek My sheep and look after them.

34:12  LA: Sicut visitat pastor gregem suum, in die quando fuerit in medio ovium suarum dissipatarum, sic visitabo oves meas, et liberabo eas de omnibus locis in quibus dispersæ fuerant in die nubis et caliginis.
       EN: As a shepherd looks after his flock on the day when he is among his scattered sheep, so I will look after My sheep and rescue them from all the places where they were scattered on the day of cloud and gloom.

34:13  LA: Et educam eas de populis, et congregabo eas de terris, et inducam eas in terram suam, et pascam eas in montibus Israël, in rivis, et in cunctis sedibus terræ.
       EN: I will bring them out from the peoples, gather them from the lands, and lead them into their own land. I will pasture them on the mountains of Israel, beside the streams, and in all the settled places of the land.

34:14  LA: In pascuis uberrimis pascam eas, et in montibus excelsis Israël erunt pascua earum : ibi requiescent in herbis virentibus, et in pascuis pinguibus pascentur super montes Israël.
       EN: I will feed them in the richest pastures, and their grazing places will be on Israel's high mountains. There they will rest in green grass and graze in rich pastures upon the mountains of Israel.

34:15  LA: Ego pascam oves meas, et ego eas accubare faciam, dicit Dominus Deus.
       EN: I will pasture My sheep, and I will make them lie down, says the Lord God.

34:16  LA: Quod perierat requiram, et quod abjectum erat reducam, et quod confractum fuerat alligabo, et quod infirmum fuerat consolidabo, et quod pingue et forte custodiam : et pascam illas in judicio.
       EN: I will seek the lost, bring back the outcast, bind up the broken, strengthen the weak, and guard the fat and strong. I will pasture them with justice.

34:17  LA: Vos autem, greges mei, hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego judico inter pecus et pecus, arietum et hircorum.
       EN: As for you, My flocks, thus says the Lord God: See, I judge between beast and beast, between rams and goats.

34:18  LA: Nonne satis vobis erat pascua bona depasci ? insuper et reliquias pascuarum vestrarum conculcastis pedibus vestris : et cum purissimam aquam biberetis, reliquam pedibus vestris turbabatis :
       EN: Was it not enough for you to graze on good pasture? You also trampled the rest of your pasture with your feet. When you drank the clearest water, you muddied the remainder with your feet.

34:19  LA: et oves meæ his quæ conculcata pedibus vestris fuerant, pascebantur : et quæ pedes vestri turbaverant, hæc bibebant.
       EN: My sheep grazed on what your feet had trampled and drank what your feet had muddied.

34:20  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus ad vos : Ecce ego ipse judico inter pecus pingue et macilentum :
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God to you: See, I Myself judge between the fat beast and the lean.

34:21  LA: pro eo quod lateribus et humeris impingebatis, et cornibus vestris ventilabatis omnia infirma pecora, donec dispergerentur foras,
       EN: Because you pushed with flank and shoulder and tossed all the weak animals with your horns until they were scattered outside,

34:22  LA: salvabo gregem meum, et non erit ultra in rapinam, et judicabo inter pecus et pecus.]
       EN: I will save My flock, and it will no longer be plunder; I will judge between beast and beast.

34:23  LA: [Et suscitabo super eas pastorem unum qui pascat eas, servum meum David : ipse pascet eas, et ipse erit eis in pastorem.
       EN: I will raise up one shepherd over them to pasture them, My servant David. He will pasture them and be their shepherd.

34:24  LA: Ego autem Dominus ero eis in Deum, et servus meus David princeps in medio eorum : ego Dominus locutus sum.
       EN: I, the Lord, will be their God, and My servant David a prince among them. I, the Lord, have spoken.

34:25  LA: Et faciam cum eis pactum pacis, et cessare faciam bestias pessimas de terra : et qui habitant in deserto, securi dormient in saltibus.
       EN: I will make a covenant of peace with them and remove the most dangerous beasts from the land. Those dwelling in the wilderness will sleep safely in the forests.

34:26  LA: Et ponam eos in circuitu collis mei benedictionem, et deducam imbrem in tempore suo : pluviæ benedictionis erunt.
       EN: I will make them a blessing around My hill and send down rain in its season; there will be showers of blessing.

34:27  LA: Et dabit lignum agri fructum suum, et terra dabit germen suum, et erunt in terra sua absque timore : et scient quia ego Dominus, cum contrivero catenas jugi eorum, et eruero eos de manu imperantium sibi.
       EN: The tree of the field will yield its fruit, and the land will yield its growth. They will be in their land without fear, and they will know that I am the Lord when I break the chains of their yoke and rescue them from the hand of those ruling over them.

34:28  LA: Et non erunt ultra in rapinam in gentibus, neque bestiæ terræ devorabunt eos : sed habitabunt confidenter absque ullo terrore.
       EN: They will no longer be plunder among the nations, nor will the beasts of the earth devour them. They will dwell confidently, without any terror.

34:29  LA: Et suscitabo eis germen nominatum, et non erunt ultra imminuti fame in terra, neque portabunt ultra opprobrium gentium.
       EN: I will raise up renowned growth for them, and they will no longer be diminished by famine in the land or bear the reproach of the nations.

34:30  LA: Et scient quia ego Dominus Deus eorum cum eis, et ipsi populus meus domus Israël, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: They will know that I, the Lord their God, am with them, and that they, the house of Israel, are My people, says the Lord God.

34:31  LA: Vos autem, greges mei, greges pascuæ meæ, homines estis : et ego Dominus Deus vester, dicit Dominus Deus.]
       EN: As for you, My flocks, the flocks of My pasture, you are human beings, and I am the Lord your God, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 35

35:1  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

35:2  LA: Fili hominis, pone faciem tuam adversum montem Seir, et prophetabis de eo, et dices illi :
      EN: “Son of man, set your face against Mount Seir, prophesy concerning it, and say to it:

35:3  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego ad te, mons Seir : et extendam manum meam super te, et dabo te desolatum atque desertum.
      EN: Thus says the Lord God: See, I am against you, Mount Seir. I will stretch out My hand against you and make you a desolate wilderness.

35:4  LA: Urbes tuas demoliar, et tu desertus eris : et scies quia ego Dominus.
      EN: I will demolish your cities, and you will be deserted, and you will know that I am the Lord.

35:5  LA: Eo quod fueris inimicus sempiternus, et concluseris filios Israël in manus gladii in tempore afflictionis eorum, in tempore iniquitatis extremæ :
      EN: Because you were an enduring enemy and shut the children of Israel into the power of the sword at the time of their affliction, at the time of their final iniquity,

35:6  LA: propterea vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, quoniam sanguini tradam te, et sanguis te persequetur : et cum sanguinem oderis, sanguis persequetur te.
      EN: therefore, as I live, says the Lord God, I will hand you over to blood, and blood will pursue you. Though you hate blood, blood will pursue you.

35:7  LA: Et dabo montem Seir desolatum atque desertum, et auferam de eo euntem et redeuntem.
      EN: I will make Mount Seir a desolate wilderness and remove from it anyone going out or coming back.

35:8  LA: Et implebo montes ejus occisorum suorum : in collibus tuis, et in vallibus tuis atque in torrentibus, interfecti gladio cadent.
      EN: I will fill its mountains with its slain; on your hills, in your valleys, and in your torrents those slain by the sword will fall.

35:9  LA: In solitudines sempiternas tradam te, et civitates tuæ non habitabuntur : et scietis quia ego Dominus Deus.
      EN: I will make you an everlasting wasteland, and your cities will be uninhabited, and you will know that I am the Lord God.

35:10  LA: Eo quod dixeris : Duæ gentes et duæ terræ meæ erunt, et hæreditate possidebo eas, cum Dominus esset ibi :
       EN: Because you said, ‘The two nations and the two lands will be mine, and I will possess them as an inheritance,’ though the Lord was there,

35:11  LA: propterea vivo ego, dicit Dominus Deus, quia faciam juxta iram tuam, et secundum zelum tuum, quem fecisti odio habens eos : et notus efficiar per eos, cum te judicavero.
       EN: therefore, as I live, says the Lord God, I will act according to your anger and according to the jealousy you displayed in your hatred of them. I will make Myself known through them when I judge you.

35:12  LA: Et scies quia ego Dominus audivi universa opprobria tua quæ locutus es de montibus Israël, dicens : Deserti nobis ad devorandum dati sunt.
       EN: You will know that I, the Lord, heard all the insults you spoke against the mountains of Israel, saying, ‘They are deserted; they have been given to us to devour.’

35:13  LA: Et insurrexistis super me ore vestro, et derogastis adversum me verba vestra : ego audivi.
       EN: You rose against Me with your mouth and spoke your disparaging words against Me. I heard.

35:14  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Lætante universa terra, in solitudinem te redigam :
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: While the whole earth rejoices, I will reduce you to a wilderness.

35:15  LA: sicuti gavisus es super hæreditatem domus Israël eo quod fuerit dissipata, sic faciam tibi : dissipatus eris, mons Seir, et Idumæa omnis : et scient quia ego Dominus.]
       EN: As you rejoiced over the inheritance of the house of Israel because it was devastated, so I will do to you. You will be devastated, Mount Seir and all Edom, and they will know that I am the Lord.”

EZEKIEL 36

36:1  LA: Tu autem, fili hominis, propheta super montes Israël, et dices : [Montes Israël, audite verbum Domini.
      EN: “You, son of man, prophesy concerning the mountains of Israel and say: Mountains of Israel, hear the word of the Lord.

36:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Eo quod dixerit inimicus de vobis : Euge, altitudines sempiternæ in hæreditatem datæ sunt nobis :
      EN: Thus says the Lord God: Because the enemy said of you, ‘Aha! The everlasting heights have been given to us as an inheritance,’

36:3  LA: propterea vaticinare, et dic : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pro eo quod desolati estis, et conculcati per circuitum, et facti in hæreditatem reliquis gentibus, et ascendistis super labium linguæ et opprobrium populi,
      EN: therefore prophesy and say: Thus says the Lord God: Because you have been desolated and trampled on every side, become an inheritance for the remaining nations, and been taken up on people's lips as a byword and reproach,

36:4  LA: propterea, montes Israël, audite verbum Domini Dei. Hæc dicit Dominus Deus montibus et collibus, torrentibus, vallibusque et desertis, parietinis et urbibus derelictis, quæ depopulatæ sunt et subsannatæ a reliquis gentibus per circuitum.
      EN: therefore, mountains of Israel, hear the word of the Lord God. Thus says the Lord God to the mountains and hills, torrents and valleys, wastelands, ruined walls, and abandoned cities, which have been plundered and mocked by the remaining nations all around:

36:5  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Quoniam in igne zeli mei locutus sum de reliquis gentibus, et de Idumæa universa, quæ dederunt terram meam sibi in hæreditatem cum gaudio, et toto corde et ex animo, et ejecerunt eam ut vastarent :
      EN: Thus says the Lord God: In the fire of My jealousy I have spoken concerning the remaining nations and all Edom, who assigned My land to themselves as an inheritance with joy, with all their heart and soul, and cast it out to devastate it.

36:6  LA: idcirco vaticinare super humum Israël, et dices montibus et collibus, jugis et vallibus : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego in zelo meo et in furore meo locutus sum, eo quod confusionem gentium sustinueritis.
      EN: Therefore prophesy concerning the soil of Israel and say to the mountains and hills, ridges and valleys: Thus says the Lord God: See, in My jealousy and My fury I have spoken, because you have endured the shame inflicted by the nations.

36:7  LA: Idcirco hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ego levavi manum meam, ut gentes quæ in circuitu vestro sunt, ipsæ confusionem suam portent.
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: I have raised My hand so that the nations around you will themselves bear their shame.

36:8  LA: Vos autem, montes Israël, ramos vestros germinetis, et fructum vestrum afferatis populo meo Israël : prope enim est ut veniat.
      EN: But you, mountains of Israel, put forth your branches and bear your fruit for My people Israel, for their coming is near.

36:9  LA: Quia ecce ego ad vos, et convertar ad vos : et arabimini, et accipietis sementem.
      EN: For see, I am for you and will turn toward you; you will be plowed and receive seed.

36:10  LA: Et multiplicabo in vobis homines, omnemque domum Israël : et habitabuntur civitates, et ruinosa instaurabuntur.
       EN: I will multiply people upon you, the whole house of Israel; the cities will be inhabited, and the ruined places restored.

36:11  LA: Et replebo vos hominibus et jumentis, et multiplicabuntur, et crescent : et habitare vos faciam sicut a principio, bonisque donabo majoribus quam habuistis ab initio : et scietis quia ego Dominus.
       EN: I will fill you with people and beasts, and they will multiply and increase. I will make you inhabited as at first and bestow greater good upon you than you had at the beginning, and you will know that I am the Lord.

36:12  LA: Et adducam super vos homines, populum meum Israël, et hæreditate possidebunt te : et eris eis in hæreditatem, et non addes ultra ut absque eis sis.
       EN: I will bring people upon you, My people Israel, and they will possess you as an inheritance. You will be their inheritance, and you will no longer be without them.

36:13  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Pro eo quod dicunt de vobis : Devoratrix hominum es, et suffocans gentem tuam :
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: Because they say of you, ‘You devour human beings and choke your own nation,’

36:14  LA: propterea homines non comedes amplius, et gentem tuam non necabis ultra, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: therefore you will no longer eat human beings or kill your nation, says the Lord God.

36:15  LA: Nec auditam faciam in te amplius confusionem gentium, et opprobrium populorum nequaquam portabis : et gentem tuam non amittes amplius, ait Dominus Deus.]
       EN: I will no longer let the nations' taunts be heard in you, and you will bear the peoples' reproach no more. You will no longer lose your nation, says the Lord God.”

36:16  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

36:17  LA: [Fili hominis, domus Israël habitaverunt in humo sua, et polluerunt eam in viis suis et in studiis suis : juxta immunditiam menstruatæ facta est via eorum coram me.
       EN: “Son of man, the house of Israel dwelt upon their own soil and defiled it by their ways and practices. Their way before Me was like the uncleanness of a menstruating woman.

36:18  LA: Et effudi indignationem meam super eos pro sanguine quem fuderunt super terram, et in idolis suis polluerunt eam.
       EN: I poured out My indignation upon them for the blood they shed upon the land, and because they defiled it with their idols.

36:19  LA: Et dispersi eos in gentes, et ventilati sunt in terras : juxta vias eorum et adinventiones eorum judicavi eos.
       EN: I scattered them among the nations, and they were dispersed through the lands. According to their ways and their contrivances I judged them.

36:20  LA: Et ingressi sunt ad gentes ad quas introierunt : et polluerunt nomen sanctum meum, cum diceretur de eis : Populus Domini iste est, et de terra ejus egressi sunt.
       EN: They entered the nations to which they went and profaned My holy name, when it was said of them, ‘These are the Lord's people, yet they have gone out from His land.’

36:21  LA: Et peperci nomini sancto meo, quod polluerat domus Israël in gentibus ad quas ingressi sunt.
       EN: I had regard for My holy name, which the house of Israel had profaned among the nations to which they went.

36:22  LA: Idcirco dices domui Israël : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Non propter vos ego faciam, domus Israël, sed propter nomen sanctum meum, quod polluistis in gentibus ad quas intrastis.
       EN: Therefore say to the house of Israel: Thus says the Lord God: I will act, house of Israel, not for your sake, but for My holy name, which you profaned among the nations you entered.

36:23  LA: Et sanctificabo nomen meum magnum quod pollutum est inter gentes, quod polluistis in medio earum : ut sciant gentes quia ego Dominus, ait Dominus exercituum, cum sanctificatus fuero in vobis coram eis.
       EN: I will sanctify My great name, profaned among the nations, which you profaned in their midst, so that the nations may know that I am the Lord, says the Lord of hosts, when I manifest My holiness in you before them.

36:24  LA: Tollam quippe vos de gentibus, et congregabo vos de universis terris, et adducam vos in terram vestram.
       EN: For I will take you from the nations, gather you from all the lands, and bring you into your own land.

36:25  LA: Et effundam super vos aquam mundam, et mundabimini ab omnibus inquinamentis vestris, et ab universis idolis vestris mundabo vos.
       EN: I will pour clean water over you, and you will be cleansed of all your defilements; I will cleanse you of all your idols.

36:26  LA: Et dabo vobis cor novum, et spiritum novum ponam in medio vestri : et auferam cor lapideum de carne vestra, et dabo vobis cor carneum.
       EN: I will give you a new heart and place a new spirit within you. I will remove the heart of stone from your flesh and give you a heart of flesh.

36:27  LA: Et spiritum meum ponam in medio vestri : et faciam ut in præceptis meis ambuletis, et judicia mea custodiatis et operemini.
       EN: I will place My Spirit within you and cause you to walk in My precepts, keep My judgments, and carry them out.

36:28  LA: Et habitabitis in terra quam dedi patribus vestris : et eritis mihi in populum, et ego ero vobis in Deum.
       EN: You will dwell in the land that I gave your fathers; you will be My people, and I will be your God.

36:29  LA: Et salvabo vos ex universis inquinamentis vestris : et vocabo frumentum et multiplicabo illud, et non imponam vobis famem.
       EN: I will save you from all your defilements; I will call for grain and multiply it, and I will not impose famine upon you.

36:30  LA: Et multiplicabo fructum ligni, et genimina agri, ut non portetis ultra opprobrium famis in gentibus.
       EN: I will multiply the fruit of the tree and the produce of the field, so that you may no longer bear the reproach of famine among the nations.

36:31  LA: Et recordabimini viarum vestrarum pessimarum, studiorumque non bonorum : et displicebunt vobis iniquitates vestræ et scelera vestra.
       EN: You will remember your most evil ways and your practices that were not good, and your iniquities and your crimes will displease you.

36:32  LA: Non propter vos ego faciam, ait Dominus Deus, notum sit vobis : confundimini, et erubescite super viis vestris, domus Israël.]
       EN: It is not for your sake that I will act, says the Lord God; let that be known to you. Be ashamed and blush over your ways, house of Israel.

36:33  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : In die qua mundavero vos ex omnibus iniquitatibus vestris, et inhabitari fecero urbes, et instauravero ruinosa,
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: On the day when I cleanse you of all your iniquities, make the cities inhabited, and restore the ruined places,

36:34  LA: et terra deserta fuerit exculta, quæ quondam erat desolata in oculis omnis viatoris,
       EN: and the desolate land is cultivated, which once lay waste before the eyes of every traveler,

36:35  LA: dicent : Terra illa inculta facta est ut hortus voluptatis : et civitates desertæ, et destitutæ atque suffossæ, munitæ sederunt.
       EN: they will say, ‘That uncultivated land has become like a garden of delight, and the deserted, abandoned, and undermined cities stand fortified.’

36:36  LA: Et scient gentes quæcumque derelictæ fuerint in circuitu vestro, quia ego Dominus ædificavi dissipata, plantavique inculta : ego Dominus locutus sim, et fecerim.
       EN: Whatever nations remain around you will know that I, the Lord, rebuilt the devastated places and planted the uncultivated land; I, the Lord, have spoken and have acted.

36:37  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Adhuc in hoc invenient me domus Israël, ut faciam eis : multiplicabo eos sicut gregem hominum,
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: In this too the house of Israel will find Me, that I may act for them: I will multiply them like a flock of people.

36:38  LA: ut gregem sanctum, ut gregem Jerusalem in solemnitatibus ejus : sic erunt civitates desertæ, plenæ gregibus hominum : et scient quia ego Dominus.]
       EN: Like a holy flock, like Jerusalem's flock at her solemn feasts, so will the deserted cities be filled with flocks of people, and they will know that I am the Lord.”

EZEKIEL 37

37:1  LA: Facta est super me manus Domini, et eduxit me in spiritu Domini, et dimisit me in medio campi qui erat plenus ossibus.
      EN: The Lord's hand came upon me, and He brought me out in the Spirit of the Lord and set me down in the midst of a plain full of bones.

37:2  LA: Et circumduxit me per ea in gyro : erant autem multa valde super faciem campi, siccaque vehementer.
      EN: He led me around among them. There were very many upon the surface of the plain, and they were exceedingly dry.

37:3  LA: Et dixit ad me : Fili hominis, putasne vivent ossa ista ? Et dixi : Domine Deus, tu nosti.
      EN: He said to me, “Son of man, do you think these bones will live?” I said, “Lord God, You know.”

37:4  LA: Et dixit ad me : Vaticinare de ossibus istis, et dices eis : Ossa arida, audite verbum Domini.
      EN: He said to me, “Prophesy concerning these bones and say to them: Dry bones, hear the word of the Lord.

37:5  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus ossibus his : Ecce ego intromittam in vos spiritum, et vivetis.
      EN: Thus says the Lord God to these bones: See, I will put breath into you, and you will live.

37:6  LA: Et dabo super vos nervos, et succrescere faciam super vos carnes, et superextendam in vobis cutem, et dabo vobis spiritum, et vivetis : et scietis quia ego Dominus.
      EN: I will place sinews upon you, make flesh grow over you, spread skin over you, and give you breath, and you will live. You will know that I am the Lord.”

37:7  LA: Et prophetavi sicut præceperat mihi : factus est autem sonitus, prophetante me, et ecce commotio : et accesserunt ossa ad ossa, unumquodque ad juncturam suam.
      EN: I prophesied as He had commanded me. As I prophesied there was a sound, and see, a shaking; bones came to bones, each to its own joint.

37:8  LA: Et vidi, et ecce super ea nervi et carnes ascenderunt, et extenta est in eis cutis desuper : et spiritum non habebant.
      EN: I looked, and see, sinews and flesh grew over them, and skin was spread over them, but they had no breath.

37:9  LA: Et dixit ad me : Vaticinare ad spiritum : vaticinare, fili hominis, et dices ad spiritum : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : A quatuor ventis veni, spiritus, et insuffla super interfectos istos, et reviviscant.
      EN: He said to me, “Prophesy to the breath; prophesy, son of man, and say to the breath: Thus says the Lord God: Come from the four winds, O breath, and breathe upon these slain, that they may live again.”

37:10  LA: Et prophetavi sicut præceperat mihi : et ingressus est in ea spiritus, et vixerunt : steteruntque super pedes suos, exercitus grandis nimis valde.
       EN: I prophesied as He had commanded me, and breath entered them, and they lived. They stood upon their feet, an exceedingly great army.

37:11  LA: Et dixit ad me : Fili hominis, ossa hæc universa, domus Israël est. Ipsi dicunt : Aruerunt ossa nostra, et periit spes nostra, et abscissi sumus.
       EN: He said to me, “Son of man, all these bones are the house of Israel. They say, ‘Our bones have dried up, our hope has perished, and we have been cut off.’

37:12  LA: Propterea vaticinare, et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego aperiam tumulos vestros, et educam vos de sepulchris vestris, populus meus, et inducam vos in terram Israël.
       EN: Therefore prophesy and say to them: Thus says the Lord God: See, I will open your tombs and bring you out of your graves, My people, and lead you into the land of Israel.

37:13  LA: Et scietis quia ego Dominus, cum aperuero sepulchra vestra, et eduxero vos de tumulis vestris, popule meus,
       EN: You will know that I am the Lord when I open your graves and bring you out of your tombs, My people,

37:14  LA: et dedero spiritum meum in vobis, et vixeritis : et requiescere vos faciam super humum vestram, et scietis quia ego Dominus locutus sum, et feci, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: and give My Spirit within you, and you live. I will settle you upon your own soil, and you will know that I, the Lord, have spoken and have acted, says the Lord God.”

37:15  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

37:16  LA: Et tu, fili hominis, sume tibi lignum unum, et scribe super illud : Judæ, et filiorum Israël sociorum ejus : et tolle lignum alterum, et scribe super illud : Joseph, ligno Ephraim, et cunctæ domui Israël sociorumque ejus.
       EN: “You, son of man, take one stick and write upon it, ‘Of Judah and of the children of Israel his companions.’ Take another stick and write upon it, ‘Of Joseph, the stick of Ephraim, and of the whole house of Israel and his companions.’

37:17  LA: Et adjunge illa unum ad alterum tibi in lignum unum : et erunt in unionem in manu tua.
       EN: Join them to one another for yourself as one stick, and they will become one in your hand.

37:18  LA: Cum autem dixerint ad te filii populi tui, loquentes : Nonne indicas nobis quid in his tibi velis ?
       EN: When the children of your people say to you, ‘Will you not tell us what you mean by these?’

37:19  LA: loqueris ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego assumam lignum Joseph, quod est in manu Ephraim, et tribus Israël, quæ sunt ei adjunctæ, et dabo eas pariter cum ligno Juda, et faciam eas in lignum unum : et erunt unum in manu ejus.
       EN: you will say to them: Thus says the Lord God: See, I will take Joseph's stick, which is in Ephraim's hand, and the tribes of Israel joined to him. I will put them together with Judah's stick and make them one stick, and they will be one in his hand.

37:20  LA: Erunt autem ligna super quæ scripseris in manu tua in oculis eorum.
       EN: The sticks upon which you write will be in your hand before their eyes.

37:21  LA: Et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego assumam filios Israël de medio nationum ad quas abierunt : et congregabo eos undique, et adducam eos ad humum suam.
       EN: Say to them: Thus says the Lord God: See, I will take the children of Israel from among the nations to which they went, gather them from every side, and bring them to their own soil.

37:22  LA: Et faciam eos in gentem unam in terra in montibus Israël, et rex unus erit omnibus imperans : et non erunt ultra duæ gentes, nec dividentur amplius in duo regna,
       EN: I will make them one nation in the land, upon the mountains of Israel, and one king will rule them all. They will no longer be two nations or be divided into two kingdoms.

37:23  LA: neque polluentur ultra in idolis suis, et abominationibus suis, et cunctis iniquitatibus suis : et salvos eos faciam de universis sedibus in quibus peccaverunt, et emundabo eos : et erunt mihi populus, et ego ero eis Deus.
       EN: They will no longer be defiled by their idols, their abominations, and all their iniquities. I will save them from all the dwelling places where they sinned and cleanse them. They will be My people, and I will be their God.

37:24  LA: Et servus meus David rex super eos, et pastor unus erit omnium eorum. In judiciis meis ambulabunt, et mandata mea custodient, et facient ea :
       EN: My servant David will be king over them, and they will all have one shepherd. They will walk in My judgments, keep My commandments, and carry them out.

37:25  LA: et habitabunt super terram quam dedi servo meo Jacob, in qua habitaverunt patres vestri : et habitabunt super eam ipsi, et filii eorum, et filii filiorum eorum, usque in sempiternum : et David servus meus princeps eorum in perpetuum.
       EN: They will dwell upon the land that I gave My servant Jacob, in which your fathers dwelt. They, their children, and their children's children will dwell upon it forever, and My servant David will be their prince forever.

37:26  LA: Et percutiam illis fœdus pacis : pactum sempiternum erit eis. Et fundabo eos, et multiplicabo, et dabo sanctificationem meam in medio eorum in perpetuum.
       EN: I will make a covenant of peace with them; it will be an everlasting covenant for them. I will establish them, multiply them, and place My sanctuary among them forever.

37:27  LA: Et erit tabernaculum meum in eis : et ero eis Deus, et ipsi erunt mihi populus.
       EN: My tabernacle will be among them; I will be their God, and they will be My people.

37:28  LA: Et scient gentes quia ego Dominus sanctificator Israël, cum fuerit sanctificatio mea in medio eorum in perpetuum.
       EN: The nations will know that I am the Lord who sanctifies Israel when My sanctuary is among them forever.”

EZEKIEL 38

38:1  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

38:2  LA: Fili hominis, pone faciem tuam contra Gog, terram Magog, principem capitis Mosoch et Thubal, et vaticinare de eo.
      EN: “Son of man, set your face against Gog, the land of Magog, the chief ruler of Meshech and Tubal, and prophesy concerning him.

38:3  LA: Et dices ad eum : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego ad te, Gog, principem capitis Mosoch et Thubal.
      EN: Say to him: Thus says the Lord God: See, I am against you, Gog, chief ruler of Meshech and Tubal.

38:4  LA: Et circumagam te, et ponam frenum in maxillis tuis : et educam te, et omnem exercitum tuum, equos et equites vestitos loricis universos, multitudinem magnam, hastam et clypeum arripientium et gladium.
      EN: I will turn you about and put a bridle in your jaws. I will bring you out with all your army, horses and horsemen all clothed in breastplates, a great multitude grasping spear and shield and sword.

38:5  LA: Persæ, Æthiopes, et Libyes cum eis, omnes scutati et galeati.
      EN: Persians, Ethiopians, and Libyans are with them, all with shields and helmets;

38:6  LA: Gomer et universa agmina ejus, domus Thogorma, latera aquilonis, et totum robur ejus, populique multi tecum.
      EN: Gomer and all its troops, the house of Togarmah from the far north and all its forces, and many peoples with you.

38:7  LA: Præpara et instrue te, et omnem multitudinem tuam quæ coacervata est ad te : et esto eis in præceptum.
      EN: Prepare and equip yourself, you and all your multitude gathered to you, and be their commander.

38:8  LA: Post dies multos visitaberis : in novissimo annorum venies ad terram quæ reversa est a gladio, et congregata est de populis multis ad montes Israël, qui fuerunt deserti jugiter : hæc de populis educta est, et habitabunt in ea confidenter universi.
      EN: After many days you will be visited. In the last years you will come to a land recovered from the sword and gathered from many peoples to the mountains of Israel, which had long been deserted. It has been brought out from the peoples, and all will dwell in it confidently.

38:9  LA: Ascendens autem quasi tempestas venies, et quasi nubes, ut operias terram : tu et omnia agmina tua, et populi multi tecum.]
      EN: You will come up like a storm, like a cloud to cover the land, you and all your troops and many peoples with you.

38:10  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : In die illa, ascendent sermones super cor tuum, et cogitabis cogitationem pessimam :
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: On that day thoughts will rise in your heart, and you will devise a most evil plan.

38:11  LA: et dices : Ascendam ad terram absque muro : veniam ad quiescentes habitantesque secure : hi omnes habitant sine muro ; vectes et portæ non sunt eis :
       EN: You will say, ‘I will go up against a land without a wall; I will come upon those at rest, dwelling securely. They all dwell without a wall; they have neither bars nor gates,’

38:12  LA: ut diripias spolia, et invadas prædam ; ut inferas manum tuam super eos qui deserti fuerant, et postea restituti, et super populum qui est congregatus ex gentibus, qui possidere cœpit et esse habitator umbilici terræ.
       EN: to seize spoil and take booty, to lay your hand upon those who had been deserted and afterward restored, and upon a people gathered from the nations, who began to acquire possessions and dwell at the navel of the earth.

38:13  LA: Saba, et Dedan, et negotiatores Tharsis, et omnes leones ejus, dicent tibi : Numquid ad sumenda spolia tu venis ? ecce ad diripiendam prædam congregasti multitudinem tuam, ut tollas argentum et aurum, et auferas supellectilem atque substantiam, et diripias manubias infinitas.
       EN: Sheba and Dedan, the merchants of Tarshish and all its lions, will say to you, ‘Have you come to take spoil? See, you have gathered your multitude to plunder booty, to carry off silver and gold, take equipment and property, and seize limitless spoils.’

38:14  LA: Propterea vaticinare, fili hominis, et dices ad Gog : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Numquid non in die illo, cum habitaverit populus meus Israël confidenter, scies ?
       EN: Therefore prophesy, son of man, and say to Gog: Thus says the Lord God: On that day, when My people Israel dwell confidently, will you not know?

38:15  LA: Et venies de loco tuo a lateribus aquilonis, tu et populi multi tecum, ascensores equorum universi : cœtus magnus, et exercitus vehemens.
       EN: You will come from your place in the far north, you and many peoples with you, all riding horses, a great assembly and a powerful army.

38:16  LA: Et ascendes super populum meum Israël quasi nubes, ut operias terram. In novissimis diebus eris, et adducam te super terram meam : ut sciant gentes me cum sanctificatus fuero in te in oculis eorum, o Gog !]
       EN: You will come up against My people Israel like a cloud to cover the land. You will be in the last days, and I will bring you against My land, so that the nations may know Me when I manifest My holiness in you before their eyes, O Gog.

38:17  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Tu ergo ille es, de quo locutus sum in diebus antiquis in manu servorum meorum prophetarum Israël, qui prophetaverunt in diebus illorum temporum, ut adducerem te super eos.
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: You, then, are the one of whom I spoke in ancient days through My servants the prophets of Israel, who prophesied in the days of those times that I would bring you against them.

38:18  LA: Et erit in die illa, in die adventus Gog super terram Israël, ait Dominus Deus, ascendet indignatio mea in furore meo.
       EN: On that day, on the day of Gog's coming against the land of Israel, says the Lord God, My indignation will rise in My fury.

38:19  LA: Et in zelo meo, in igne iræ meæ locutus sum, quia in die illa erit commotio magna super terram Israël :
       EN: In My jealousy, in the fire of My anger, I have spoken: on that day there will be a great shaking upon the land of Israel.

38:20  LA: et commovebuntur a facie mea pisces maris, et volucres cæli, et bestiæ agri, et omne reptile quod movetur super humum, cunctique homines qui sunt super faciem terræ : et subvertentur montes, et cadent sepes, et omnis murus corruet in terram.
       EN: The fish of the sea, the birds of the sky, the wild beasts, every creeping thing that moves upon the soil, and all human beings upon the face of the earth will quake before Me. The mountains will be overturned, the enclosures will fall, and every wall will collapse to the ground.

38:21  LA: Et convocabo adversus eum in cunctis montibus meis gladium, ait Dominus Deus : gladius uniuscujusque in fratrem suum dirigetur.
       EN: I will summon a sword against him upon all My mountains, says the Lord God; each one's sword will be directed against his brother.

38:22  LA: Et judicabo eum peste, et sanguine, et imbre vehementi, et lapidibus immensis : ignem et sulphur pluam super eum, et super exercitum ejus, et super populos multos qui sunt cum eo.
       EN: I will judge him with pestilence, blood, violent rain, and enormous stones. I will rain fire and sulfur upon him, his army, and the many peoples with him.

38:23  LA: Et magnificabor, et sanctificabor, et notus ero in oculis multarum gentium : et scient quia ego Dominus.]
       EN: I will be magnified and sanctified and will be known before the eyes of many nations, and they will know that I am the Lord.”

EZEKIEL 39

39:1  LA: Tu autem, fili hominis, vaticinare adversum Gog, et dices : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce ego super te, Gog, principem capitis Mosoch et Thubal.
      EN: “You, son of man, prophesy against Gog and say: Thus says the Lord God: See, I am against you, Gog, chief ruler of Meshech and Tubal.

39:2  LA: Et circumagam te, et educam te, et ascendere te faciam de lateribus aquilonis, et adducam te super montes Israël.
      EN: I will turn you about, lead you out, bring you up from the far north, and bring you upon the mountains of Israel.

39:3  LA: Et percutiam arcum tuum in manu sinistra tua, et sagittas tuas de manu dextera tua dejiciam.
      EN: I will strike your bow in your left hand and knock your arrows from your right hand.

39:4  LA: Super montes Israël cades tu, et omnia agmina tua, et populi tui qui sunt tecum : feris, avibus, omnique volatili et bestiis terræ dedi te ad devorandum.
      EN: Upon the mountains of Israel you will fall, you and all your troops and the peoples with you. I have given you to wild creatures, birds, every winged thing, and the beasts of the earth to devour.

39:5  LA: Super faciem agri cades, quia ego locutus sum, ait Dominus Deus.
      EN: You will fall upon the open field, for I have spoken, says the Lord God.

39:6  LA: Et immittam ignem in Magog, et in his qui habitant in insulis confidenter : et scient quia ego Dominus.
      EN: I will send fire upon Magog and upon those dwelling confidently on the islands, and they will know that I am the Lord.

39:7  LA: Et nomen sanctum meum notum faciam in medio populi mei Israël, et non polluam nomen sanctum meum amplius : et scient gentes quia ego Dominus, Sanctus Israël.
      EN: I will make My holy name known among My people Israel and will no longer profane My holy name, and the nations will know that I am the Lord, the Holy One of Israel.

39:8  LA: Ecce venit, et factum est, ait Dominus Deus : hæc est dies de qua locutus sum.
      EN: See, it comes and is accomplished, says the Lord God. This is the day of which I spoke.

39:9  LA: Et egredientur habitatores de civitatibus Israël, et succendent et comburent arma, clypeum et hastas, arcum et sagittas, et baculos manuum et contos : et succendent ea igni septem annis.
      EN: The inhabitants will go out from Israel's cities and kindle and burn the weapons, shield and spears, bow and arrows, clubs and lances. They will burn them with fire for seven years.

39:10  LA: Et non portabunt ligna de regionibus, neque succident de saltibus : quoniam arma succendent igni, et deprædabuntur eos quibus prædæ fuerant, et diripient vastatores suos, ait Dominus Deus.]
       EN: They will not carry wood from the countryside or cut it from the forests, for they will burn the weapons with fire. They will plunder those who plundered them and despoil their despoilers, says the Lord God.

39:11  LA: [Et erit in die illa : dabo Gog locum nominatum sepulchrum in Israël, vallem viatorum ad orientem maris, quæ obstupescere faciet prætereuntes : et sepelient ibi Gog, et omnem multitudinem ejus, et vocabitur vallis multitudinis Gog.
       EN: On that day I will give Gog a notable place of burial in Israel, the valley of travelers east of the sea, which will astonish those passing by. There they will bury Gog and all his multitude, and it will be called the valley of Gog's multitude.

39:12  LA: Et sepelient eos domus Israël, ut mundent terram septem mensibus.
       EN: The house of Israel will bury them for seven months to cleanse the land.

39:13  LA: Sepeliet autem eum omnis populus terræ : et erit eis nominata dies in qua glorificatus sum, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: All the people of the land will bury him, and the day on which I was glorified will be renowned for them, says the Lord God.

39:14  LA: Et viros jugiter constituent lustrantes terram, qui sepeliant et requirant eos qui remanserant super faciem terræ, ut emundent eam : post menses autem septem quærere incipient.
       EN: They will appoint men to traverse the land continually, to bury and search for those remaining upon the land's surface, to cleanse it. After seven months they will begin the search.

39:15  LA: Et circuibunt peragrantes terram : cumque viderint os hominis, statuent juxta illud titulum, donec sepeliant illud pollinctores in valle multitudinis Gog.
       EN: They will go about traversing the land; when they see a human bone, they will set a marker beside it until the buriers bury it in the valley of Gog's multitude.

39:16  LA: Nomen autem civitatis Amona : et mundabunt terram.
       EN: The city's name will be Hamonah, and they will cleanse the land.

39:17  LA: Tu ergo, fili hominis, hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Dic omni volucri, et universis avibus, cunctisque bestiis agri : Convenite, properate, concurrite undique ad victimam meam quam ego immolo vobis, victimam grandem super montes Israël, ut comedatis carnem, et bibatis sanguinem.
       EN: You, then, son of man—thus says the Lord God—say to every winged creature, all birds, and all wild beasts: Gather, hurry, come together from every side to My sacrifice, which I sacrifice for you, a great sacrifice upon the mountains of Israel, that you may eat flesh and drink blood.

39:18  LA: Carnes fortium comedetis, et sanguinem principum terræ bibetis : arietum, et agnorum, et hircorum, taurorumque et altilium, et pinguium omnium.
       EN: You will eat the flesh of the mighty and drink the blood of the princes of the earth: rams, lambs, goats, bulls, and all the fattened and well-fed animals.

39:19  LA: Et comedetis adipem in saturitatem, et bibetis sanguinem in ebrietatem, de victima quam ego immolabo vobis :
       EN: You will eat fat until satisfied and drink blood until drunk from the sacrifice that I will sacrifice for you.

39:20  LA: et saturabimini super mensam meam de equo, et equite forti, et de universis viris bellatoribus, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: At My table you will be satisfied with horse and mighty rider and all warriors, says the Lord God.

39:21  LA: Et ponam gloriam meam in gentibus : et videbunt omnes gentes judicium meum quod fecerim, et manum meam quam posuerim super eos.
       EN: I will set My glory among the nations, and all nations will see the judgment I have carried out and the hand I have laid upon them.

39:22  LA: Et scient domus Israël quia ego Dominus Deus eorum, a die illa et deinceps.
       EN: The house of Israel will know that I am the Lord their God from that day onward.

39:23  LA: Et scient gentes quoniam in iniquitate sua capta sit domus Israël, eo quod dereliquerint me, et absconderim faciem meam ab eis, et tradiderim eos in manus hostium, et ceciderint in gladio universi.
       EN: The nations will know that the house of Israel was taken captive for their iniquity, because they abandoned Me and I hid My face from them, handed them over to their enemies, and they all fell by the sword.

39:24  LA: Juxta immunditiam eorum et scelus feci eis, et abscondi faciem meam ab illis.]
       EN: According to their uncleanness and their crime I dealt with them and hid My face from them.

39:25  LA: [Propterea hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Nunc reducam captivitatem Jacob, et miserebor omnis domus Israël, et assumam zelum pro nomine sancto meo.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Now I will bring back Jacob's captives, have mercy on the whole house of Israel, and be zealous for My holy name.

39:26  LA: Et portabunt confusionem suam, et omnem prævaricationem qua prævaricati sunt in me, cum habitaverint in terra sua confidenter, neminem formidantes :
       EN: They will bear their shame and all the treachery by which they betrayed Me, when they dwell confidently in their land, fearing no one,

39:27  LA: et reduxero eos de populis, et congregavero de terris inimicorum suorum, et sanctificatus fuero in eis, in oculis gentium plurimarum.
       EN: when I bring them back from the peoples, gather them from the lands of their enemies, and manifest My holiness in them before the eyes of many nations.

39:28  LA: Et scient quia ego Dominus Deus eorum, eo quod transtulerim eos in nationes, et congregaverim eos super terram suam, et non dereliquerim quemquam ex eis ibi.
       EN: They will know that I am the Lord their God, because I deported them among the nations and gathered them upon their own land and left none of them there.

39:29  LA: Et non abscondam ultra faciem meam ab eis, eo quod effuderim spiritum meum super omnem domum Israël, ait Dominus Deus.]
       EN: I will no longer hide My face from them, because I have poured out My Spirit upon the whole house of Israel, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 40

40:1  LA: In vigesimo quinto anno transmigrationis nostræ, in exordio anni, decima mensis, quartodecimo anno postquam percussa est civitas, in ipsa hac die, facta est super me manus Domini, et adduxit me illuc.
      EN: In the twenty-fifth year of our exile, at the beginning of the year, on the tenth day of the month, in the fourteenth year after the city was struck, on that very day the Lord's hand came upon me, and He brought me there.

40:2  LA: In visionibus Dei adduxit me in terram Israël, et dimisit me super montem excelsum nimis, super quem erat quasi ædificium civitatis vergentis ad austrum.
      EN: In visions of God He brought me into the land of Israel and set me upon a very high mountain, on which was something like the structure of a city facing south.

40:3  LA: Et introduxit me illuc : et ecce vir cujus erat species quasi species æris, et funiculus lineus in manu ejus, et calamus mensuræ in manu ejus : stabat autem in porta.
      EN: He brought me there, and see, a man whose appearance was like bronze, with a linen cord in his hand and a measuring reed in his hand, stood in the gateway.

40:4  LA: Et locutus est ad me idem vir : Fili hominis, vide oculis tuis, et auribus tuis audi, et pone cor tuum in omnia quæ ego ostendam tibi, quia ut ostendantur tibi adductus es huc. Annuntia omnia quæ tu vides domui Israël.
      EN: That same man spoke to me: “Son of man, see with your eyes, hear with your ears, and set your heart upon everything I will show you, for you were brought here so that these things might be shown to you. Declare everything you see to the house of Israel.”

40:5  LA: Et ecce murus forinsecus in circuitu domus undique : et in manu viri calamus mensuræ sex cubitorum et palmo : et mensus est latitudinem ædificii calamo uno, altitudinem quoque calamo uno.
      EN: See, there was a wall outside the house, surrounding it on every side. In the man's hand was a measuring reed of six cubits and a handbreadth. He measured the structure's thickness: one reed, and its height: one reed.

40:6  LA: Et venit ad portam quæ respiciebat viam orientalem, et ascendit per gradus ejus : et mensus est limen portæ calamo uno latitudinem, id est, limen unum calamo uno in latitudine.
      EN: He came to the gate facing east and climbed its steps. He measured the width of the gate's threshold: one reed, that is, one threshold one reed wide.

40:7  LA: Et thalamum uno calamo in longum, et uno calamo in latum : et inter thalamos, quinque cubitos.
      EN: He measured a chamber: one reed long and one reed wide, and between the chambers: five cubits.

40:8  LA: Et limen portæ, juxta vestibulum portæ intrinsecus, calamo uno.
      EN: The gate's threshold beside the gate's inner vestibule was one reed.

40:9  LA: Et mensus est vestibulum portæ octo cubitorum, et frontem ejus duobus cubitis : vestibulum autem portæ erat intrinsecus.
      EN: He measured the gate's vestibule: eight cubits, and its front face: two cubits. The gate's vestibule was on the inside.

40:10  LA: Porro thalami portæ ad viam orientalem, tres hinc et tres inde : mensura una trium, et mensura una frontium ex utraque parte.
       EN: The chambers of the east-facing gate were three on this side and three on that side. The three had one measurement, and the piers on either side had one measurement.

40:11  LA: Et mensus est latitudinem liminis portæ decem cubitorum, et longitudinem portæ tredecim cubitorum.
       EN: He measured the width of the gate's threshold: ten cubits, and the length of the gate: thirteen cubits.

40:12  LA: Et marginem ante thalamos, cubiti unius, et cubitus unus finis utrimque : thalami autem sex cubitorum erant hinc et inde.
       EN: The border before the chambers was one cubit, a boundary of one cubit on either side. The chambers on this side and that were six cubits.

40:13  LA: Et mensus est portam a tecto thalami usque ad tectum ejus, latitudinem viginti quinque cubitorum, ostium contra ostium.
       EN: He measured the gate from the roof of one chamber to its opposite roof, a width of twenty-five cubits, doorway opposite doorway.

40:14  LA: Et fecit frontes per sexaginta cubitos, et ad frontem atrium portæ undique per circuitum.
       EN: He made the piers sixty cubits, and the gate's court extended to the pier on every side around.

40:15  LA: Et ante faciem portæ quæ pertingebat usque ad faciem vestibuli portæ interioris, quinquaginta cubitos.
       EN: From the front of the gate, reaching to the front of the inner vestibule of the gate, there were fifty cubits.

40:16  LA: Et fenestras obliquas in thalamis et in frontibus eorum, quæ erant intra portam undique per circuitum : similiter autem erant et in vestibulis fenestræ per gyrum intrinsecus, et ante frontes pictura palmarum.
       EN: There were recessed windows in the chambers and in their piers, inside the gate all around. Likewise there were windows all around inside the vestibules, and palm-tree designs upon the piers.

40:17  LA: Et eduxit me ad atrium exterius : et ecce gazophylacia, et pavimentum stratum lapide in atrio per circuitum : triginta gazophylacia in circuitu pavimenti.
       EN: He brought me out to the outer court, and see, there were chambers and a pavement laid with stone in the court all around: thirty chambers around the pavement.

40:18  LA: Et pavimentum in fronte portarum, secundum longitudinem portarum erat inferius.
       EN: The pavement in front of the gates, corresponding to the length of the gates, was the lower pavement.

40:19  LA: Et mensus est latitudinem a facie portæ inferioris usque ad frontem atrii interioris extrinsecus : centum cubitos ad orientem et ad aquilonem.
       EN: He measured the width from the front of the lower gate to the outer front of the inner court: a hundred cubits, to the east and to the north.

40:20  LA: Portam quoque quæ respiciebat viam aquilonis atrii exterioris, mensus est tam in longitudine quam in latitudine.
       EN: He also measured the gate of the outer court that faced north, both its length and its width.

40:21  LA: Et thalamos ejus tres hinc et tres inde, et frontem ejus et vestibulum ejus secundum mensuram portæ prioris, quinquaginta cubitorum longitudinem ejus, et latitudinem viginti quinque cubitorum.
       EN: Its chambers were three on this side and three on that; its pier and vestibule followed the measurements of the first gate. Its length was fifty cubits, and its width twenty-five cubits.

40:22  LA: Fenestræ autem ejus, et vestibulum, et sculpturæ secundum mensuram portæ quæ respiciebat ad orientem : et septem graduum erat ascensus ejus, et vestibulum ante eam.
       EN: Its windows, vestibule, and carvings followed the measurements of the gate facing east. Its ascent had seven steps, and its vestibule was before it.

40:23  LA: Et porta atrii interioris contra portam aquilonis et orientalem : et mensus est a porta usque ad portam centum cubitos.
       EN: The inner court's gate stood opposite the north gate and the east gate, and he measured from gate to gate: a hundred cubits.

40:24  LA: Et eduxit me ad viam australem : et ecce porta quæ respiciebat ad austrum : et mensus est frontem ejus et vestibulum ejus juxta mensuras superiores.
       EN: He brought me toward the south, and see, a gate faced south. He measured its pier and vestibule according to the previous measurements.

40:25  LA: Et fenestras ejus, et vestibula in circuitu, sicut fenestras ceteras : quinquaginta cubitorum longitudine, et latitudine viginti quinque cubitorum.
       EN: Its windows and vestibules all around were like the other windows. It was fifty cubits long and twenty-five cubits wide.

40:26  LA: Et in gradibus septem ascendebatur ad eam, et vestibulum ante fores ejus : et cælatæ palmæ erant, una hinc, et altera inde, in fronte ejus.
       EN: Seven steps led up to it, and its vestibule was before its doors. Carved palms were on its pier, one on this side and another on that.

40:27  LA: Et porta atrii interioris in via australi : et mensus est a porta usque ad portam in via australi, centum cubitos.
       EN: The inner court had a gate toward the south. He measured from gate to gate toward the south: a hundred cubits.

40:28  LA: Et introduxit me in atrium interius ad portam australem : et mensus est portam juxta mensuras superiores.
       EN: He brought me into the inner court at the south gate and measured the gate according to the previous measurements.

40:29  LA: Thalamum ejus, et frontem ejus, et vestibulum ejus eisdem mensuris, et fenestras ejus, et vestibulum ejus in circuitu, quinquaginta cubitos longitudinis, et latitudinis viginti quinque cubitos.
       EN: Its chamber, pier, and vestibule had the same measurements, and its windows and vestibule were all around. Its length was fifty cubits and its width twenty-five cubits.

40:31  LA: et vestibulum ejus ad atrium exterius, et palmas ejus in fronte : et octo gradus erant quibus ascendebatur per eam.
       EN: Its vestibule faced the outer court, and its palms were upon the pier; eight steps led up through it.

40:32  LA: Et introduxit me in atrium interius, per viam orientalem : et mensus est portam secundum mensuras superiores.
       EN: He brought me into the inner court by the eastern way and measured the gate according to the previous measurements.

40:33  LA: Thalamum ejus, et frontem ejus, et vestibulum ejus, sicut supra : et fenestras ejus, et vestibula ejus in circuitu, longitudine quinquaginta cubitorum, et latitudine viginti quinque cubitorum.
       EN: Its chamber, pier, and vestibule were as above; its windows and vestibules were all around. It was fifty cubits long and twenty-five cubits wide.

40:34  LA: Et vestibulum ejus, id est, atrii exterioris, et palmæ cælatæ in fronte ejus, hinc et inde : et in octo gradibus ascensus ejus.
       EN: Its vestibule, that is, the one toward the outer court, had carved palms on its pier on this side and that; its ascent had eight steps.

40:35  LA: Et introduxit me ad portam quæ respiciebat ad aquilonem : et mensus est secundum mensuras superiores.
       EN: He brought me to the gate facing north and measured it according to the previous measurements.

40:36  LA: Thalamum ejus, et frontem ejus, et vestibulum ejus, et fenestras ejus per circuitum, longitudine quinquaginta cubitorum, et latitudine viginti quinque cubitorum.
       EN: Its chamber, pier, vestibule, and windows were all around. It was fifty cubits long and twenty-five cubits wide.

40:37  LA: Et vestibulum ejus respiciebat ad atrium exterius : et cælatura palmarum in fronte ejus, hinc et inde : et in octo gradibus ascensus ejus.
       EN: Its vestibule faced the outer court, and palm carvings were upon its pier on this side and that; its ascent had eight steps.

40:38  LA: Et per singula gazophylacia ostium in frontibus portarum : ibi lavabant holocaustum.
       EN: There was a doorway for each chamber at the gate piers; there they washed the burnt offering.

40:39  LA: Et in vestibulo portæ, duæ mensæ hinc, et duæ mensæ inde, ut immoletur super eas holocaustum, et pro peccato et pro delicto.
       EN: In the gate's vestibule were two tables on this side and two on that, for slaughtering upon them the burnt offering, the sin offering, and the guilt offering.

40:40  LA: Et ad latus exterius, quod ascendit ad ostium portæ quæ pergit ad aquilonem, duæ mensæ : et ad latus alterum, ante vestibulum portæ, duæ mensæ :
       EN: On the outer side, as one goes up to the entrance of the north-facing gate, were two tables, and on the other side, before the gate's vestibule, two tables.

40:41  LA: quatuor mensæ hinc, et quatuor mensæ inde : per latera portæ octo mensæ erant, super quas immolabant.
       EN: Four tables were on this side and four on that; along the sides of the gate were eight tables upon which they slaughtered the sacrifices.

40:42  LA: Quatuor autem mensæ ad holocaustum de lapidibus quadris exstructæ, longitudine cubiti unius et dimidii, et latitudine cubiti unius et dimidii, et altitudine cubiti unius : super quas ponant vasa in quibus immolatur holocaustum et victima.
       EN: The four tables for the burnt offering were built of dressed stones, a cubit and a half long, a cubit and a half wide, and one cubit high. On them they were to place the instruments with which the burnt offering and sacrificial victim are slaughtered.

40:43  LA: Et labia earum palmi unius, reflexa intrinsecus per circuitum : super mensas autem carnes oblationis.
       EN: Their rims were one handbreadth, turned inward all around; upon the tables was the flesh of the offering.

40:44  LA: Et extra portam interiorem, gazophylacia cantorum in atrio interiori, quod erat in latere portæ respicientis ad aquilonem : et facies eorum contra viam australem : una ex latere portæ orientalis, quæ respiciebat ad viam aquilonis.
       EN: Outside the inner gate were chambers for the singers in the inner court, which was beside the north-facing gate; their fronts faced south. One was beside the east gate, facing north.

40:45  LA: Et dixit ad me : Hoc est gazophylacium quod respicit viam meridianam : sacerdotum erit, qui excubant in custodiis templi.
       EN: He said to me, “This chamber facing south will belong to the priests who keep watch over the temple.
