40:46  LA: Porro gazophylacium quod respicit ad viam aquilonis, sacerdotum erit, qui excubant ad ministerium altaris : isti sunt filii Sadoc, qui accedunt de filiis Levi ad Dominum ut ministrent ei.
       EN: The chamber facing north will belong to the priests who keep watch over the service of the altar. These are the sons of Zadok, who from among the sons of Levi approach the Lord to minister to Him.”

40:47  LA: Et mensus est atrium longitudine centum cubitorum, et latitudine centum cubitorum per quadrum : et altare ante faciem templi.
       EN: He measured the court: a hundred cubits long and a hundred cubits wide, a square; the altar was in front of the temple.

40:48  LA: Et introduxit me in vestibulum templi : et mensus est vestibulum quinque cubitis hinc, et quinque cubitis inde : et latitudinem portæ trium cubitorum hinc, et trium cubitorum inde.
       EN: He brought me into the temple's vestibule and measured the vestibule: five cubits on this side and five on that, and the width of the gateway: three cubits on this side and three on that.

40:49  LA: Longitudinem autem vestibuli viginti cubitorum, et latitudinem undecim cubitorum, et octo gradibus ascendebatur ad eam. Et columnæ erant in frontibus : una hinc, et altera inde.
       EN: The vestibule was twenty cubits long and eleven cubits wide, and eight steps led up to it. There were columns at the piers, one on this side and another on that.

EZEKIEL 41

41:1  LA: Et introduxit me in templum, et mensus est frontes : sex cubitos latitudinis hinc, et sex cubitos latitudinis inde, latitudinem tabernaculi.
      EN: He brought me into the temple and measured the front faces: six cubits wide on this side and six cubits wide on that, the width of the tabernacle.

41:2  LA: Et latitudo portæ decem cubitorum erat : et latera portæ, quinque cubitis hinc, et quinque cubitis inde : et mensus est longitudinem ejus quadraginta cubitorum, et latitudinem viginti cubitorum.
      EN: The gateway was ten cubits wide, and the sides of the gateway were five cubits on this side and five on that. He measured its length: forty cubits, and its width: twenty cubits.

41:3  LA: Et introgressus intrinsecus, mensus est in fronte portæ duos cubitos : et portam, sex cubitorum : et latitudinem portæ septem cubitorum.
      EN: Going farther inside, he measured two cubits at the gateway's pier, the gateway: six cubits, and the width of the gateway: seven cubits.

41:4  LA: Et mensus est longitudinem ejus viginti cubitorum, et latitudinem ejus viginti cubitorum, ante faciem templi. Et dixit ad me : Hoc est Sanctum sanctorum.
      EN: He measured its length: twenty cubits, and its width: twenty cubits, across the front of the temple. He said to me, “This is the Holy of Holies.”

41:5  LA: Et mensus est parietem domus sex cubitorum : et latitudinem lateris quatuor cubitorum undique per circuitum domus.
      EN: He measured the wall of the house: six cubits, and the width of the side chamber: four cubits, all around the house on every side.

41:6  LA: Latera autem, latus ad latus, bis triginta tria : et erant eminentia, quæ ingrederentur per parietem domus, in lateribus per circuitum, ut continerent, et non attingerent parietem templi.
      EN: The side chambers, side by side, were twice thirty-three. There were projections that entered along the wall of the house, at the side chambers all around, to support them without touching the temple wall.

41:7  LA: Et platea erat in rotundum, ascendens sursum per cochleam, et in cœnaculum templi deferebat per gyrum : idcirco latius erat templum in superioribus : et sic de inferioribus ascendebatur ad superiora in medium.
      EN: There was a passage all around, ascending by a spiral and leading around to the temple's upper chamber. Therefore the temple was broader in its upper parts, and thus one went up from the lower parts to the upper through the middle.

41:8  LA: Et vidi in domo altitudinem per circuitum, fundata latera ad mensuram calami sex cubitorum spatio :
      EN: I saw the height of the house all around: the side chambers were founded to the measure of a reed, a space of six cubits.

41:9  LA: et latitudinem per parietem lateris forinsecus quinque cubitorum : et erat interior domus in lateribus domus.
      EN: The thickness of the wall of the side chamber outside was five cubits, and the inner part of the house was within the side chambers of the house.

41:10  LA: Et inter gazophylacia latitudinem viginti cubitorum in circuitu domus undique,
       EN: Between the chambers was a width of twenty cubits around the house on every side.

41:11  LA: et ostium lateris ad orationem : ostium unum ad viam aquilonis, et ostium unum ad viam australem : et latitudinem loci ad orationem, quinque cubitorum in circuitu.
       EN: The doorway of the side chamber led to the place of prayer: one doorway toward the north and one toward the south. The place of prayer was five cubits wide all around.

41:12  LA: Et ædificium, quod erat separatum, versumque ad viam respicientem ad mare, latitudinis septuaginta cubitorum : paries autem ædificii, quinque cubitorum latitudinis per circuitum : et longitudo ejus nonaginta cubitorum.
       EN: The separate building facing the direction of the sea was seventy cubits wide; the building's wall was five cubits thick all around, and its length was ninety cubits.

41:13  LA: Et mensus est domus longitudinem, centum cubitorum : et quod separatum erat ædificium, et parietes ejus, longitudinis centum cubitorum.
       EN: He measured the length of the house: a hundred cubits; the separate building and its walls were a hundred cubits long.

41:14  LA: Latitudo autem ante faciem domus, et ejus quod erat separatum contra orientem, centum cubitorum.
       EN: The width across the front of the house and of the separate area toward the east was a hundred cubits.

41:15  LA: Et mensus est longitudinem ædificii contra faciem ejus, quod erat separatum ad dorsum : ethecas ex utraque parte centum cubitorum : et templum interius, et vestibula atrii.
       EN: He measured the length of the building facing the separate area at the back, with its galleries on either side: a hundred cubits, and the inner temple and the vestibules of the court.

41:16  LA: Limina, et fenestras obliquas, et ethecas in circuitu per tres partes, contra uniuscujusque limen, stratumque ligno per gyrum in circuitu : terra autem usque ad fenestras, et fenestræ clausæ super ostia.
       EN: The thresholds, recessed windows, and galleries all around on three sides, opposite each threshold, were covered with wood all around, from the ground to the windows; the windows were closed above the doorways.

41:17  LA: Et usque ad domum interiorem, et forinsecus per omnem parietem in circuitu, intrinsecus et forinsecus, ad mensuram.
       EN: It extended to the inner house and outside, along every wall all around, inside and outside, according to measure.

41:18  LA: Et fabrefacta cherubim et palmæ : et palma inter cherub et cherub, duasque facies habebat cherub.
       EN: Cherubim and palm trees were fashioned, with a palm tree between cherub and cherub. Each cherub had two faces:

41:19  LA: Faciem hominis juxta palmam ex hac parte, et faciem leonis juxta palmam ex alia parte : expressam per omnem domum in circuitu.
       EN: a human face beside the palm tree on this side and a lion's face beside the palm tree on the other side, depicted throughout the house all around.

41:20  LA: De terra usque ad superiora portæ, cherubim et palmæ cælatæ erant in pariete templi.
       EN: From the ground to the tops of the doorways, cherubim and palm trees were carved upon the temple wall.

41:21  LA: Limen quadrangulum, et facies sanctuarii, aspectus contra aspectum.
       EN: The threshold was square, and the front of the sanctuary had an appearance matching the other.

41:22  LA: Altaris lignei trium cubitorum altitudo, et longitudo ejus duorum cubitorum : et anguli ejus, et longitudo ejus, et parietes ejus lignei. Et locutus est ad me : Hæc est mensa coram Domino.
       EN: The wooden altar was three cubits high and two cubits long; its corners, its length, and its walls were wooden. He said to me, “This is the table before the Lord.”

41:23  LA: Et duo ostia erant in templo et in sanctuario.
       EN: There were two doors in the temple and in the sanctuary.

41:24  LA: Et in duobus ostiis ex utraque parte bina erant ostiola quæ in se invicem plicabantur : bina enim ostia erant ex utraque parte ostiorum.
       EN: In the two doors, on either side, were two leaves that folded back upon one another; for there were two leaves on each side of the doorways.

41:25  LA: Et cælata erant in ipsis ostiis templi cherubim, et sculpturæ palmarum, sicut in parietibus quoque expressæ erant : quam ob rem et grossiora erant ligna in vestibuli fronte forinsecus.
       EN: On the temple doors themselves cherubim and palm designs were carved, as they were depicted upon the walls. For this reason the timbers at the front of the vestibule outside were also thicker.

41:26  LA: Super quæ fenestræ obliquæ, et similitudo palmarum hinc atque inde in humerulis vestibuli, secundum latera domus, latitudinemque parietum.
       EN: Above them were recessed windows and palm-tree forms on this side and that, upon the vestibule's shoulders, along the sides of the house and the breadth of the walls.

EZEKIEL 42

42:1  LA: Et eduxit me in atrium exterius, per viam ducentem ad aquilonem, et introduxit me in gazophylacium quod erat contra separatum ædificium, et contra ædem vergentem ad aquilonem.
      EN: He brought me out to the outer court by the way leading north and brought me into the chamber opposite the separate building and opposite the structure facing north.

42:2  LA: In facie longitudinis, centum cubitos ostii aquilonis, et latitudinis quinquaginta cubitos,
      EN: Along the front, the length at the north entrance was a hundred cubits, and the width fifty cubits,

42:3  LA: contra viginti cubitos atrii interioris, et contra pavimentum stratum lapide atrii exterioris, ubi erat porticus juncta porticui triplici.
      EN: opposite the twenty cubits of the inner court and opposite the stone pavement of the outer court, where a portico adjoined a threefold portico.

42:4  LA: Et ante gazophylacia deambulatio decem cubitorum latitudinis, ad interiora respiciens viæ cubiti unius. Et ostia eorum ad aquilonem :
      EN: Before the chambers was a walkway ten cubits wide, facing inward toward a passage of one cubit. Their doorways faced north.

42:5  LA: ubi erant gazophylacia in superioribus humiliora, quia supportabant porticus quæ ex illis eminebant de inferioribus, et de mediis ædificii.
      EN: There the upper chambers were lower, because they supported the porticoes projecting from them beyond the building's lower and middle levels.

42:6  LA: Tristega enim erant, et non habebant columnas, sicut erant columnæ atriorum : propterea eminebant de inferioribus, et de mediis a terra cubitis quinquaginta.
      EN: For they were three stories high and had no columns like the columns of the courts; therefore they projected beyond the lower and middle levels, fifty cubits from the ground.

42:7  LA: Et peribolus exterior secundum gazophylacia, quæ erant in via atrii exterioris ante gazophylacia : longitudo ejus quinquaginta cubitorum :
      EN: The outer enclosure alongside the chambers, toward the outer court before the chambers, was fifty cubits long.

42:8  LA: quia longitudo erat gazophylaciorum atrii exterioris quinquaginta cubitorum, et longitudo ante faciem templi, centum cubitorum.
      EN: For the chambers of the outer court were fifty cubits long, and those facing the temple were a hundred cubits long.

42:9  LA: Et erat subter gazophylacia hæc introitus ab oriente, ingredientium in ea de atrio exteriori.
      EN: Below these chambers was an entrance from the east for those entering them from the outer court.

42:10  LA: In latitudine periboli atrii quod erat contra viam orientalem, in faciem ædificii separati, et erant ante ædificium gazophylacia.
       EN: Along the width of the court's enclosure facing east, opposite the separate building, there were chambers before the building.

42:11  LA: Et via ante faciem eorum, juxta similitudinem gazophylaciorum quæ erant in via aquilonis : secundum longitudinem eorum, sic et latitudo eorum, et omnis introitus eorum, et similitudines, et ostia eorum.
       EN: A passage was before them, like the chambers toward the north. Their length and width corresponded, as did all their entrances, arrangements, and doorways.

42:12  LA: Secundum ostia gazophylaciorum, quæ erant in via respiciente ad notum : ostium in capite viæ, quæ via erat ante vestibulum separatum per viam orientalem ingredientibus.
       EN: Corresponding to the doorways of the chambers facing south was a doorway at the head of the passage, the passage before the separate vestibule as one entered from the east.

42:13  LA: Et dixit ad me : Gazophylacia aquilonis, et gazophylacia austri, quæ sunt ante ædificium separatum, hæc sunt gazophylacia sancta, in quibus vescuntur sacerdotes qui appropinquant ad Dominum in Sancta sanctorum : ibi ponent Sancta sanctorum et oblationem pro peccato et pro delicto : locus enim sanctus est.
       EN: He said to me, “The north chambers and the south chambers before the separate building are the holy chambers in which the priests who approach the Lord into the Holy of Holies eat. There they will place the most holy things and the offering for sin and for guilt, for the place is holy.

42:14  LA: Cum autem ingressi fuerint sacerdotes, non egredientur de sanctis in atrium exterius : et ibi reponent vestimenta sua in quibus ministrant, quia sancta sunt, vestienturque vestimentis aliis : et sic procedent ad populum.
       EN: When the priests enter, they will not go out from the holy places into the outer court. There they will put away the garments in which they minister, because they are holy, and put on other garments; then they will go out to the people.”

42:15  LA: Cumque complesset mensuras domus interioris, eduxit me per viam portæ quæ respiciebat ad viam orientalem : et mensus est eam undique per circuitum.
       EN: When he finished measuring the inner house, he brought me out through the gate facing east and measured it all around.

42:16  LA: Mensus est autem contra ventum orientalem calamo mensuræ, quingentos calamos in calamo mensuræ per circuitum.
       EN: He measured on the eastern side with the measuring reed: five hundred reeds by the measuring reed, all around.

42:17  LA: Et mensus est contra ventum aquilonis quingentos calamos in calamo mensuræ per gyrum.
       EN: He measured on the northern side: five hundred reeds by the measuring reed, all around.

42:18  LA: Et ad ventum australem mensus est quingentos calamos in calamo mensuræ per circuitum.
       EN: On the southern side he measured five hundred reeds by the measuring reed, all around.

42:19  LA: Et ad ventum occidentalem mensus est quingentos calamos in calamo mensuræ.
       EN: On the western side he measured five hundred reeds by the measuring reed.

42:20  LA: Per quatuor ventos mensus est murum ejus undique per circuitum, longitudinem quingentorum cubitorum, et latitudinem quingentorum cubitorum, dividentem inter sanctuarium et vulgi locum.
       EN: On the four sides he measured its wall all around, a length of five hundred cubits and a width of five hundred cubits, dividing the sanctuary from the common place.

EZEKIEL 43

43:1  LA: Et duxit me ad portam quæ respiciebat ad viam orientalem.
      EN: He led me to the gate facing east.

43:2  LA: Et ecce gloria Dei Israël ingrediebatur per viam orientalem : et vox erat ei quasi vox aquarum multarum, et terra splendebat a majestate ejus.
      EN: See, the glory of the God of Israel was entering from the east. His voice was like the sound of many waters, and the earth shone with His majesty.

43:3  LA: Et vidi visionem secundum speciem quam videram quando venit ut disperderet civitatem, et species secundum aspectum quem videram juxta fluvium Chobar : et cecidi super faciem meam.
      EN: I saw a vision like the appearance I had seen when He came to destroy the city; its appearance was like what I had seen beside the river Chebar. I fell upon my face.

43:4  LA: Et majestas Domini ingressa est templum per viam portæ quæ respiciebat ad orientem.
      EN: The majesty of the Lord entered the temple through the gate facing east.

43:5  LA: Et elevavit me spiritus, et introduxit me in atrium interius : et ecce repleta erat gloria Domini domus.
      EN: The Spirit lifted me and brought me into the inner court, and see, the house was filled with the glory of the Lord.

43:6  LA: Et audivi loquentem ad me de domo : et vir qui stabat juxta me
      EN: I heard someone speaking to me from the house, and the man standing beside me

43:7  LA: dixit ad me : Fili hominis, locus solii mei, et locus vestigiorum pedum meorum, ubi habito in medio filiorum Israël in æternum : et non polluent ultra domus Israël nomen sanctum meum, ipsi et reges eorum, in fornicationibus suis, et in ruinis regum suorum, et in excelsis.
      EN: said to me, “Son of man, this is the place of My throne and the place of the soles of My feet, where I dwell among the children of Israel forever. The house of Israel will no longer profane My holy name, neither they nor their kings, by their prostitution, by the ruins of their kings, and by the high places.

43:8  LA: Qui fabricati sunt limen suum juxta limen meum, et postes suos juxta postes meos, et murus erat inter me et eos : et polluerunt nomen sanctum meum in abominationibus quas fecerunt : propter quod consumpsi eos in ira mea.
      EN: They built their threshold beside My threshold and their doorposts beside My doorposts, with only a wall between Me and them. They profaned My holy name by the abominations they committed; therefore I consumed them in My anger.

43:9  LA: Nunc ergo repellant procul fornicationem suam et ruinas regum suorum a me, et habitabo in medio eorum semper.
      EN: Now, then, let them put their prostitution and the ruins of their kings far from Me, and I will dwell among them always.

43:10  LA: Tu autem, fili hominis, ostende domui Israël templum, et confundantur ab iniquitatibus suis, et metiantur fabricam,
       EN: You, son of man, show the temple to the house of Israel, that they may be ashamed of their iniquities and measure the structure,

43:11  LA: et erubescant ex omnibus quæ fecerunt. Figuram domus, et fabricæ ejus, exitus et introitus, et omnem descriptionem ejus, et universa præcepta ejus, cunctumque ordinem ejus, et omnes leges ejus ostende eis, et scribes in oculis eorum, ut custodiant omnes descriptiones ejus, et præcepta illius, et faciant ea.
       EN: and blush over everything they have done. Show them the house's form and its construction, its exits and entrances, its whole design, all its precepts, its entire arrangement, and all its laws. Write it before their eyes so that they may keep all its designs and its precepts and carry them out.

43:12  LA: Ista est lex domus in summitate montis : omnis finis ejus in circuitu, Sanctum sanctorum est. Hæc est ergo lex domus.
       EN: This is the law of the house upon the summit of the mountain: its entire boundary all around is most holy. This, then, is the law of the house.

43:13  LA: Istæ autem mensuræ altaris in cubito verissimo, qui habebat cubitum et palmum : in sinu ejus erat cubitus, et cubitus in latitudine : et definitio ejus usque ad labium ejus, et in circuitu palmus unus : hæc quoque erat fossa altaris.
       EN: These are the measurements of the altar by the full cubit, which was a cubit and a handbreadth. Its basin was a cubit deep and a cubit wide; its border along its rim was one handbreadth all around. This was the altar's trench.

43:14  LA: Et de sinu terræ usque ad crepidinem novissimam duo cubiti, et latitudo cubiti unius : et a crepidine minore usque ad crepidinem majorem quatuor cubiti, et latitudo cubiti unius.
       EN: From the basin at ground level to the lowest ledge were two cubits, with a width of one cubit; from the smaller ledge to the greater ledge were four cubits, with a width of one cubit.

43:15  LA: Ipse autem ariel quatuor cubitorum, et ab ariel usque ad sursum cornua quatuor.
       EN: The ariel itself was four cubits, and above the ariel were four horns.

43:16  LA: Et ariel duodecim cubitorum in longitudine per duodecim cubitos latitudinis, quadrangulatum æquis lateribus.
       EN: The ariel was twelve cubits long by twelve cubits wide, square with equal sides.

43:17  LA: Et crepido quatuordecim cubitorum longitudinis per quatuordecim cubitos latitudinis in quatuor angulis ejus : et corona in circuitu ejus dimidii cubiti, et sinus ejus unius cubiti per circuitum : gradus autem ejus versi ad orientem.
       EN: The ledge was fourteen cubits long by fourteen cubits wide at its four corners. The border around it was half a cubit, and its basin one cubit all around. Its steps faced east.”

43:18  LA: Et dixit ad me : Fili hominis, hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Hi sunt ritus altaris, in quacumque die fuerit fabricatum, ut offeratur super illud holocaustum, et effundatur sanguis.
       EN: He said to me, “Son of man, thus says the Lord God: These are the rites of the altar on the day it is built, for offering burnt offerings upon it and pouring out blood.

43:19  LA: Et dabis sacerdotibus et Levitis qui sunt de semine Sadoc, qui accedunt ad me, ait Dominus Deus, ut offerant mihi vitulum de armento pro peccato.
       EN: You will give to the priests and Levites of Zadok's offspring, who approach Me, says the Lord God, a young bull from the herd to offer Me as a sin offering.

43:20  LA: Et assumens de sanguine ejus, pones super quatuor cornua ejus, et super quatuor angulos crepidinis, et super coronam in circuitu : et mundabis illud et expiabis.
       EN: Take some of its blood and put it upon the altar's four horns, upon the four corners of the ledge, and upon the border all around; cleanse it and make atonement for it.

43:21  LA: Et tolles vitulum qui oblatus fuerit pro peccato, et combures eum in separato loco domus, extra sanctuarium.
       EN: Take the young bull offered for sin and burn it in the separate place of the house, outside the sanctuary.

43:22  LA: Et in die secunda offeres hircum caprarum immaculatum pro peccato : et expiabunt altare sicut expiaverunt in vitulo.
       EN: On the second day you will offer an unblemished male goat as a sin offering, and they will make atonement for the altar as they did with the bull.

43:23  LA: Cumque compleveris expians illud, offeres vitulum de armento immaculatum, et arietem de grege immaculatum.
       EN: When you have finished making atonement for it, offer an unblemished young bull from the herd and an unblemished ram from the flock.

43:24  LA: Et offeres eos in conspectu Domini : et mittent sacerdotes super eos sal, et offerent eos holocaustum Domino.
       EN: Offer them before the Lord; the priests will put salt upon them and offer them as a burnt offering to the Lord.

43:25  LA: Septem diebus facies hircum pro peccato quotidie : et vitulum de armento, et arietem de pecoribus immaculatos offerent.
       EN: For seven days you will provide a male goat daily as a sin offering; they will also offer an unblemished young bull from the herd and an unblemished ram from the flock.

43:26  LA: Septem diebus expiabunt altare et mundabunt illud, et implebunt manum ejus.
       EN: For seven days they will make atonement for the altar, cleanse it, and consecrate it.

43:27  LA: Expletis autem diebus, in die octava et ultra, facient sacerdotes super altare holocausta vestra, et quæ pro pace offerunt : et placatus ero vobis, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: When the days are completed, on the eighth day and afterward, the priests will offer your burnt offerings upon the altar and the offerings they bring for peace, and I will be gracious to you, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 44

44:1  LA: Et convertit me ad viam portæ sanctuarii exterioris, quæ respiciebat ad orientem : et erat clausa.
      EN: He brought me back toward the outer gate of the sanctuary facing east, and it was closed.

44:2  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : Porta hæc clausa erit : non aperietur, et vir non transibit per eam, quoniam Dominus Deus Israël ingressus est per eam : eritque clausa
      EN: The Lord said to me, “This gate will be closed; it will not be opened, and no man will pass through it, because the Lord, the God of Israel, entered through it. It will be closed

44:3  LA: principi. Princeps ipse sedebit in ea, ut comedat panem coram Domino : per viam portæ vestibuli ingredietur, et per viam ejus egredietur.
      EN: to the prince. The prince himself will sit in it to eat bread before the Lord; he will enter by way of the gate's vestibule and go out by that way.”

44:4  LA: Et adduxit me per viam portæ aquilonis in conspectu domus : et vidi, et ecce implevit gloria Domini domum Domini : et cecidi in faciem meam.
      EN: He brought me by the north gate to the front of the house. I looked, and see, the glory of the Lord filled the house of the Lord, and I fell upon my face.

44:5  LA: Et dixit ad me Dominus : Fili hominis, pone cor tuum, et vide oculis tuis, et auribus tuis audi omnia quæ ego loquor ad te de universis cæremoniis domus Domini, et de cunctis legibus ejus : et pones cor tuum in viis templi per omnes exitus sanctuarii.
      EN: The Lord said to me, “Son of man, set your heart upon, see with your eyes, and hear with your ears everything I say to you concerning all the rites of the Lord's house and all its laws. Set your heart upon the ways of the temple through all the exits of the sanctuary.

44:6  LA: Et dices ad exasperantem me domum Israël : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Sufficiant vobis omnia scelera vestra, domus Israël :
      EN: Say to the house of Israel that provokes Me: Thus says the Lord God: Enough of all your crimes, house of Israel!

44:7  LA: eo quod inducitis filios alienos incircumcisos corde, et incircumcisos carne, ut sint in sanctuario meo, et polluant domum meam : et offertis panes meos, adipem et sanguinem, et dissolvitis pactum meum in omnibus sceleribus vestris.
      EN: You bring in foreigners, uncircumcised in heart and uncircumcised in flesh, to be in My sanctuary and defile My house. You offer My bread, fat, and blood, yet dissolve My covenant by all your crimes.

44:8  LA: Et non servastis præcepta sanctuarii mei, et posuistis custodes observationum mearum in sanctuario meo vobismetipsis.
      EN: You have not kept the precepts of My sanctuary, but appointed for yourselves keepers of My observances in My sanctuary.

44:9  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Omnis alienigena incircumcisus corde, et incircumcisus carne, non ingredietur sanctuarium meum : omnis filius alienus qui est in medio filiorum Israël.
      EN: Thus says the Lord God: No foreigner uncircumcised in heart and uncircumcised in flesh will enter My sanctuary, no foreigner who is among the children of Israel.

44:10  LA: Sed et Levitæ qui longe recesserunt a me in errore filiorum Israël, et erraverunt a me post idola sua, et portaverunt iniquitatem suam,
       EN: The Levites, however, who withdrew far from Me when the children of Israel went astray, who strayed from Me after their idols and bore their iniquity,

44:11  LA: erunt in sanctuario meo æditui, et janitores portarum domus, et ministri domus : ipsi mactabunt holocausta et victimas populi, et ipsi stabunt in conspectu eorum ut ministrent eis.
       EN: will be attendants in My sanctuary, gatekeepers at the gates of the house and ministers of the house. They will slaughter the people's burnt offerings and sacrifices and stand before them to minister to them.

44:12  LA: Pro eo quod ministraverunt illis in conspectu idolorum suorum, et facti sunt domui Israël in offendiculum iniquitatis : idcirco levavi manum meam super eos, ait Dominus Deus, et portabunt iniquitatem suam.
       EN: Because they ministered to them before their idols and became a stumbling block of iniquity for the house of Israel, therefore I have raised My hand against them, says the Lord God, and they will bear their iniquity.

44:13  LA: Et non appropinquabunt ad me ut sacerdotio fungantur mihi, neque accedent ad omne sanctuarium meum juxta Sancta sanctorum : sed portabunt confusionem suam, et scelera sua quæ fecerunt.
       EN: They will not approach Me to serve Me as priests or approach any of My sanctuary near the Holy of Holies. They will bear their shame and the crimes they committed.

44:14  LA: Et dabo eos janitores domus in omni ministerio ejus, et in universis quæ fient in ea.
       EN: I will appoint them as gatekeepers of the house, for all its service and for everything done in it.

44:15  LA: Sacerdotes autem et Levitæ, filii Sadoc, qui custodierunt cæremonias sanctuarii mei, cum errarent filii Israël a me, ipsi accedent ad me ut ministrent mihi : et stabunt in conspectu meo, ut offerant mihi adipem et sanguinem, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: But the priests and Levites, the sons of Zadok, who kept the rites of My sanctuary when the children of Israel strayed from Me, will approach Me to minister to Me. They will stand before Me to offer Me fat and blood, says the Lord God.

44:16  LA: Ipsi ingredientur sanctuarium meum, et ipsi accedent ad mensam meam, ut ministrent mihi, et custodiant cæremonias meas.
       EN: They will enter My sanctuary and approach My table to minister to Me and keep My rites.

44:17  LA: Cumque ingredientur portas atrii interioris, vestibus lineis induentur : nec ascendet super eos quidquam laneum, quando ministrant in portis atrii interioris et intrinsecus.
       EN: When they enter the gates of the inner court, they will wear linen garments; nothing woolen will be upon them when they minister at the gates of the inner court and within.

44:18  LA: Vittæ lineæ erunt in capitibus eorum, et feminalia linea erunt in lumbis eorum, et non accingentur in sudore.
       EN: Linen bands will be upon their heads and linen breeches upon their loins, and they will not gird themselves so as to sweat.

44:19  LA: Cumque egredientur atrium exterius ad populum, exuent se vestimentis suis in quibus ministraverant, et reponent ea in gazophylacio sanctuarii : et vestient se vestimentis aliis, et non sanctificabunt populum in vestibus suis.
       EN: When they go out to the people in the outer court, they will remove the garments in which they ministered and put them away in the sanctuary chamber. They will put on other garments and will not sanctify the people with their garments.

44:20  LA: Caput autem suum non radent, neque comam nutrient : sed tondentes attondent capita sua.
       EN: They will neither shave their heads nor let their hair grow long, but will carefully trim their heads.

44:21  LA: Et vinum non bibet omnis sacerdos, quando ingressurus est atrium interius.
       EN: No priest will drink wine when he is about to enter the inner court.

44:22  LA: Et viduam et repudiatam non accipient uxores, sed virgines de semine domus Israël : sed et viduam quæ fuerit vidua a sacerdote, accipient.
       EN: They will not take a widow or a divorced woman as a wife, but virgins of the offspring of the house of Israel. Yet they may take a widow who is a priest's widow.

44:23  LA: Et populum meum docebunt quid sit inter sanctum et pollutum, et inter mundum et immundum ostendent eis.
       EN: They will teach My people the difference between holy and profane and show them the difference between clean and unclean.

44:24  LA: Et cum fuerit controversia, stabunt in judiciis meis, et judicabunt : leges meas et præcepta mea in omnibus solemnitatibus meis custodient, et sabbata mea sanctificabunt.
       EN: When there is a dispute, they will stand in judgment according to My judgments. They will keep My laws and precepts at all My solemn feasts and sanctify My sabbaths.

44:25  LA: Et ad mortuum hominem non ingredientur, ne polluantur, nisi ad patrem et matrem, et filium et filiam, et fratrem, et sororem quæ alterum virum non habuerit : in quibus contaminabuntur.
       EN: They will not go near a dead person and so become defiled, except for father, mother, son, daughter, brother, or a sister who has not had a husband; for these they may become defiled.

44:26  LA: Et postquam fuerit emundatus, septem dies numerabuntur ei.
       EN: After he has been cleansed, seven days will be counted for him.

44:27  LA: Et in die introitus sui in sanctuarium ad atrium interius, ut ministret mihi in sanctuario, offeret pro peccato suo, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: On the day he enters the sanctuary, into the inner court, to minister to Me in the sanctuary, he will offer for his sin, says the Lord God.

44:28  LA: Non erit autem eis hæreditas : ego hæreditas eorum. Et possessionem non dabitis eis in Israël : ego enim possessio eorum.
       EN: They will have no inheritance; I am their inheritance. You will give them no possession in Israel, for I am their possession.

44:29  LA: Victimam et pro peccato et pro delicto ipsi comedent, et omne votum in Israël ipsorum erit.
       EN: They will eat the sacrifice, the sin offering, and the guilt offering, and everything vowed in Israel will belong to them.

44:30  LA: Et primitiva omnium primogenitorum, et omnia libamenta ex omnibus quæ offeruntur, sacerdotum erunt : et primitiva ciborum vestrorum dabitis sacerdoti, ut reponat benedictionem domui tuæ.
       EN: The firstfruits of all the firstborn and all libations from everything offered will belong to the priests. You will give the first of your food to the priest, so that he may lay a blessing upon your house.

44:31  LA: Omne morticinum, et captum a bestia, de avibus et de pecoribus, non comedent sacerdotes.
       EN: The priests will eat nothing that died of itself or was taken by a beast, whether birds or livestock.

EZEKIEL 45

45:1  LA: Cumque cœperitis terram dividere sortito, separate primitias Domino, sanctificatum de terra, longitudine viginti quinque millia, et latitudine decem millia : sanctificatum erit in omni termino ejus per circuitum.
      EN: When you begin to divide the land by lot, set apart firstfruits for the Lord, a holy portion of the land, twenty-five thousand long and ten thousand wide. It will be holy throughout its boundary all around.

45:2  LA: Et erit ex omni parte sanctificatum quingentos per quingentos, quadrifariam per circuitum, et quinquaginta cubitis in suburbana ejus per gyrum.
      EN: The holy area will be a square, five hundred by five hundred, with fifty cubits of open ground all around.

45:3  LA: Et a mensura ista mensurabis longitudinem viginti quinque millium, et latitudinem decem millium : et in ipso erit templum, Sanctumque sanctorum.
      EN: From this measure you will measure a length of twenty-five thousand and a width of ten thousand, and within it will be the temple and the Holy of Holies.

45:4  LA: Sanctificatum de terra erit sacerdotibus ministris sanctuarii, qui accedunt ad ministerium Domini : et erit eis locus in domos, et in sanctuarium sanctitatis.
      EN: The holy portion of the land will belong to the priests, ministers of the sanctuary, who approach to minister to the Lord. It will be a place for their houses and for the holy sanctuary.

45:5  LA: Viginti quinque autem millia longitudinis, et decem millia latitudinis erunt Levitis qui ministrant domui : ipsi possidebunt viginti gazophylacia.
      EN: Twenty-five thousand in length and ten thousand in width will belong to the Levites who minister in the house; they will possess twenty chambers.

45:6  LA: Et possessionem civitatis dabitis quinque millia latitudinis, et longitudinis viginti quinque millia, secundum separationem sanctuarii, omni domui Israël.
      EN: You will give as the city's possession five thousand in width and twenty-five thousand in length, alongside the portion set apart for the sanctuary, for the whole house of Israel.

45:7  LA: Principi quoque hinc et inde in separationem sanctuarii, et in possessionem civitatis, contra faciem separationis sanctuarii, et contra faciem possessionis urbis, a latere maris usque ad mare, et a latere orientis usque ad orientem : longitudinis autem juxta unamquamque partem, a termino occidentali usque ad terminum orientalem.
      EN: The prince will also have land on either side of the portion set apart for the sanctuary and the city's possession, facing the sanctuary's portion and the city's possession, from the western side westward and from the eastern side eastward. Its length will run alongside each portion, from the western border to the eastern border.

45:8  LA: De terra erit ei possessio in Israël, et non depopulabuntur ultra principes populum meum : sed terram dabunt domui Israël secundum tribus eorum.
      EN: This land will be his possession in Israel, and the princes will no longer plunder My people, but will give the land to the house of Israel according to their tribes.

45:9  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Sufficiat vobis, principes Israël : iniquitatem et rapinas intermittite, et judicium et justitiam facite : separate confinia vestra a populo meo, ait Dominus Deus.
      EN: Thus says the Lord God: Enough, princes of Israel! Cease injustice and plunder, do what is just and right, and withdraw your boundaries from My people, says the Lord God.

45:10  LA: Statera justa, et ephi justum, et batus justus erit vobis.
       EN: You will have a just balance, a just ephah, and a just bath.

45:11  LA: Ephi et batus æqualia et unius mensuræ erunt, ut capiat decimam partem cori batus, et decimam partem cori ephi : juxta mensuram cori erit æqua libratio eorum.
       EN: The ephah and the bath will be equal and of one measure, so that the bath holds a tenth of a cor and the ephah a tenth of a cor. Their equal reckoning will be according to the measure of the cor.

45:12  LA: Siclus autem viginti obolos habet : porro viginti sicli, et viginti quinque sicli, et quindecim sicli, mnam faciunt.
       EN: The shekel has twenty obols; twenty shekels, twenty-five shekels, and fifteen shekels make a mina.

45:13  LA: Et hæ sunt primitiæ quas tolletis : sextam partem ephi de coro frumenti, et sextam partem ephi de coro hordei.
       EN: These are the firstfruits you will take: a sixth of an ephah from a cor of wheat and a sixth of an ephah from a cor of barley.

45:14  LA: Mensura quoque olei, batus olei, decima pars cori est : et decem bati corum faciunt, quia decem bati implent corum.
       EN: The measure for oil, a bath of oil, is a tenth of a cor, and ten baths make a cor, for ten baths fill a cor.

45:15  LA: Et arietem unum de grege ducentorum, de his quæ nutriunt Israël, in sacrificium, et in holocaustum, et in pacifica, ad expiandum pro eis, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: One ram from a flock of two hundred, from those that nourish Israel, will be for sacrifice, burnt offering, and peace offerings, to make atonement for them, says the Lord God.

45:16  LA: Omnis populus terræ tenebitur primitiis his principi in Israël.
       EN: All the people of the land will be bound to provide these firstfruits for the prince in Israel.

45:17  LA: Et super principem erunt holocausta, et sacrificium, et libamina, in solemnitatibus, et in calendis, et in sabbatis, et in universis solemnitatibus domus Israël : ipse faciet pro peccato sacrificium, et holocaustum, et pacifica, ad expiandum pro domo Israël.
       EN: The burnt offerings, sacrifice, and libations at the feasts, new moons, sabbaths, and all the solemn celebrations of the house of Israel will be the prince's responsibility. He will provide the sacrifice for sin, the burnt offering, and the peace offerings to make atonement for the house of Israel.

45:18  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : In primo mense, una mensis, sumes vitulum de armento immaculatum, et expiabis sanctuarium.
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: In the first month, on the first day of the month, take an unblemished young bull from the herd and make atonement for the sanctuary.

45:19  LA: Et tollet sacerdos de sanguine quod erit pro peccato, et ponet in postibus domus, et in quatuor angulis crepidinis altaris, et in postibus portæ atrii interioris.
       EN: The priest will take some blood from the sin offering and put it upon the doorposts of the house, upon the four corners of the altar's ledge, and upon the gateposts of the inner court.

45:20  LA: Et sic facies in septima mensis, pro unoquoque qui ignoravit, et errore deceptus est : et expiabis pro domo.
       EN: You will do the same on the seventh day of the month for anyone who acted unknowingly and was deceived by error, and you will make atonement for the house.

45:21  LA: In primo mense, quartadecima die mensis, erit vobis Paschæ solemnitas : septem diebus azyma comedentur.
       EN: In the first month, on the fourteenth day of the month, you will have the solemn feast of Passover; unleavened bread will be eaten for seven days.

45:22  LA: Et faciet princeps in die illa, pro se et pro universo populo terræ, vitulum pro peccato.
       EN: On that day the prince will provide a young bull as a sin offering for himself and for all the people of the land.

45:23  LA: Et in septem dierum solemnitate faciet holocaustum Domino, septem vitulos et septem arietes immaculatos, quotidie septem diebus : et pro peccato hircum caprarum quotidie.
       EN: During the feast of seven days he will provide a burnt offering to the Lord: seven unblemished bulls and seven unblemished rams daily for seven days, and a male goat daily as a sin offering.

45:24  LA: Et sacrificium ephi per vitulum, et ephi per arietem faciet, et olei hin per singula ephi.
       EN: He will provide a grain offering of an ephah for each bull and an ephah for each ram, and a hin of oil for every ephah.

45:25  LA: Septimo mense, quintadecima die mensis, in solemnitate, faciet sicut supra dicta sunt per septem dies, tam pro peccato quam pro holocausto, et in sacrificio, et in oleo.
       EN: In the seventh month, on the fifteenth day of the month, at the feast, he will do as stated above for seven days, for the sin offering, the burnt offering, the grain offering, and the oil.”

EZEKIEL 46

46:1  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Porta atrii interioris quæ respicit ad orientem, erit clausa sex diebus in quibus opus fit : die autem sabbati aperietur, sed et in die calendarum aperietur.
      EN: Thus says the Lord God: “The gate of the inner court that faces east will be closed during the six working days, but on the sabbath day it will be opened; it will also be opened on the day of the new moon.

46:2  LA: Et intrabit princeps per viam vestibuli portæ deforis, et stabit in limine portæ : et facient sacerdotes holocaustum ejus, et pacifica ejus : et adorabit super limen portæ, et egredietur : porta autem non claudetur usque ad vesperam.
      EN: The prince will enter from outside by way of the gate's vestibule and stand at the gate's threshold. The priests will offer his burnt offering and his peace offerings; he will worship at the gate's threshold and go out, but the gate will not be closed until evening.

46:3  LA: Et adorabit populus terræ ad ostium portæ illius in sabbatis et in calendis coram Domino.
      EN: The people of the land will worship before the Lord at the entrance of that gate on the sabbaths and new moons.

46:4  LA: Holocaustum autem hoc offeret princeps Domino : in die sabbati, sex agnos immaculatos, et arietem immaculatum,
      EN: This is the burnt offering the prince will offer to the Lord on the sabbath day: six unblemished lambs and an unblemished ram,

46:5  LA: et sacrificium ephi per arietem, in agnis autem sacrificium quod dederit manus ejus, et olei hin per singula ephi.
      EN: with a grain offering of an ephah for the ram, and for the lambs whatever offering his hand gives, and a hin of oil for every ephah.

46:6  LA: In die autem calendarum vitulum de armento immaculatum, et sex agni et arietes immaculati erunt.
      EN: On the day of the new moon there will be an unblemished young bull from the herd, six lambs, and unblemished rams.

46:7  LA: Et ephi per vitulum, ephi quoque per arietem faciet sacrificium : de agnis autem sicut invenerit manus ejus, et olei hin per singula ephi.
      EN: He will provide a grain offering of an ephah for the bull and an ephah for the ram, and for the lambs as much as his hand can supply, and a hin of oil for every ephah.

46:8  LA: Cumque ingressurus est princeps, per viam vestibuli portæ ingrediatur, et per eamdem viam exeat.
      EN: When the prince enters, let him enter by way of the gate's vestibule and go out by the same way.

46:9  LA: Et cum intrabit populus terræ in conspectu Domini in solemnitatibus, qui ingreditur per portam aquilonis ut adoret, egrediatur per viam portæ meridianæ : porro qui ingreditur per viam portæ meridianæ, egrediatur per viam portæ aquilonis. Non revertetur per viam portæ per quam ingressus est, sed e regione illius egredietur.
      EN: When the people of the land enter before the Lord at the solemn feasts, let the one entering by the north gate to worship go out by the south gate, and the one entering by the south gate go out by the north gate. He will not return by the gate through which he entered, but go out opposite it.

46:10  LA: Princeps autem in medio eorum cum ingredientibus ingredietur, et cum egredientibus egredietur.
       EN: The prince will be among them, entering with those who enter and going out with those who go out.

46:11  LA: Et in nundinis, et in solemnitatibus, erit sacrificium ephi per vitulum, et ephi per arietem : agnis autem erit sacrificium sicut invenerit manus ejus, et olei hin per singula ephi.
       EN: On market days and solemn feasts the grain offering will be an ephah for the bull and an ephah for the ram; for the lambs the offering will be as much as his hand can supply, and a hin of oil for every ephah.

46:12  LA: Cum autem fecerit princeps spontaneum holocaustum, aut pacifica voluntaria Domino, aperietur ei porta quæ respicit ad orientem, et faciet holocaustum suum et pacifica sua, sicut fieri solet in die sabbati : et egredietur, claudeturque porta postquam exierit.
       EN: When the prince makes a voluntary burnt offering or voluntary peace offerings to the Lord, the east-facing gate will be opened for him. He will offer his burnt offering and his peace offerings as is customarily done on the sabbath day; he will go out, and the gate will be closed after he leaves.

46:13  LA: Et agnum ejusdem anni immaculatum faciet holocaustum quotidie Domino : semper mane faciet illud.
       EN: He will offer an unblemished lamb of that year as a burnt offering daily to the Lord; he will offer it every morning.

46:14  LA: Et faciet sacrificium super eo cata mane mane sextam partem ephi, et de oleo tertiam partem hin, ut misceatur similæ : sacrificium Domino legitimum, juge atque perpetuum.
       EN: With it he will provide a grain offering every morning, a sixth of an ephah, and a third of a hin of oil to mix with the fine flour: a prescribed offering to the Lord, continual and perpetual.

46:15  LA: Faciet agnum, et sacrificium, et oleum cata mane mane, holocaustum sempiternum.
       EN: He will provide the lamb, the grain offering, and the oil every morning as an everlasting burnt offering.

46:16  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Si dederit princeps donum alicui de filiis suis, hæreditas ejus filiorum suorum erit : possidebunt eam hæreditarie.
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: If the prince gives a gift to one of his sons, that inheritance will belong to his sons; they will possess it by inheritance.

46:17  LA: Si autem dederit legatum de hæreditate sua uni servorum suorum, erit illius usque ad annum remissionis, et revertetur ad principem : hæreditas autem ejus filiis ejus erit.
       EN: But if he gives a grant from his inheritance to one of his servants, it will belong to him until the year of release, and then return to the prince. His inheritance, however, will belong to his sons.

46:18  LA: Et non accipiet princeps de hæreditate populi per violentiam, et de possessione eorum : sed de possessione sua hæreditatem dabit filiis suis, ut non dispergatur populus meus unusquisque a possessione sua.
       EN: The prince will not take from the people's inheritance or their possession by force, but will give an inheritance to his sons from his own possession, so that My people are not scattered, each from his possession.”

46:19  LA: Et introduxit me per ingressum qui erat ex latere portæ, in gazophylacia sanctuarii ad sacerdotes, quæ respiciebant ad aquilonem : et erat ibi locus vergens ad occidentem.
       EN: He brought me through the entrance beside the gate into the sanctuary chambers for the priests, which faced north. There was a place there facing west.

46:20  LA: Et dixit ad me : Iste est locus ubi coquent sacerdotes pro peccato et pro delicto : ubi coquent sacrificium, ut non efferant in atrium exterius, et sanctificetur populus.
       EN: He said to me, “This is the place where the priests will cook the sin offering and the guilt offering and bake the grain offering, so that they do not carry it into the outer court and thereby sanctify the people.”

46:21  LA: Et eduxit me in atrium exterius, et circumduxit me per quatuor angulos atrii : et ecce atriolum erat in angulo atrii, atriola singula per angulos atrii.
       EN: He brought me out into the outer court and led me around the court's four corners, and see, there was a small court at a corner of the court, a small court at each of the court's corners.

46:22  LA: In quatuor angulis atrii atriola disposita, quadraginta cubitorum per longum, et triginta per latum : mensuræ unius quatuor erant.
       EN: At the court's four corners small courts were arranged, forty cubits long and thirty wide; all four had the same measurements.

46:23  LA: Et paries per circuitum ambiens quatuor atriola : et culinæ fabricatæ erant subter porticus per gyrum.
       EN: A wall surrounded the four small courts, and kitchens were built beneath the porticoes all around.

46:24  LA: Et dixit ad me : Hæc est domus culinarum, in qua coquent ministri domus Domini victimas populi.
       EN: He said to me, “This is the kitchen house, in which the ministers of the Lord's house will cook the people's sacrifices.”

EZEKIEL 47

47:1  LA: Et convertit me ad portam domus, et ecce aquæ egrediebantur subter limen domus ad orientem : facies enim domus respiciebat ad orientem, aquæ autem descendebant in latus templi dextrum, ad meridiem altaris.
      EN: He brought me back to the entrance of the house, and see, waters were flowing from beneath the house's threshold toward the east, for the front of the house faced east. The waters descended at the right side of the temple, south of the altar.

47:2  LA: Et eduxit me per viam portæ aquilonis, et convertit me ad viam foras portam exteriorem, viam quæ respiciebat ad orientem : et ecce aquæ redundantes a latere dextro.
      EN: He brought me out by the north gate and led me around outside to the outer gate facing east, and see, waters were pouring out from the right side.

47:3  LA: Cum egrederetur vir ad orientem, qui habebat funiculum in manu sua, et mensus est mille cubitos, et traduxit me per aquam usque ad talos.
      EN: As the man with the cord in his hand went east, he measured a thousand cubits and led me through water reaching to the ankles.

47:4  LA: Rursumque mensus est mille, et traduxit me per aquam usque ad genua.
      EN: Again he measured a thousand and led me through water reaching to the knees.

47:5  LA: Et mensus est mille, et traduxit me per aquam usque ad renes. Et mensus est mille, torrentem quem non potui pertransire, quoniam intumuerant aquæ profundi torrentis, qui non potest transvadari.
      EN: He measured a thousand and led me through water reaching to the loins. He measured a thousand: a torrent I could not cross, for the waters of the deep torrent had risen and could not be forded.

47:6  LA: Et dixit ad me : Certe vidisti, fili hominis. Et eduxit me, et convertit ad ripam torrentis.
      EN: He said to me, “You have surely seen, son of man.” He brought me out and led me back to the torrent's bank.

47:7  LA: Cumque me convertissem, ecce in ripa torrentis ligna multa nimis ex utraque parte.
      EN: When I turned back, see, there were very many trees upon the torrent's bank on either side.

47:8  LA: Et ait ad me : Aquæ istæ quæ egrediuntur ad tumulos sabuli orientalis, et descendunt ad plana deserti, intrabunt mare et exibunt : et sanabuntur aquæ.
      EN: He said to me, “These waters flow out toward the eastern sand hills and descend to the plains of the wilderness; they will enter the sea and flow out, and the waters will be healed.

47:9  LA: Et omnis anima vivens quæ serpit quocumque venerit torrens, vivet : et erunt pisces multi satis, postquam venerint illuc aquæ istæ : et sanabuntur et vivent omnia ad quæ venerit torrens.
      EN: Every living creature that moves, wherever the torrent comes, will live. There will be very many fish after these waters come there; everything to which the torrent comes will be healed and live.

47:10  LA: Et stabunt super illas piscatores : ab Engaddi usque ad Engallim siccatio sagenarum erit : plurimæ species erunt piscium ejus, sicut pisces maris magni, multitudinis nimiæ.
       EN: Fishermen will stand beside them; from En-gedi to En-eglaim there will be a drying place for nets. Its fish will be of very many kinds, like the fish of the great sea, an exceedingly great multitude.

47:11  LA: In littoribus autem ejus et in palustribus, non sanabuntur, quia in salinas dabuntur.
       EN: But along its shores and in its marshes the waters will not be healed, for they will be given over to salt.

47:12  LA: Et super torrentem orietur in ripis ejus, ex utraque parte, omne lignum pomiferum : non defluet folium ex eo, et non deficiet fructus ejus : per singulos menses afferet primitiva, quia aquæ ejus de sanctuario egredientur : et erunt fructus ejus in cibum, et folia ejus ad medicinam.
       EN: Beside the torrent, on its banks on either side, every fruit-bearing tree will grow. Its leaf will not fall, and its fruit will not fail; every month it will bear firstfruits, because its waters flow from the sanctuary. Its fruit will be for food and its leaves for medicine.

47:13  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Hic est terminus in quo possidebitis terram in duodecim tribubus Israël : quia Joseph duplicem funiculum habet.
       EN: Thus says the Lord God: This is the boundary within which you will possess the land according to Israel's twelve tribes, for Joseph has a double allotment.

47:14  LA: Possidebitis autem eam singuli æque ut frater suus, super quam levavi manum meam ut darem patribus vestris : et cadet terra hæc vobis in possessionem.
       EN: You will possess it, each equally with his brother, the land concerning which I raised My hand to give it to your fathers. This land will fall to you as a possession.

47:15  LA: Hic est autem terminus terræ : ad plagam septentrionalem, a mari magno via Hethalon, venientibus Sedada,
       EN: This is the boundary of the land: on the northern side, from the great sea by way of Hethlon, as one comes to Zedad,

47:16  LA: Emath, Berotha, Sabarim, quæ est inter terminum Damasci et confinium Emath, domus Tichon, quæ est juxta terminum Auran.
       EN: Hamath, Berothah, Sibraim, which is between the boundary of Damascus and the border of Hamath, the house of Tichon, which is beside the boundary of Hauran.

47:17  LA: Et erit terminus a mari usque ad atrium Enon, terminus Damasci : et ab aquilone ad aquilonem, terminus Emath plaga septentrionalis.
       EN: The boundary will run from the sea to the court of Enon, the boundary of Damascus; farther north is the boundary of Hamath, the northern side.

47:18  LA: Porro plaga orientalis de medio Auran, et de medio Damasci, et de medio Galaad, et de medio terræ Israël, Jordanis disterminans ad mare orientale. Metiemini etiam plagam orientalem.
       EN: On the eastern side, from the middle of Hauran, the middle of Damascus, the middle of Gilead, and the middle of the land of Israel, the Jordan forms the boundary to the eastern sea. You will also measure the eastern side.

47:19  LA: Plaga autem australis meridiana, a Thamar usque ad aquas contradictionis Cades, et torrens usque ad mare magnum : et hæc est plaga ad meridiem australis.
       EN: The southern side, toward the south, will run from Tamar to the waters of contradiction at Kadesh, and along the torrent to the great sea. This is the southern side, toward the south.

47:20  LA: Et plaga maris, mare magnum a confinio per directum, donec venias Emath : hæc est plaga maris.
       EN: The western side will be the great sea, from the border straight on until you come to Hamath. This is the western side.

47:21  LA: Et dividetis terram istam vobis per tribus Israël :
       EN: You will divide this land among yourselves according to Israel's tribes.

47:22  LA: et mittetis eam in hæreditatem vobis, et advenis qui accesserint ad vos, qui genuerint filios in medio vestrum : et erunt vobis sicut indigenæ inter filios Israël : vobiscum divident possessionem in medio tribuum Israël.
       EN: You will allot it as an inheritance for yourselves and for the foreigners who have joined you and begotten children among you. They will be to you like native-born people among the children of Israel; with you they will share the possession among Israel's tribes.

47:23  LA: In tribu autem quacumque fuerit advena, ibi dabitis possessionem illi, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: In whatever tribe a foreigner resides, there you will give him a possession, says the Lord God.”

EZEKIEL 48

48:1  LA: Et hæc nomina tribuum a finibus aquilonis, juxta viam Hethalon, pergentibus Emath, atrium Enan terminus Damasci ad aquilonem, juxta viam Emath : et erit ei plaga orientalis mare, Dan una.
      EN: These are the names of the tribes from the northern borders, beside the way of Hethlon as one goes to Hamath, the court of Enan at the northern boundary of Damascus beside the way of Hamath; its portion will extend from the eastern side to the sea: Dan, one portion.

48:2  LA: Et super terminum Dan, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam maris, Aser una.
      EN: Along the boundary of Dan, from the eastern side to the western side: Asher, one portion.

48:3  LA: Et super terminum Aser, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam maris, Nephthali una.
      EN: Along the boundary of Asher, from the eastern side to the western side: Naphtali, one portion.

48:4  LA: Et super terminum Nephthali, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam maris, Manasse una.
      EN: Along the boundary of Naphtali, from the eastern side to the western side: Manasseh, one portion.

48:5  LA: Et super terminum Manasse, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam maris, Ephraim una.
      EN: Along the boundary of Manasseh, from the eastern side to the western side: Ephraim, one portion.

48:6  LA: Et super terminum Ephraim, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam maris, Ruben una.
      EN: Along the boundary of Ephraim, from the eastern side to the western side: Reuben, one portion.

48:7  LA: Et super terminum Ruben, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam maris, Juda una.
      EN: Along the boundary of Reuben, from the eastern side to the western side: Judah, one portion.

48:8  LA: Et super terminum Juda, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam maris, erunt primitiæ quas separabitis, viginti quinque millibus latitudinis et longitudinis, sicuti singulæ partes a plaga orientali usque ad plagam maris : et erit sanctuarium in medio ejus.
      EN: Along the boundary of Judah, from the eastern side to the western side, will be the firstfruits that you set apart, twenty-five thousand in width and length, like each portion from the eastern side to the western side. The sanctuary will be in its midst.

48:9  LA: Primitiæ quas separabitis Domino, longitudo viginti quinque millibus, et latitudo decem millibus.
      EN: The firstfruits that you set apart for the Lord will be twenty-five thousand long and ten thousand wide.

48:10  LA: Hæ autem erunt primitiæ sanctuarii sacerdotum, ad aquilonem longitudinis viginti quinque millia, et ad mare latitudinis decem millia, sed et ad orientem latitudinis decem millia, et ad meridiem longitudinis viginti quinque millia : et erit sanctuarium Domini in medio ejus.
       EN: These will be the holy firstfruits for the priests: on the north, twenty-five thousand in length; on the west, ten thousand in width; on the east also, ten thousand in width; and on the south, twenty-five thousand in length. The Lord's sanctuary will be in its midst.

48:11  LA: Sacerdotibus sanctuarium erit de filiis Sadoc, qui custodierunt cæremonias meas, et non erraverunt cum errarent filii Israël, sicut erraverunt et Levitæ.
       EN: The holy portion will belong to the priests among the sons of Zadok, who kept My rites and did not stray when the children of Israel strayed, as the Levites also strayed.

48:12  LA: Et erunt eis primitiæ de primitiis terræ Sanctum sanctorum, juxta terminum Levitarum.
       EN: Theirs will be firstfruits from the firstfruits of the land, a most holy portion, beside the boundary of the Levites.

48:13  LA: Sed et Levitis similiter, juxta fines sacerdotum, viginti quinque millia longitudinis, et latitudinis decem millia. Omnis longitudo viginti et quinque millium, et latitudo decem millium.
       EN: The Levites will likewise have, beside the priests' borders, twenty-five thousand in length and ten thousand in width. The entire length will be twenty-five thousand, and the width ten thousand.

48:14  LA: Et non venundabunt ex eo, neque mutabunt : neque transferentur primitiæ terræ, quia sanctificatæ sunt Domino.
       EN: They will not sell any of it or exchange it, nor will the firstfruits of the land be transferred, because they are consecrated to the Lord.

48:15  LA: Quinque millia autem quæ supersunt in latitudine per viginti quinque millia, profana erunt urbis in habitaculum et in suburbana : et erit civitas in medio ejus.
       EN: The five thousand remaining in width, alongside the twenty-five thousand, will be for the city's common use, for dwellings and open ground. The city will be in its midst.

48:16  LA: Et hæ mensuræ ejus : ad plagam septentrionalem, quingenta et quatuor millia : et ad plagam meridianam, quingenta et quatuor millia : et ad plagam orientalem, quingenta et quatuor millia : et ad plagam occidentalem, quingenta et quatuor millia.
       EN: These are its measurements: on the northern side, four thousand five hundred; on the southern side, four thousand five hundred; on the eastern side, four thousand five hundred; and on the western side, four thousand five hundred.

48:17  LA: Erunt autem suburbana civitatis ad aquilonem, ducenta quinquaginta : et ad meridiem, ducenta quinquaginta : et ad orientem, ducenta quinquaginta : et ad mare, ducenta quinquaginta.
       EN: The city's open ground will be two hundred fifty to the north, two hundred fifty to the south, two hundred fifty to the east, and two hundred fifty to the west.

48:18  LA: Quod autem reliquum fuerit in longitudine secundum primitias sanctuarii, decem millia in orientem, et decem millia in occidentem, erunt sicut primitiæ sanctuarii : et erunt fruges ejus in panes his qui serviunt civitati.
       EN: What remains in length alongside the sacred allotment, ten thousand to the east and ten thousand to the west, will be like the sacred allotment. Its produce will provide bread for those who serve the city.

48:19  LA: Servientes autem civitati, operabuntur ex omnibus tribubus Israël.
       EN: Those who serve the city, from all Israel's tribes, will cultivate it.

48:20  LA: Omnes primitiæ viginti quinque millium, per viginti quinque millia in quadrum, separabuntur in primitias sanctuarii, et in possessionem civitatis.
       EN: The entire firstfruits, twenty-five thousand by twenty-five thousand square, will be set apart as the holy firstfruits and the city's possession.

48:21  LA: Quod autem reliquum fuerit, principis erit ex omni parte primitiarum sanctuarii, et possessionis civitatis e regione viginti quinque millium primitiarum usque ad terminum orientalem : sed et ad mare, e regione viginti quinque millium, usque ad terminum maris, similiter in partibus principis erit : et erunt primitiæ sanctuarii, et sanctuarium templi, in medio ejus.
       EN: What remains will belong to the prince on either side of the holy firstfruits and the city's possession, alongside the twenty-five thousand of the firstfruits to the eastern border, and westward alongside the twenty-five thousand to the western border. It will likewise belong to the prince's portions, and the holy firstfruits and the temple sanctuary will be in its midst.

48:22  LA: De possessione autem Levitarum, et de possessione civitatis in medio partium principis, erit inter terminum Juda et inter terminum Benjamin, et ad principem pertinebit.
       EN: Apart from the Levites' possession and the city's possession in the midst of the prince's portions, the land between Judah's boundary and Benjamin's boundary will belong to the prince.

48:23  LA: Et reliquis tribubus, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam occidentalem, Benjamin una.
       EN: For the remaining tribes, from the eastern side to the western side: Benjamin, one portion.

48:24  LA: Et contra terminum Benjamin, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam occidentalem, Simeon una.
       EN: Along the boundary of Benjamin, from the eastern side to the western side: Simeon, one portion.

48:25  LA: Et super terminum Simeonis, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam occidentalem, Issachar una.
       EN: Along the boundary of Simeon, from the eastern side to the western side: Issachar, one portion.

48:26  LA: Et super terminum Issachar, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam occidentalem, Zabulon una.
       EN: Along the boundary of Issachar, from the eastern side to the western side: Zebulun, one portion.

48:27  LA: Et super terminum Zabulon, a plaga orientali usque ad plagam maris, Gad una.
       EN: Along the boundary of Zebulun, from the eastern side to the western side: Gad, one portion.

48:28  LA: Et super terminum Gad, ad plagam austri in meridie : et erit finis de Thamar usque ad aquas contradictionis Cades : hæreditas contra mare magnum.
       EN: Along the boundary of Gad, toward the southern side, the border will run from Tamar to the waters of contradiction at Kadesh, an inheritance facing the great sea.

48:29  LA: Hæc est terra quam mittetis in sortem tribubus Israël, et hæ partitiones earum, ait Dominus Deus.
       EN: This is the land that you will allot to Israel's tribes, and these are their divisions, says the Lord God.

48:30  LA: Et hi egressus civitatis : a plaga septentrionali, quingentos et quatuor millia mensurabis.
       EN: These are the city's exits: on the northern side you will measure four thousand five hundred.

48:31  LA: Et portæ civitatis ex nominibus tribuum Israël : portæ tres a septentrione : porta Ruben una, porta Juda una, porta Levi una.
       EN: The city's gates will bear the names of Israel's tribes: three gates on the north—one gate for Reuben, one gate for Judah, and one gate for Levi.

48:32  LA: Et ad plagam orientalem, quingentos et quatuor millia, et portæ tres : porta Joseph una, porta Benjamin una, porta Dan una.
       EN: On the eastern side, four thousand five hundred, with three gates—one gate for Joseph, one gate for Benjamin, and one gate for Dan.

48:33  LA: Et ad plagam meridianam, quingentos et quatuor millia metieris, et portæ tres : porta Simeonis una, porta Issachar una, porta Zabulon una.
       EN: On the southern side you will measure four thousand five hundred, with three gates—one gate for Simeon, one gate for Issachar, and one gate for Zebulun.

48:34  LA: Et ad plagam occidentalem, quingentos et quatuor millia, et portæ eorum tres : porta Gad una, porta Aser una, porta Nephthali una.
       EN: On the western side, four thousand five hundred, with their three gates—one gate for Gad, one gate for Asher, and one gate for Naphtali.

48:35  LA: Per circuitum, decem et octo millia : et nomen civitatis ex illa die, Dominus ibidem.
       EN: The circuit will be eighteen thousand, and the city's name from that day will be: The Lord Is There.
